"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function AccountsPage() {
  const [accounts, setAccounts] = useState<any[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      setAccounts(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("freshcut-accounts") || "[]"));
    } catch {
      setAccounts([]);
    }
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1600px]">
        <Header title="Accounts" subtitle="Manage business accounts and financial records." />
        <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
          <Kpi title="Total Accounts" value={accounts.length} />
          <Kpi title="Active" value={accounts.length} />
          <Kpi title="Business Status" value="Active" />
          <Kpi title="System" value="Ready" />
        </div>
        <Panel title="Account Overview">
          {!accounts.length ? (
            <Empty text="No account records available." />
          ) : (
            <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
              {accounts.map((item) => (
                <div key={item.id} className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 p-5">
                  <h3 className="font-bold text-[#123b38]">{item.name || item.title || "Account"}</h3>
                  <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">{item.email || item.type || "Business Account"}</p>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </Panel>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Header({ title, subtitle }: any) {
  return <div className="mb-7"><p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[.18em] text-emerald-700">Fresh2Cook Admin</p><h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38] md:text-4xl">{title}</h1><p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">{subtitle}</p></div>;
}
function Kpi({ title, value }: any) {
  return <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm"><p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500">{title}</p><p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">{value}</p></div>;
}
function Panel({ title, children }: any) {
  return <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm"><h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">{title}</h2><div className="mt-5">{children}</div></div>;
}
function Empty({ text }: any) {
  return <div className="rounded-xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 p-10 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">{text}</div>;
}







==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\activity-log\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function ActivityLogPage() {
  const [logs, setLogs] = useState<any[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => { loadLogs(); }, []);

  function loadLogs() {
    try { setLogs(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("freshcut-activity-log") || "[]")); }
    catch { setLogs([]); }
  }

  function addDemoLogs() {
    const demoLogs = [
      { id: Date.now(), action: "New Order Received", user: "System", date: new Date().toLocaleString() },
      { id: Date.now() + 1, action: "Product Updated", user: "Admin", date: new Date().toLocaleString() },
      { id: Date.now() + 2, action: "Customer Registered", user: "Website", date: new Date().toLocaleString() },
    ];
    localStorage.setItem("freshcut-activity-log", JSON.stringify(demoLogs));
    setLogs(demoLogs);
  }

  function clearLogs() {
    if (!confirm("Clear Activity Log?")) return;
    localStorage.removeItem("freshcut-activity-log");
    setLogs([]);
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1500px]">
        <Header title="Activity Log" subtitle="Track important system and admin activities." />
        <div className="mb-6 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
          <button onClick={addDemoLogs} className="rounded-xl bg-[#063f3d] px-6 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white">Generate Demo Logs</button>
          <button onClick={clearLogs} className="rounded-xl bg-red-50 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-red-700">Clear Logs</button>
        </div>
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
          {!logs.length ? <Empty text="No Activity Found" /> : logs.map(log => (
            <div key={log.id} className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 p-5 last:border-0">
              <div>
                <h2 className="font-bold text-[#123b38]">{log.action}</h2>
                <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">{log.user}</p>
              </div>
              <span className="text-xs text-slate-400">{log.date}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
function Header({ title, subtitle }: any) {
  return <div className="mb-7"><p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[.18em] text-emerald-700">Fresh2Cook Admin</p><h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">{title}</h1><p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">{subtitle}</p></div>;
}
function Empty({ text }: any) {
  return <div className="p-12 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">{text}</div>;
}







==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\admin-delivery-orders\route.ts ====================

import { NextResponse } from "next/server";
import { prisma } from "@/lib/prisma";

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const assignments =
      await prisma.deliveryAssignment.findMany({
        orderBy: {
          assignedAt: "desc",
        },
      });

    const orders =
      await Promise.all(
        assignments.map(
  async (
    assignment: any
  ) => {
            const order =
              await prisma.order.findUnique({
                where: {
                  id:
                    assignment.orderId,
                },
              });

            const rider =
              await prisma.deliveryBoy.findUnique({
                where: {
                  id:
                    assignment.deliveryBoyId,
                },
              });

            return {
              ...assignment,

              order,

              rider,
            };
          }
        )
      );

    return NextResponse.json(
      orders
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.error(
      "ADMIN DELIVERY ORDERS ERROR",
      error
    );

    return NextResponse.json(
      {
        error:
          "Failed To Load Orders",
      },
      {
        status: 500,
      }
    );
  }
}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function AnalyticsV3Page() {
  const [inventory, setInventory] = useState<any>();
  const [segment, setSegment] = useState<any>();
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    Promise.all([
      fetch("/api/analytics/inventory").then(r => r.json()),
      fetch("/api/analytics/segmentation").then(r => r.json()),
    ]).then(([a, b]) => {
      setInventory(a);
      setSegment(b);
    }).catch(() => {}).finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, []);

  const cards = [
    ["Products", inventory?.totalProducts ?? "â€”"],
    ["Stock", inventory?.totalStock ?? "â€”"],
    ["Customers", segment?.totalCustomers ?? "â€”"],
    ["VIP Customers", segment?.vipCustomers ?? "â€”"],
  ];

  return <AnalyticsShell title="Analytics V3" subtitle="Inventory and customer segmentation intelligence.">
    <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
      {cards.map(([title, value]) => <Kpi key={title} title={title} value={loading ? "â€¦" : value} />)}
    </div>
    <Panel title="Analytics Status"><p className="text-sm text-slate-500">{loading ? "Loading live analytics..." : "Live analytics data loaded from the analytics APIs."}</p></Panel>
  </AnalyticsShell>;
}
function AnalyticsShell({ title, subtitle, children }: any) { return <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7"><div className="mx-auto max-w-[1600px]"><Header title={title} subtitle={subtitle}/>{children}</div></div>; }
function Header({ title, subtitle }: any) { return <div className="mb-7"><p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[.18em] text-emerald-700">Fresh2Cook Admin</p><h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38] md:text-4xl">{title}</h1><p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">{subtitle}</p></div>; }
function Kpi({ title, value }: any) { return <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm"><p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500">{title}</p><p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">{value}</p></div>; }
function Panel({ title, children }: any) { return <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm"><h2 className="font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">{title}</h2><div className="mt-4">{children}</div></div>; }

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function AnalyticsV4Page() {
  const [repeat, setRepeat] = useState<any>();
  const [churn, setChurn] = useState<any>();
  const [trend, setTrend] = useState<any>();

  useEffect(() => {
    Promise.all([
      fetch("/api/analytics/repeat-customers").then(r => r.json()),
      fetch("/api/analytics/churn-risk").then(r => r.json()),
      fetch("/api/analytics/revenue-trends").then(r => r.json()),
    ]).then(([a,b,c]) => {
      setRepeat(a); setChurn(b); setTrend(c);
    }).catch(() => {});
  }, []);

  return (
    <Shell title="Executive Analytics V4" subtitle="Repeat customer, churn and revenue intelligence.">
      <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
        <Kpi title="Repeat Customers" value={repeat?.repeatCustomers ?? "â€”"} />
        <Kpi title="Churn Risk" value={churn?.churnRisk ?? "â€”"} />
        <Kpi title="Total Customers" value={repeat?.totalCustomers ?? "â€”"} />
      </div>
      <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
        <h2 className="font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">Revenue Trends</h2>
        <div className="mt-5 space-y-3">
          {trend?.trend?.map((item: any, index: number) => (
            <div key={index} className="flex justify-between rounded-xl bg-slate-50 p-4 text-sm">
              <span className="font-semibold text-slate-600">{item.month}</span>
              <strong className="text-[#123b38]">â‚¹{item.value}</strong>
            </div>
          )) || <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">No trend data available.</p>}
        </div>
      </div>
    </Shell>
  );
}
function Shell({title,subtitle,children}:any){return <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7"><div className="mx-auto max-w-[1600px]"><Header title={title} subtitle={subtitle}/>{children}</div></div>}
function Header({title,subtitle}:any){return <div className="mb-7"><p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[.18em] text-emerald-700">Fresh2Cook Admin</p><h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38] md:text-4xl">{title}</h1><p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">{subtitle}</p></div>}
function Kpi({title,value}:any){return <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm"><p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500">{title}</p><p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">{value}</p></div>}







==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\assign-orders\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function AssignOrdersPage() {
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [boys, setBoys] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [zones, setZones] = useState<any[]>([]);

  const [orderId, setOrderId] = useState("");
  const [deliveryBoyId, setDeliveryBoyId] =
    useState("");

  const [zoneId, setZoneId] =
    useState("");

  const [assignments, setAssignments] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, []);

  async function loadData() {
    try {
      const [
        ordersRes,
        boysRes,
        zonesRes,
        assignmentsRes,
      ] = await Promise.all([
        fetch("/api/orders"),
        fetch("/api/delivery-boys"),
        fetch("/api/delivery-zones"),
        fetch("/api/delivery-assignments"),
      ]);

      const ordersData =
        await ordersRes.json();

      const boysData =
        await boysRes.json();

      const zonesData =
        await zonesRes.json();

      const assignmentsData =
        await assignmentsRes.json();

      setOrders(ordersData);
      setBoys(boysData);
      setZones(zonesData);
      setAssignments(assignmentsData);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
    }
  }

  async function assignOrder() {
    if (
      !orderId ||
      !deliveryBoyId ||
      !zoneId
    ) {
      alert("Select all fields");
      return;
    }

    const res = await fetch(
      "/api/delivery-assignments",
      {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type":
            "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          orderId,
          deliveryBoyId,
          zoneId,
        }),
      }
    );

    if (!res.ok) {
      alert("Assignment Failed");
      return;
    }

    alert("Order Assigned");

    setOrderId("");
    setDeliveryBoyId("");
    setZoneId("");

    loadData();
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto">

        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-8">
          Assign Orders
        </h1>

        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow mb-8">

          <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">

            <select
              value={orderId}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setOrderId(e.target.value)
              }
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            >
              <option value="">
                Select Order
              </option>

              {orders.map((o) => (
                <option
                  key={o.id}
                  value={o.id}
                >
                  Order #{o.id}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>

            <select
              value={deliveryBoyId}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setDeliveryBoyId(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            >
              <option value="">
                Delivery Boy
              </option>

              {boys.map((boy) => (
                <option
                  key={boy.id}
                  value={boy.id}
                >
                  {boy.name}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>

            <select
              value={zoneId}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setZoneId(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            >
              <option value="">
                Delivery Zone
              </option>

              {zones.map((zone) => (
                <option
                  key={zone.id}
                  value={zone.id}
                >
                  {zone.zoneName}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>

          </div>

          <button
            onClick={assignOrder}
            className="mt-5 bg-green-600 text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold"
          >
            Assign Order
          </button>

        </div>

        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">

          {assignments.map(
            (item) => (
              <div
                key={item.id}
                className="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow"
              >
                <h2 className="font-bold text-xl">
                  Order #{item.orderId}
                </h2>

                <p className="mt-2">
                  Delivery Boy ID:
                  {" "}
                  {item.deliveryBoyId}
                </p>

                <p className="mt-2">
                  Zone ID:
                  {" "}
                  {item.zoneId}
                </p>

                <p className="mt-2 text-blue-600 font-bold">
                  OTP:
                  {" "}
                  {item.otp}
                </p>

                <p className="mt-2">
                  Status:
                  {" "}
                  {item.status}
                </p>

              </div>
            )
          )}

        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}







==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\attendance\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function AttendancePage() {
  const [staff, setStaff] = useState<any[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => { loadStaff(); }, []);

  function loadStaff() {
    try {
      const data = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("freshcut-staff") || "[]");
      setStaff(data.map((item: any) => ({ ...item, attendance: item.attendance || "Absent" })));
    } catch { setStaff([]); }
  }

  function updateAttendance(id: number, status: string) {
    const updated = staff.map(item => item.id === id ? { ...item, attendance: status } : item);
    localStorage.setItem("freshcut-staff", JSON.stringify(updated));
    setStaff(updated);
  }

  const present = staff.filter(s => s.attendance === "Present").length;
  const absent = staff.filter(s => s.attendance === "Absent").length;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1500px]">
        <Header title="Staff Attendance" subtitle="Daily attendance control for the Fresh2Cook team." />
        <div className="mb-6 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
          <Kpi title="Total Staff" value={staff.length} />
          <Kpi title="Present" value={present} green />
          <Kpi title="Absent" value={absent} red />
        </div>
        <div className="grid gap-4">
          {staff.map(employee => (
            <div key={employee.id} className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">{employee.name}</h2>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">{employee.role || "Staff"} Â· {employee.mobile || "â€”"}</p>
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                  <button onClick={() => updateAttendance(employee.id, "Present")} className="rounded-xl bg-emerald-50 px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-emerald-700">Present</button>
                  <button onClick={() => updateAttendance(employee.id, "Absent")} className="rounded-xl bg-red-50 px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-red-700">Absent</button>
                </div>
              </div>
              <span className={`mt-4 inline-block rounded-full px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold ${employee.attendance === "Present" ? "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700" : "bg-red-100 text-red-700"}`}>{employee.attendance}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
          {!staff.length && <Empty text="No staff records available." />}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
function Header({ title, subtitle }: any) { return <div className="mb-7"><p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[.18em] text-emerald-700">Fresh2Cook Admin</p><h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">{title}</h1><p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">{subtitle}</p></div>; }
function Kpi({ title, value, green, red }: any) { return <div className={`rounded-2xl border p-5 shadow-sm ${green ? "border-emerald-100 bg-emerald-50" : red ? "border-red-100 bg-red-50" : "border-slate-200 bg-white"}`}><p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500">{title}</p><p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">{value}</p></div>; }
function Empty({ text }: any) { return <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-white p-10 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">{text}</div>; }







==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\auto-purchase-orders\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function AutoPurchaseOrdersPage() {
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<any[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => { generateOrders(); }, []);

  function generateOrders() {
    try {
      const products = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("freshcut-products") || "[]");
      const purchaseOrders = products
        .filter((item:any) => Number(item.stock || 0) < 20)
        .map((item:any) => ({
          id: Date.now() + Math.random(),
          product: item.name,
          currentStock: item.stock,
          requiredQty: 100 - Number(item.stock || 0),
          vendor: item.vendor || "Default Vendor",
          status: "Pending",
          date: new Date().toLocaleDateString(),
        }));
      setOrders(purchaseOrders);
    } catch { setOrders([]); }
  }

  function updateStatus(id:number, status:string) {
    setOrders(prev => prev.map(item => item.id === id ? {...item, status} : item));
  }

  const pending = orders.filter(o => o.status === "Pending").length;
  const approved = orders.filter(o => o.status === "Approved").length;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1600px]">
        <Header title="Auto Purchase Orders" subtitle="Automatically identify low-stock products and prepare purchase orders." />

        <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
          <Kpi title="Generated PO" value={orders.length} />
          <Kpi title="Pending" value={pending} red />
          <Kpi title="Approved" value={approved} green />
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm">
          <div className="border-b p-6">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">Purchase Orders</h2>
          </div>

          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-sm">
              <thead className="bg-slate-50">
                <tr>
                  {["Product","Current Stock","Required","Vendor","Status","Action"].map(h => (
                    <th key={h} className="whitespace-nowrap px-5 py-4 text-left font-bold text-slate-600">{h}</th>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {orders.map(item => (
                  <tr key={item.id} className="border-t border-slate-100">
                    <td className="px-5 py-4 font-bold text-[#123b38]">{item.product}</td>
                    <td className="px-5 py-4">{item.currentStock}</td>
                    <td className="px-5 py-4 font-bold text-red-600">{item.requiredQty}</td>
                    <td className="px-5 py-4">{item.vendor}</td>
                    <td className="px-5 py-4">
                      <span className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold ${
                        item.status === "Approved" ? "bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700" :
                        item.status === "Sent To Vendor" ? "bg-blue-100 text-blue-700" :
                        "bg-amber-100 text-amber-700"
                      }`}>{item.status}</span>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-5 py-4">
                      <div className="flex gap-2">
                        <button onClick={() => updateStatus(item.id,"Approved")} className="rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-emerald-700">Approve</button>
                        <button onClick={() => { updateStatus(item.id,"Sent To Vendor"); alert("Purchase Order Sent To Vendor"); }} className="rounded-lg bg-blue-50 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-blue-700">Send</button>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>

            {!orders.length && <div className="p-12 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">No low-stock purchase orders generated.</div>}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Header({title,subtitle}:any) {
  return <div className="mb-7"><p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[.18em] text-emerald-700">Fresh2Cook Admin</p><h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38] md:text-4xl">{title}</h1><p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">{subtitle}</p></div>;
}
function Kpi({title,value,green,red}:any) {
  return <div className={`rounded-2xl border p-6 shadow-sm ${green?"border-emerald-100 bg-emerald-50":red?"border-red-100 bg-red-50":"border-slate-200 bg-white"}`}><p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500">{title}</p><p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">{value}</p></div>;
}

==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\backup\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function BackupPage() {
  const [backupSize,setBackupSize] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => { calculateSize(); }, []);

  function calculateSize() {
    let size = 0;
    for(let i=0;i<localStorage.length;i++){
      const key=localStorage.key(i);
      if(key) size += key.length + (localStorage.getItem(key)||"").length;
    }
    setBackupSize(Math.round(size/1024));
  }

  function exportBackup() {
    const data:any = {};
    for(let i=0;i<localStorage.length;i++){
      const key=localStorage.key(i);
      if(key) data[key]=localStorage.getItem(key);
    }
    const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(data,null,2)],{type:"application/json"});
    const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a=document.createElement("a");
    a.href=url;
    a.download="freshcut-backup.json";
    a.click();
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  }

  function importBackup(event:any) {
    const file=event.target.files?.[0];
    if(!file)return;
    const reader=new FileReader();
    reader.onload=(e:any)=>{
      try{
        const data=JSON.parse(e.target.result);
        Object.keys(data).forEach(key=>localStorage.setItem(key,data[key]));
        alert("Backup Restored Successfully");
        calculateSize();
      }catch{ alert("Invalid Backup File"); }
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
  }

  function clearDatabase() {
    if(!confirm("Delete Complete Database?"))return;
    localStorage.clear();
    alert("Database Cleared");
    window.location.reload();
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl">
        <Header title="Backup & Restore" subtitle="Protect and restore Fresh2Cook browser data." />

        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-7 shadow-sm">
          <div className="rounded-2xl bg-[#063f3d] p-7 text-white">
            <p className="text-sm text-emerald-100">Current Local Data Size</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-5xl font-extrabold">{backupSize} KB</p>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6 grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
            <button onClick={exportBackup} className="rounded-xl bg-[#063f3d] py-4 font-bold text-white">Export Backup</button>
            <label className="cursor-pointer rounded-xl bg-blue-600 py-4 text-center font-bold text-white">
              Import Backup
              <input type="file" accept=".json" onChange={importBackup} className="hidden"/>
            </label>
            <button onClick={clearDatabase} className="rounded-xl bg-red-50 py-4 font-bold text-red-700">Clear Database</button>
          </div>

          <p className="mt-5 text-xs text-slate-500">Export creates a JSON backup of the current browser localStorage data.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Header({title,subtitle}:any){return <div className="mb-7"><p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[.18em] text-emerald-700">Fresh2Cook Admin</p><h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38] md:text-4xl">{title}</h1><p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">{subtitle}</p></div>}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function BannersPage() {
  const [banners, setBanners] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [image, setImage] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadBanners();
  }, []);

  function loadBanners() {
    try {
      setBanners(
        JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("freshcut-banners") || "[]")
      );
    } catch {
      setBanners([]);
    }
  }

  function addBanner() {
    if (!title || !image) {
      alert("Enter Banner Title & Image URL");
      return;
    }

    const banner = {
      id: Date.now(),
      title,
      image,
      active: true,
    };

    const updated = [banner, ...banners];
    localStorage.setItem("freshcut-banners", JSON.stringify(updated));
    setBanners(updated);
    setTitle("");
    setImage("");
  }

  function toggleBanner(id: number) {
    const updated = banners.map((banner) =>
      banner.id === id
        ? { ...banner, active: !banner.active }
        : banner
    );

    localStorage.setItem("freshcut-banners", JSON.stringify(updated));
    setBanners(updated);
  }

  function deleteBanner(id: number) {
    if (!confirm("Delete Banner?")) return;

    const updated = banners.filter((banner) => banner.id !== id);
    localStorage.setItem("freshcut-banners", JSON.stringify(updated));
    setBanners(updated);
  }

  const activeCount = banners.filter((banner) => banner.active).length;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1600px]">
        <div className="mb-7">
          <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-emerald-700">
            Fresh2Cook Admin
          </p>
          <h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38] md:text-4xl">
            Banner Management
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
            Manage promotional banners displayed in the system.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-7 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500">
              Total Banners
            </p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
              {banners.length}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-100 bg-emerald-50 p-5 shadow-sm">
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-emerald-700">
              Active Banners
            </p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-extrabold text-emerald-800">
              {activeCount}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-7 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
            Add New Banner
          </h2>

          <div className="mt-5 grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
            <input
              value={title}
              onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Banner Title"
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
            />

            <input
              value={image}
              onChange={(e) => setImage(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Banner Image URL"
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
            />

            <button
              onClick={addBanner}
              className="rounded-xl bg-[#063f3d] py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white hover:bg-[#0a514d]"
            >
              + Add Banner
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
          {banners.map((banner) => (
            <div
              key={banner.id}
              className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white shadow-sm"
            >
              <img
                src={banner.image}
                alt={banner.title}
                className="h-56 w-full object-cover"
              />

              <div className="p-5">
                <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                  <h2 className="font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
                    {banner.title}
                  </h2>

                  <span
                    className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold ${
                      banner.active
                        ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700"
                        : "bg-slate-100 text-slate-500"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {banner.active ? "Active" : "Inactive"}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div className="mt-5 flex gap-2">
                  <button
                    onClick={() => toggleBanner(banner.id)}
                    className="flex-1 rounded-xl bg-blue-50 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-100"
                  >
                    Toggle
                  </button>

                  <button
                    onClick={() => deleteBanner(banner.id)}
                    className="flex-1 rounded-xl bg-red-50 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-red-700 hover:bg-red-100"
                  >
                    Delete
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}

          {!banners.length && (
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-white p-12 text-center text-sm text-slate-500 md:col-span-2 xl:col-span-3">
              No banners available.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect,useState } from "react";

export default function AdminProductsPage() {
  const [products,setProducts]=useState<any[]>([]);
  const [name,setName]=useState("");
  const [price,setPrice]=useState("");
  const [stock,setStock]=useState("");
  const [pack,setPack]=useState("");
  const [image,setImage]=useState("");

  useEffect(()=>loadProducts(),[]);

  function loadProducts(){
    try{setProducts(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("freshcut-products")||"[]"))}
    catch{setProducts([])}
  }

  function addProduct(){
    if(!name.trim()||!price){alert("Product Name & Price Required");return}
    const product={id:Date.now(),name:name.trim(),price:Number(price),stock,pack,image};
    const updated=[product,...products];
    localStorage.setItem("freshcut-products",JSON.stringify(updated));
    setProducts(updated);
    setName("");setPrice("");setStock("");setPack("");setImage("");
    alert("Product Added Successfully");
  }

  function deleteProduct(id:number){
    const updated=products.filter(p=>p.id!==id);
    localStorage.setItem("freshcut-products",JSON.stringify(updated));
    setProducts(updated);
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7">

            <input value={name} onChange={e=>setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="Product Name" className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 text-sm"/>
            <input value={price} onChange={e=>setPrice(e.target.value)} placeholder="Price" className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 text-sm"/>
            <input value={stock} onChange={e=>setStock(e.target.value)} placeholder="Stock" className="rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 text-sm"/>

          </div>
          <input value={image} onChange={e=>setImage(e.target.value)} placeholder="Image URL" className="mt-4 w-full rounded-xl border px-4 py-3 text-sm"/>
          <button onClick={addProduct} className="mt-4 rounded-xl bg-[#063f3d] px-7 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white">+ Add Product</button>
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-4">

                  <p>Stock: {product.stock || 0}</p>
                  <p>Pack: {product.pack || "â€”"}</p>

==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\bulk-upload\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useState } from "react";

export default function BulkUploadPage() {
  const [loading,setLoading]=useState(false);
  const [imported,setImported]=useState(0);

  async function uploadCSV(e:any){
    const file=e.target.files?.[0];
    if(!file)return;
    setLoading(true);
    setImported(0);

    try{
      const text=await file.text();
      const rows=text.split(/\r?\n/).filter((row:string)=>row.trim()!=="");
      let count=0;

      for(let i=1;i<rows.length;i++){
        const cols=rows[i].split(",");
        if(cols.length<4)continue;

        const product={
          name:cols[0]?.trim(),
          price:Number(cols[1])||0,
          pack:cols[2]?.trim(),
          stock:Number(cols[3])||0,
          image:cols[4]?.trim()||"",
          category:"Vegetables",
          description:"",
        };

        const res=await fetch("/api/products",{
          method:"POST",
          headers:{"Content-Type":"application/json"},
          body:JSON.stringify(product),
        });

        if(res.ok)count++;
      }

      setImported(count);
      alert(`${count} Products Imported Successfully`);
    }catch(error){
      console.error(error);
      alert("CSV Upload Failed");
    }

    setLoading(false);
    e.target.value="";
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl">
        <Header title="Bulk Product Upload" subtitle="Import multiple products directly from a CSV file." />

        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-7 shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">Upload Product CSV</h2>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">CSV columns must follow this order:</p>

          <div className="mt-5 rounded-xl bg-[#063f3d] p-5 font-mono text-sm text-white">
            name,price,pack,stock,image<br/>
            Fresh2Cook Bhindi,49,500g,100,<br/>
            Fresh2Cook Beans,59,500g,80,
          </div>

          <label className="mt-6 block cursor-pointer rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-emerald-300 bg-emerald-50 p-8 text-center">
            <span className="font-bold text-emerald-800">Choose CSV File</span>
            <input type="file" accept=".csv" onChange={uploadCSV} className="hidden"/>
          </label>

          {loading && <p className="mt-5 font-bold text-emerald-700">Uploading Products...</p>}
          {!loading && imported>0 && <p className="mt-5 font-bold text-emerald-700">{imported} products imported.</p>}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
function Header({title,subtitle}:any){return <div className="mb-7"><p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[.18em] text-emerald-700">Fresh2Cook Admin</p><h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38] md:text-4xl">{title}</h1><p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">{subtitle}</p></div>}

};

        0

==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\categories\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

type Category = {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  image: string;
};

export default function CategoriesPage() {
  const [categories, setCategories] = useState<Category[]>([]);
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [image, setImage] = useState("");
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadCategories();
  }, []);

  function loadCategories() {
    try {
      const data = JSON.parse(
        localStorage.getItem("freshcut-categories") || "[]"
      );
      setCategories(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
    } catch {
      setCategories([]);
    }
  }

  function saveCategories(updated: Category[]) {
    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-categories",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );
    setCategories(updated);
  }

  function addCategory() {
    if (!name.trim()) {
      alert("Enter Category Name");
      return;
    }

    const category: Category = {
      id: Date.now(),
      name: name.trim(),
      image,
    };

    saveCategories([category, ...categories]);
    setName("");
    setImage("");
    alert("Category Added Successfully");
  }

  function deleteCategory(id: number) {
    if (!confirm("Delete Category?")) return;

    saveCategories(
      categories.filter((category) => category.id !== id)
    );
  }

  async function uploadImage(file: File) {
    try {
      setUploading(true);

      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.append("file", file);

      const response = await fetch("/api/upload-file", {
        method: "POST",
        body: formData,
      });

      const data = await response.json();
      return data.url || "";
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
      alert("Image upload failed");
      return "";
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
    }
  }

  const filtered = categories.filter((category) =>
    category.name.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())
  );

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f8] p-4 md:p-6">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1500px] space-y-6">

        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#159447]">
              Catalog Management
            </p>
            <h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-bold text-[#123b39]">
              Product Categories
            </h1>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
              Manage the categories displayed across Fresh2Cook.
            </p>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={loadCategories}
            className="rounded-xl bg-[#063f3d] px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-[#0a504c]"
          >
            â†» Refresh
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
          <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-5 border border-gray-100 shadow-sm">
            <p className="text-xs uppercase font-semibold text-gray-400">
              Total Categories
            </p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-[#123b39]">
              {categories.length}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-5 border border-gray-100 shadow-sm">
            <p className="text-xs uppercase font-semibold text-gray-400">
              With Images
            </p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-green-600">
              {categories.filter((c) => !!c.image).length}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="hidden md:block rounded-2xl bg-white p-5 border border-gray-100 shadow-sm">
            <p className="text-xs uppercase font-semibold text-gray-400">
              Storage
            </p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-xl font-bold text-[#123b39]">
              Local Catalog
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white border border-gray-100 p-5 shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-[#123b39]">
            Add Category
          </h2>

          <div className="mt-4 grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-4">
            <input
              value={name}
              onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Category Name"
              className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#159447]"
            />

            <input
              type="file"
              accept="image/*"
              disabled={uploading}
              className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm"
              onChange={async (e) => {
                const file = e.target.files?.[0];
                if (!file) return;

                const url = await uploadImage(file);
                setImage(url);
              }}
            />

            <div className="flex items-center">
              {image ? (
                <img
                  src={image}
                  alt="Preview"
                  className="h-12 w-12 rounded-xl border object-cover"
                />
              ) : (
                <span className="text-sm text-gray-400">
                  No image selected
                </span>
              )}
            </div>

            <button
              onClick={addCategory}
              disabled={uploading}
              className="rounded-xl bg-[#159447] px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white hover:bg-[#117b3b] disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {uploading ? "Uploading..." : "+ Add Category"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white border border-gray-100 shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-b border-gray-100 p-5 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-[#123b39]">
                Category Library
              </h2>
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
                {filtered.length} category(s)
              </p>
            </div>

            <input
              value={search}
              onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Search category..."
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#159447] md:w-80"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 p-5 md:grid-cols-4 lg:grid-cols-6">
            {filtered.map((category) => (
              <div
                key={category.id}
                className="rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 bg-[#fbfcfc] p-3"
              >
                <img
                  src={category.image || "/placeholder-product.png"}
                  alt={category.name}
                  className="h-28 w-full rounded-xl bg-white object-contain"
                />

                <input
                  value={category.name}
                  onChange={(e) => {
                    const updated = categories.map((c) =>
                      c.id === category.id
                        ? { ...c, name: e.target.value }
                        : c
                    );
                    saveCategories(updated);
                  }}
                  className="mt-3 w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-200 bg-white px-2 py-2 text-sm font-semibold outline-none focus:border-[#159447]"
                />

                <input
                  type="file"
                  accept="image/*"
                  className="mt-2 w-full text-xs"
                  onChange={async (e) => {
                    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
                    if (!file) return;

                    const url = await uploadImage(file);

                    const updated = categories.map((c) =>
                      c.id === category.id
                        ? { ...c, image: url }
                        : c
                    );

                    saveCategories(updated);
                  }}
                />

                <button
                  onClick={() => deleteCategory(category.id)}
                  className="mt-3 w-full rounded-lg bg-red-50 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-red-600 hover:bg-red-100"
                >
                  Delete
                </button>
              </div>
            ))}

            {filtered.length === 0 && (
              <div className="col-span-full p-10 text-center text-gray-500">
                No categories found.
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function CEOCommandCenter2() {
  const [stats, setStats] = useState({
    revenue: 0,
    orders: 0,
    customers: 0,
    products: 0,
    vendors: 0,
    franchises: 0,
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, []);

  function loadData() {
    const orders = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem("freshcut-orders") || "[]"
    );

    const customers = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem("freshcut-customers") || "[]"
    );

    const products = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem("freshcut-products") || "[]"
    );

    const vendors = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem("freshcut-vendors") || "[]"
    );

    const franchises = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem("freshcut-franchises") || "[]"
    );

    const revenue = orders.reduce(
      (sum: number, item: any) =>
        sum + Number(item.total || 0),
      0
    );

    setStats({
      revenue,
      orders: orders.length,
      customers: customers.length,
      products: products.length,
      vendors: vendors.length,
      franchises: franchises.length,
    });
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto">

        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-8">
          CEO Command Center 2.0

          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow">
            <p>Total Revenue</p>

==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\coupons\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function CouponsPage() {
  const [coupons, setCoupons] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  const [code, setCode] =
    useState("");

  const [discount, setDiscount] =
    useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadCoupons();
  }, []);

  function loadCoupons() {
    const data = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem(
        "freshcut-coupons"
      ) || "[]"
    );

    setCoupons(data);
  }

  function addCoupon() {
    if (!code || !discount) {
      alert(
        "Enter Coupon Code & Discount"
      );
      return;
    }

    const newCoupon = {
      id: Date.now(),
      code:
        code.toUpperCase(),
      discount:
        Number(discount),
    };

    const updated = [
      newCoupon,
      ...coupons,
    ];

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-coupons",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setCoupons(updated);

    setCode("");
    setDiscount("");

    alert(
      "Coupon Added Successfully"
    );
  }

  function deleteCoupon(
    id: number
  ) {
    if (
      !confirm(
        "Delete Coupon?"
      )
    )
      return;

    const updated =
      coupons.filter(
        (coupon) =>
          coupon.id !== id
      );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-coupons",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setCoupons(updated);
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">

      <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto">

        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-8">
          Coupon Management
        </h1>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6 mb-8">

          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">
            Add New Coupon
          </h2>

          <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">

            <input
              type="text"
              value={code}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setCode(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              placeholder="Coupon Code"
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

            <input
              type="number"
              value={discount}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setDiscount(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              placeholder="Discount %"
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

            <button
              onClick={
                addCoupon
              }
              className="bg-green-600 text-white rounded-xl font-bold"
            >
              Add Coupon
            </button>

          </div>

            Available Coupons
          </h2>

          0 ? (
            <p className="text-gray-500">
              No Coupons Found
            </p>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-4">

              {coupons.map(
                (coupon) => (

                    key={
                      coupon.id

                    className="border rounded-2xl p-4 flex justify-between items-center"

                    <div>

                        {
                          coupon.code
                        }
                      </h3>

                      <p className="text-green-600 font-bold">
                        {
                          coupon.discount
                        }
                        % Discount
                      </p>

                    </div>

                    <button
                      onClick={() =>
                        deleteCoupon(
                          coupon.id
                        )
                      }
                      className="bg-red-600 text-white px-5 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold"
                    >
                      Delete
                    </button>

                  </div>

                )
              )}

          )}

  );

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function CustomerFeedbackPage() {
  const [feedbacks,
    setFeedbacks] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  const [loading,
    setLoading] =
    useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadFeedbacks();
  }, []);

  async function loadFeedbacks() {
    try {
      const res =
        await fetch(
          "/api/admin-delivery-performance"
        );

      const data =
        await res.json();

      setFeedbacks(
        Array.isArray(data)
          ? data
          : []
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  function getBadge(
    rating: number
  ) {
    if (rating >= 4.8)
      return "ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã‚Â¥Ã¢â‚¬Â¡ Gold";

    if (rating >= 4.5)
      return "ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã‚Â¥Ã‹â€  Silver";

    if (rating >= 4)
      return "ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã‚Â¥Ã¢â‚¬Â° Bronze";

    return "ÃƒÂ¢Ã‚Â­Ã‚Â Standard";
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="p-6">
        Loading...
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6 space-y-6">

                  4.8
              ).length
            }
          </h2>

        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-5">

          <p>
            Silver Riders
          </p>

          <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-gray-600 mt-2">
            {
              feedbacks.filter(
                (
                  rider
                ) =>
                  rider.rating >=
                    4.5 &&
                  rider.rating <
                    4.8
              ).length
            }
          </h2>

        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border p-5">

          <p>
            Bronze Riders
          </p>

          <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-orange-600 mt-2">
            {
              feedbacks.filter(
                (
                  rider
                ) =>
                  rider.rating >=
                    4 &&
                  rider.rating <
                    4.5
              ).length
            }
          </h2>

        </div>

      </div>

      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border overflow-hidden">

        <div className="p-5 border-b">

          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">
            Rider Leaderboard
          </h2>

        </div>

        <table className="w-full">

          <thead className="bg-gray-50">

            <tr>

              <th className="text-left p-4">
                Rider
              </th>

              <th className="text-left p-4">
                Rating
              </th>

                Deliveries

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function CustomerNotificationsPage() {
  const [notifications,
    setNotifications] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, []);

  function loadData() {
    const data = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem(
        "customer-notifications"
      ) || "[]"
    );

    setNotifications(data);
  }

  function addDemoNotification() {
    const notification = {
      id: Date.now(),
      customer:
        "Demo Customer",
      phone:
        "9999999999",
      message:
        "Order Out For Delivery",
      createdAt:
        new Date().toLocaleString(),
    };

    const updated = [
      notification,
      ...notifications,
    ];

    localStorage.setItem(
      "customer-notifications",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setNotifications(updated);
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6 space-y-6">

      <div className="flex justify-between items-center">

        <div>

          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">
            Customer Notifications
          </h1>

          <p className="text-gray-500">
            Delivery SMS / WhatsApp Logs
          </p>

        </div>

        <button
          onClick={
            addDemoNotification
          }
          className="bg-green-600 text-white px-5 py-3 rounded-xl"
        >
          Test Notification
        </button>

      </div>

      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border overflow-hidden">

        <table className="w-full">

          <thead className="bg-gray-50">

            <tr>

              <th className="p-4 text-left">
                Customer
              </th>

              <th className="p-4 text-left">
                Phone
              </th>

                Message
              </th>

              <th className="p-4 text-left">
                Date

            0 ? (

                  No Notifications

                    className="border-t"

                    <td className="p-4">

==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\customers\[phone]\page.tsx ====================



==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\customers\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";

type Order = {
  id: number;
  customer: string;
  phone?: string | null;
  address?: string | null;
  amount: number;
  status: string;
  createdAt: string;
};

type Feedback = {
  id: number;
  customer: string;
  phone: string;
  rating: number;
  comment: string;
  createdAt: string;
};

type Customer = {
  name: string;
  phone: string;
  address: string;
  orders: number;
  totalSpend: number;
  lastOrder: string;
  status: string;
  rating: number | null;
};

export default function AdminCustomersPage() {
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<Order[]>([]);
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState<Feedback[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState("All");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, []);

  async function loadData() {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      const [ordersRes, feedbackRes] = await Promise.all([
        fetch("/api/orders", { cache: "no-store" }),
        fetch("/api/feedback", { cache: "no-store" }),
      ]);

      const ordersData = await ordersRes.json();
      const feedbackData = await feedbackRes.json();

      if (!ordersRes.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          ordersData?.error || "Failed to load orders"
        );
      }

      if (!feedbackRes.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          feedbackData?.error || "Failed to load feedback"
        );
      }

      setOrders(
        Array.isArray(ordersData)
          ? ordersData
          : ordersData?.orders || []
      );

      setFeedback(
        Array.isArray(feedbackData)
          ? feedbackData
          : feedbackData?.feedback || []
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("CUSTOMERS LOAD ERROR:", error);

      setError(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "Failed to load customers"
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  const customers = useMemo<Customer[]>(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, Customer>();

    for (const order of orders) {
      const phone = String(order.phone || "").trim();

      const key =
        phone ||
        order.customer.trim().toLowerCase();

      const existing = map.get(key);

      if (existing) {
        existing.orders += 1;
        existing.totalSpend += Number(
          order.amount || 0
        );

        if (
          new Date(order.createdAt).getTime() >
          new Date(existing.lastOrder).getTime()
        ) {
          existing.lastOrder = order.createdAt;
          existing.address =
            order.address || existing.address;
          existing.status = order.status;
        }

        continue;
      }

      const customerReviews = feedback.filter(
        (item) =>
          item.phone === phone ||
          item.customer.trim().toLowerCase() ===
            order.customer.trim().toLowerCase()
      );

      const rating =
        customerReviews.length > 0
          ? customerReviews.reduce(
              (sum, item) =>
                sum + Number(item.rating || 0),
              0
            ) / customerReviews.length
          : null;

      map.set(key, {
        name: order.customer,
        phone,
        address: order.address || "",
        orders: 1,
        totalSpend: Number(order.amount || 0),
        lastOrder: order.createdAt,
        status: order.status,
        rating,
      });
    }

    return Array.from(map.values()).sort(
      (a, b) => b.totalSpend - a.totalSpend
    );
  }, [orders, feedback]);

  const statuses = useMemo(() => {
    const values = customers
      .map((customer) => customer.status)
      .filter(Boolean);

    return [
      "All",
      ...Array.from(new Set(values)),
    ];
  }, [customers]);

  const filteredCustomers = useMemo(() => {
    const query = search.trim().toLowerCase();

    return customers.filter((customer) => {
      const matchesSearch =
        !query ||
        customer.name
          .toLowerCase()
          .includes(query) ||
        customer.phone
          .toLowerCase()
          .includes(query) ||
        customer.address
          .toLowerCase()
          .includes(query);

      const matchesStatus =
        statusFilter === "All" ||
        customer.status === statusFilter;

      return matchesSearch && matchesStatus;
    });
  }, [customers, search, statusFilter]);

  const totalCustomers = customers.length;

  const repeatCustomers = customers.filter(
    (customer) => customer.orders > 1
  ).length;

  const totalCustomerValue = customers.reduce(
    (sum, customer) =>
      sum + customer.totalSpend,
    0
  );

  const averageOrderValue =
    orders.length > 0
      ? Math.round(
          orders.reduce(
            (sum, order) =>
              sum + Number(order.amount || 0),
            0
          ) / orders.length
        )
      : 0;

  const activeCustomers = customers.filter(
    (customer) =>
      !["Delivered", "Cancelled"].includes(
        customer.status
      )
  ).length;

  function openWhatsApp(customer: Customer) {
    const cleanedPhone =
      customer.phone.replace(/\D/g, "");

    if (!cleanedPhone) {
      alert(
        "Customer mobile number is not available."
      );
      return;
    }

    const number =
      cleanedPhone.length === 10
        ? `91${cleanedPhone}`
        : cleanedPhone;

    const message = [
      `Hello ${customer.name},`,
      "",
      "Thank you for being a Fresh2Cook customer! ??",
      "",
      "How can we help you today?",
    ].join("\n");

    window.open(
      `https://wa.me/${number}?text=${encodeURIComponent(
        message
      )}`,
      "_blank",
      "noopener,noreferrer"
    );
  }

  function formatDate(date: string) {
    if (!date) return "ï¿½";

    const parsed = new Date(date);

    if (Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime())) {
      return "ï¿½";
    }

    return parsed.toLocaleDateString(
      "en-IN",
      {
        day: "2-digit",
        month: "short",
        year: "numeric",
      }
    );
  }

  function getStatusClass(status: string) {
    const value = status.toLowerCase();

    if (value.includes("deliver")) {
      return "bg-green-50 text-green-700 border-green-200";
    }

    if (value.includes("cancel")) {
      return "bg-red-50 text-red-700 border-red-200";
    }

    if (value.includes("assign")) {
      return "bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border-blue-200";
    }

    if (value.includes("process")) {
      return "bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border-purple-200";
    }

    return "bg-orange-50 text-orange-700 border-orange-200";
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f4f7f7] p-4 md:p-6">
      <div className="max-w-[1500px] mx-auto space-y-6">

        {/* HEADER */}
        <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-2xl shadow-sm overflow-hidden">

          <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-[#063f3d] to-[#0b625c] px-6 py-7 text-white">

            <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between gap-5">

              <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-white/10 border border-white/20 flex items-center justify-center text-2xl">
                  ??
                </div>

                <div>
                  <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold">
                    Customer Management
                  </h1>

                  <p className="text-sm text-white/70 mt-1">
                    Customers, orders, spending and activity
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <button
                onClick={loadData}
                className="bg-white text-[#063f3d] px-5 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm hover:bg-gray-100 transition"
              >
                ? Refresh Customers
              </button>

            </div>
          </div>

          {/* KPI */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5 divide-x divide-gray-100">

            <div className="p-5">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-gray-400">
                Total Customers
              </p>
              <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-[#063f3d] mt-1">
                {totalCustomers}
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="p-5">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-gray-400">
                Repeat Customers
              </p>
              <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-[#159447] mt-1">
                {repeatCustomers}
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="p-5">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-gray-400">
                Customer Value
              </p>
              <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-blue-600 mt-1">
                ?{totalCustomerValue.toLocaleString("en-IN")}
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="p-5">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-gray-400">
                Avg Order Value
              </p>
              <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-purple-600 mt-1">
                ?{averageOrderValue.toLocaleString("en-IN")}
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="p-5">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-gray-400">
                Active Customers
              </p>
              <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-orange-600 mt-1">
                {activeCustomers}
              </p>
            </div>

          </div>
        </div>

        {/* ERROR */}
        {error && (
          <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 rounded-2xl p-4 font-semibold">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* FILTER */}
        <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-2xl shadow-sm p-4">

          <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-3">

            <div className="relative flex-1">
              <span className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400">
                ??
              </span>

              <input
                type="text"
                value={search}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setSearch(e.target.value)
                }
                placeholder="Search customer, mobile or address..."
                className="w-full border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl pl-11 pr-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#159447] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#159447]/10"
              />
            </div>

            <select
              value={statusFilter}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setStatusFilter(e.target.value)
              }
              className="md:w-56 border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold outline-none focus:border-[#159447]"
            >
              {statuses.map((status) => (
                <option
                  key={status}
                  value={status}
                >
                  {status === "All"
                    ? "All Statuses"
                    : status}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>

          </div>

          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-3">
            Showing {filteredCustomers.length} of{" "}
            {customers.length} customers
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* CUSTOMER LIST */}
        {loading ? (
          <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-2xl shadow-sm p-12 text-center">
            <div className="w-10 h-10 border-4 border-[#159447] border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto" />
            <p className="mt-4 font-semibold text-gray-500">
              Loading Customers...
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : filteredCustomers.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-2xl shadow-sm p-14 text-center">

            <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-gray-100 flex items-center justify-center text-3xl mx-auto">
              ??
            </div>

            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-[#063f3d] mt-5">
              No Customers Found
            </h2>

            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mt-2">
              Customers will appear here after orders are created.
            </p>

          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-5">

            {filteredCustomers.map((customer) => (

              <div
                key={
                  customer.phone ||
                  customer.name
                }
                className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-2xl shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition overflow-hidden"
              >

                {/* CARD HEADER */}
                <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-[#063f3d] to-[#0b625c] p-5 text-white">

                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">

                      <div className="w-11 h-11 shrink-0 rounded-full bg-white/10 border border-white/20 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-lg">
                        {customer.name
                          ?.charAt(0)
                          ?.toUpperCase() || "C"}
                      </div>

                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <h2 className="font-bold text-lg truncate">
                          {customer.name}
                        </h2>

                        <p className="text-white/70 text-xs mt-1">
                          {customer.phone || "Mobile unavailable"}
                        </p>
                      </div>

                    </div>

                    <div className="text-right shrink-0">
                      <div className="font-extrabold text-green-300">
                        ?{customer.totalSpend.toLocaleString("en-IN")}
                      </div>

                      <div className="text-[10px] text-white/60">
                        Lifetime Spend
                      </div>
                    </div>

                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="p-5">

                  {/* STATUS */}
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">

                    <span
                      className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full border text-[11px] font-bold ${getStatusClass(
                        customer.status
                      )}`}

                      {customer.status || "Unknown"}

                        Rating
                      </p>

                        {customer.rating !== null
                          ? `${customer.rating.toFixed(1)} ?`
                          : "ï¿½"}
                      </p>
                    </div>

                        Avg Order

                                customer.orders

                    WhatsApp Customer
                  </button>

              </div>

            ))}

          </div>

      </div>

  );

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function DeliveryPage() {
  const [riders, setRiders] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [alerts, setAlerts] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();

    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      loadData();
    }, 10000);

    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  async function loadData() {
    try {
      const riderRes = await fetch(
        "/api/delivery-live-status"
      );

      const riderData =
        await riderRes.json();

      setRiders(
        Array.isArray(riderData)
          ? riderData
          : []
      );

      const alertRes = await fetch(
        "/api/admin-delivery-alerts"
      );

      const alertData =
        await alertRes.json();

      const pendingAlerts =
        alertData.alerts || [];

      setAlerts(pendingAlerts);

      if (pendingAlerts.length > 0) {
        const audio = new Audio(
          "/warning.mp3"
        );

        audio.play().catch(() => {});
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6">

      {/* ALERT POPUP */}

      {alerts.length > 0 && (
        <div className="mb-6 bg-red-600 text-white p-5 rounded-2xl shadow-lg animate-pulse">

                ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã…Â¡Ã‚Â¨ Delivery Alert

                {alerts.length} Order(s)
                Rejected / Expired

        <button
          onClick={loadData}
          className="bg-green-600 text-white px-5 py-3 rounded-xl"
        >
          Refresh
        </button>

      </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-10 text-center">

                  <h2 className="font-bold text-xl">
                    {rider.name}
                  </h2>

                  <p className="text-gray-500">
                    {rider.mobile}
                  </p>

                </div>

              </div>

              <div className="mt-5 space-y-2">

                <p>
                  <strong>Status:</strong>{" "}
                  <span
                    className={
                      rider.status ===
