"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function DeliveryAlertsPage() {
  const [alerts, setAlerts] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  const [showPopup, setShowPopup] =
    useState(false);

  const [latestAlert, setLatestAlert] =
    useState<any>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadAlerts();

    const interval =
      setInterval(() => {
        loadAlerts();
      }, 10000);

    return () =>
      clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  async function playWarning() {
    try {
      const audio =
        new Audio(
          "/warning.mp3"
        );

      audio.volume = 1;

      await audio.play();
    } catch (error) {
      console.log(error);
    }
  }

  async function loadAlerts() {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(
        "/api/admin-delivery-alerts"
      );

      const data =
        await res.json();

      const list =
        Array.isArray(data)
          ? data
          : [];

      setAlerts(list);

      if (list.length > 0) {
        setLatestAlert(list[0]);

        setShowPopup(true);

        playWarning();
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
    }
  }

  function openManualAssign(
    assignmentId: number
  ) {
    window.location.href =
      `/admin/manual-assign?assignmentId=${assignmentId}`;
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6">

      <div className="mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">
          Delivery Alerts
        </h1>

        <p className="text-gray-500">
          Rider Missed Pickup Monitoring
        </p>
      </div>

      {showPopup &&
        latestAlert && (
          <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/60 flex items-center justify-center z-50">

            <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-8 w-[500px]">

              <div className="text-center">

                <div className="text-6xl mb-3">
                  ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã…Â¡Ã‚Â¨
                </div>

                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-red-600">
                  Delivery Alert
                </h2>

                <p className="mt-4">
                  Assignment #
                  {
                    latestAlert.id
                  }
                </p>

                <p>
                  Rider did not
                  accept order in
                  time.
                </p>

                <div className="flex gap-3 mt-6">

                  <button
                    onClick={() =>
                      openManualAssign(
                        latestAlert.id
                      )
                    }
                    className="flex-1 bg-red-600 text-white py-3 rounded-xl"
                  >
                    Assign Manually
                  </button>

                  <button
                    onClick={() =>
                      setShowPopup(
                        false
                      )
                    }
                    className="flex-1 border py-3 rounded-xl"
                  >
                    Close
                  </button>

                </div>

              </div>

            </div>

          </div>
        )}

      <div className="space-y-4">

        {alerts.map(
          (alert) => (
            <div
              key={alert.id}
              className="bg-white border rounded-2xl p-5"
            >
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center">

                <div>

                  <h3 className="font-bold">
                    Assignment #
                    {alert.id}
                  </h3>

                  <p>
                    Order :
                    {
                      alert.orderId
                    }
                  </p>

                  <p>
                    Rider :
                    {
                      alert.deliveryBoyId
                    }
                  </p>

                  <p>
                    Status :
                    {
                      alert.status
                    }
                  </p>

                </div>

                <button
                  onClick={() =>
                    openManualAssign(
                      alert.id
                    )
                  }
                  className="bg-red-600 text-white px-5 py-3 rounded-xl"
                >
                  Assign Now
                </button>

              </div>
            </div>
          )
        )}

        {alerts.length ===
          0 && (
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-10 text-center">
            No Alerts Found
          </div>
        )}

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\delivery-boys\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";

interface DeliveryBoy {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  mobile: string;
  vehicle: string;
  status: string;
  createdAt: string;
}

export default function DeliveryBoysPage() {
  const [boys, setBoys] = useState<DeliveryBoy[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [mobile, setMobile] = useState("");
  const [vehicle, setVehicle] = useState("");
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadBoys();
  }, []);

  async function loadBoys() {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      const res = await fetch("/api/delivery-boys", { cache: "no-store" });
      const data = await res.json();

      setBoys(
        Array.isArray(data)
          ? data
          : Array.isArray(data?.riders)
          ? data.riders
          : []
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
      alert("Failed To Load Delivery Boys");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  async function addBoy() {
    if (!name.trim() || !mobile.trim() || !vehicle.trim()) {
      alert("Please fill all fields");
      return;
    }

    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/delivery-boys", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          name: name.trim(),
          mobile: mobile.trim(),
          vehicle: vehicle.trim(),
        }),
      });

      const data = await res.json();

      if (!res.ok || data?.success === false) {
        throw new Error(data?.error || "Failed to add delivery boy");
      }

      setName("");
      setMobile("");
      setVehicle("");

      await loadBoys();
      alert("Delivery Boy Added Successfully");
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error(error);
      alert(error?.message || "Failed To Add Delivery Boy");
    }
  }

  const filteredBoys = useMemo(() => {
    const q = search.toLowerCase().trim();

    if (!q) return boys;

    return boys.filter((boy) =>
      `${boy.name} ${boy.mobile} ${boy.vehicle} ${boy.status}`
        .toLowerCase()
        .includes(q)
    );
  }, [boys, search]);

  const available = boys.filter(
    (boy) => boy.status === "Available" || boy.status === "Online"
  ).length;

  const busy = boys.filter((boy) => boy.status === "Busy").length;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f8] p-4 md:p-6">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1500px] space-y-6">

        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#159447]">
              Delivery Management
            </p>
            <h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-bold text-[#123b39]">
              Delivery Boys
            </h1>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
              Manage riders, vehicles and availability.
            </p>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={loadBoys}
            className="rounded-xl bg-[#063f3d] px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white shadow-sm transition hover:bg-[#0a504c]"
          >
            â†» Refresh Riders
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 lg:grid-cols-4">
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-400">
              Total Riders
            </p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-[#123b39]">
              {boys.length}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-400">
              Available
            </p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-green-600">
              {available}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-400">
              Busy
            </p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-orange-500">
              {busy}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-gray-400">
              Offline / Other
            </p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-gray-500">
              {Math.max(0, boys.length - available - busy)}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
          <div className="mb-5">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-[#123b39]">
              Add Delivery Boy
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
              Create a new rider profile for Fresh2Cook delivery operations.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
            <input
              value={name}
              onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Rider Name"
              className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none transition focus:border-[#159447] focus:bg-white"
            />

            <input
              value={mobile}
              onChange={(e) => setMobile(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Mobile Number"
              className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none transition focus:border-[#159447] focus:bg-white"
            />

            <input
              value={vehicle}
              onChange={(e) => setVehicle(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Vehicle Number / Type"
              className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none transition focus:border-[#159447] focus:bg-white"
            />
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={addBoy}
            className="mt-4 rounded-xl bg-[#159447] px-6 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-sm transition hover:bg-[#117b3b]"
          >
            + Add Delivery Boy
          </button>
        </div>

          <div className="mb-5 flex flex-col gap-3 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">

                Rider Directory
              </h2>
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">

              </p>
            </div>

            <input
              value={search}
              onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Search rider, mobile, vehicle..."
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#159447] md:w-80"
            />
          </div>

          {loading ? (
            <div className="rounded-xl bg-gray-50 p-12 text-center text-sm text-gray-500">
              Loading riders...
            </div>
          ) : filteredBoys.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="rounded-xl bg-gray-50 p-12 text-center">
              <p className="font-semibold text-gray-600">
                No Delivery Boys Found
              </p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-400">
                Add a rider or change your search.
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
              {filteredBoys.map((boy) => {
                const isAvailable =
                  boy.status === "Available" || boy.status === "Online";

                return (
                  <div
                    key={boy.id}
                    className="rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 bg-[#fbfcfc] p-5 transition hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:shadow-md"
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-[#063f3d] text-lg font-bold text-white">
                          {boy.name?.charAt(0)?.toUpperCase() || "R"}
                        </div>

                        <div>
                          <h3 className="font-bold text-[#123b39]">
                            {boy.name}
                          </h3>
                          <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
                            Rider #{boy.id}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <span
                        className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold ${
                          isAvailable
                            ? "bg-green-100 text-green-700"
                            : boy.status === "Busy"
                            ? "bg-orange-100 text-orange-700"
                            : "bg-gray-100 text-gray-600"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {boy.status}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="mt-5 space-y-3 border-t border-gray-100 pt-4 text-sm">
                      <div className="flex justify-between gap-3">
                        <span className="text-gray-400">Mobile</span>
                        <span className="font-semibold text-gray-700">
                          {boy.mobile}
                        </span>
                      </div>

                      <div className="flex justify-between gap-3">
                        <span className="text-gray-400">Vehicle</span>
                        <span className="font-semibold text-gray-700">
                          {boy.vehicle}
                        </span>
                      </div>

                      <div className="flex justify-between gap-3">
                        <span className="text-gray-400">Joined</span>
                        <span className="font-semibold text-gray-700">
                          {boy.createdAt
                            ? new Date(boy.createdAt).toLocaleDateString()
                            : "â€”"}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function DeliveryControlPage() {
  const [riders, setRiders] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  const [loading, setLoading] =
    useState(false);

  const [assigning, setAssigning] =
    useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadRiders();
  }, []);

  async function loadRiders() {
    try {
      setLoading(true);

      const res = await fetch(
        "/api/delivery-boys"
      );

      const data =
        await res.json();

      setRiders(data || []);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);

      alert(
        "Failed To Load Riders"
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  async function autoAssignOrders() {
    try {
      setAssigning(true);

      const res =
        await fetch(
          "/api/delivery-smart-assign",
          {
            method: "POST",
          }
        );

      const data =
        await res.json();

      if (!res.ok) {
        alert(
          data.error ||
            "Assignment Failed"
        );

        return;
      }

      alert(
        data.message
      );

      loadRiders();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);

      alert(
        "Assignment Failed"
      );
    } finally {
      setAssigning(false);
    }
  }

  const onlineRiders =
    riders.filter(
      (r) =>
        r.status === "Online"
    ).length;

  const offlineRiders =
    riders.filter(
      (r) =>
        r.status !== "Online"
    ).length;

  const totalDeliveries =
    riders.reduce(
      (sum, rider) =>
        sum +
        (rider.completedDeliveries ||
          0),
      0
    );

  const totalEarnings =
    riders.reduce(
      (sum, rider) =>
        sum +
        (rider.earnings || 0),
      0
    );

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto">

        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-8">

          <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold">
            Delivery Control Center
          </h1>

          <div className="flex gap-3">

            <button
              onClick={
                loadRiders
              }
              className="bg-blue-600 text-white px-5 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold"
            >
              Refresh
            </button>

            <button
              onClick={
                autoAssignOrders
              }
              disabled={
                assigning
              }
              className="bg-green-600 text-white px-5 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold"
            >
              {assigning
                ? "Assigning..."
                : "Auto Assign Orders"}
            </button>

          </div>

        </div>

        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-4 gap-5 mb-8">

          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6">

            <p className="text-gray-500">
              Total Riders
            </p>

            <h2 className="text-4xl font-bold">
              {riders.length}
            </h2>

          </div>

          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6">

            <p className="text-gray-500">
              Online Riders
            </p>

            <h2 className="text-4xl font-bold text-green-600">
              {onlineRiders}
            </h2>

          </div>

          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6">

            <p className="text-gray-500">
              Offline Riders
            </p>

            <h2 className="text-4xl font-bold text-red-600">
              {offlineRiders}
            </h2>

          </div>

          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6">

            <p className="text-gray-500">
              Total Deliveries
            </p>

            <h2 className="text-4xl font-bold text-blue-600">
              {totalDeliveries}
            </h2>

          </div>

        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6 mb-8">

          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">
            Delivery Earnings
          </h2>

          <h3 className="text-5xl font-bold text-green-600">
            Ã¢â€šÂ¹{totalEarnings}
          </h3>

        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow overflow-hidden">

          <div className="p-6 border-b">

            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold">
              Rider Status
            </h2>

          </div>

          {loading ? (
            <div className="p-10 text-center">
              Loading...
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">

              <table className="w-full">

                <thead>

                  <tr className="bg-gray-50">

                    <th className="p-4 text-left">
                      Rider
                    </th>

                    <th className="p-4 text-left">
                      Mobile
                    </th>

                    <th className="p-4 text-left">
                      Status
                    </th>

                    <th className="p-4 text-left">
                      Rating
                    </th>

                    <th className="p-4 text-left">
                      Deliveries
                    </th>

                    <th className="p-4 text-left">
                      Earnings
                    </th>

                  </tr>

                </thead>

                <tbody>

                  {riders.map(
                    (rider) => (
                      <tr
                        key={
                          rider.id
                        }
                        className="border-b"
                      >

                        <td className="p-4 font-bold">
                          {
                            rider.name
                          }
                        </td>

                        <td className="p-4">
                          {
                            rider.mobile
                          }
                        </td>

                        <td className="p-4">

                          <span
                            className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-sm font-bold ${
                              rider.status ===
                              "Online"
                                ? "bg-green-100 text-green-700"
                                : "bg-red-100 text-red-700"
                            }`}
                          >
                            {
                              rider.status
                            }
                          </span>

                        </td>

                        <td className="p-4 font-bold text-orange-600">
                          ÃƒÂ¢Ã‚Â­Ã‚Â
                          {" "}
                          {rider.rating ||
                            5}
                        </td>

                        <td className="p-4 font-bold text-blue-600">
                          {
                            rider.completedDeliveries ||
                            0
                          }
                        </td>

                        <td className="p-4 font-bold text-green-600">
                          Ã¢â€šÂ¹
                          {rider.earnings ||
                            0}
                        </td>

                      </tr>
                    )
                  )}

                  {riders.length ===
                    0 && (
                    <tr>

                      <td
                        colSpan={
                          6
                        }
                        className="text-center p-10 text-gray-500"
                      >
                        No Riders Found
                      </td>

                    </tr>
                  )}

                </tbody>

              </table>

            </div>

  );
}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import {
  useEffect,
  useState,
} from "react";

export default function DeliveryControlTowerPage() {
  const [data, setData] =
    useState<any>(null);

  const [loading, setLoading] =
    useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();

    const interval =
      setInterval(
        loadData,
        10000
      );

    return () =>
      clearInterval(
        interval
      );
  }, []);

  async function loadData() {
    try {
      const res =
        await fetch(
          "/api/admin-delivery-control-tower"
        );

      const result =
        await res.json();

      console.error(error);

      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function DeliveryHeatmapPage() {
  const [data, setData] =
    useState<any>(null);

  const [loading, setLoading] =
    useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();

    const interval =
      setInterval(
        loadData,
        15000
      );

    return () =>
      clearInterval(
        interval
      );
  }, []);

  async function loadData() {
    try {
      const res =
        await fetch(
          "/api/admin-delivery-heatmap"
        );

      const result =
        await res.json();

      setData(result);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
    } finally {

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function DeliveryLivePage() {
  const [riders, setRiders] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  const [assignments, setAssignments] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  const [loading, setLoading] =
    useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();

    const interval =
      setInterval(
        loadData,
        5000
      );

    return () =>
      clearInterval(
        interval
      );
  }, []);

  async function loadData() {
    try {
      const ridersRes =
        await fetch(
          "/api/delivery-boys"
        );

      const ridersData =
        await ridersRes.json();

      const assignmentRes =
        await fetch(
          "/api/delivery-assignments"
        );

      const assignmentData =
        await assignmentRes.json();

      setRiders(
        ridersData || []
      );

      setAssignments(
        assignmentData || []
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  const onlineRiders =
    riders.filter(
      (r) =>
        r.status ===
        "Online"
    );

  const offlineRiders =
    riders.filter(
      (r) =>
        r.status !==
        "Online"
    );

  const assignedOrders =
    assignments.filter(
      (a) =>
        a.status ===
        "Assigned"
    );

  const acceptedOrders =
    assignments.filter(
      (a) =>
        a.status ===
        "Accepted"
    );

  const deliveredOrders =
    assignments.filter(
      (a) =>
        a.status ===
        "Delivered"
    );

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="p-10">
        Loading...
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-5">

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto">

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6 mb-6">

          <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold">
            Live Delivery Control
          </h1>

          <p className="text-gray-500 mt-2">
            Real Time Rider Monitoring
          </p>

        </div>

        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-5 gap-4 mb-6">

          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-5">

            <p>
              Total Riders
            </p>

            <h2 className="text-4xl font-bold">
              {riders.length}
            </h2>

          </div>

          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-5">

            <p>
              Online
            </p>

            <h2 className="text-4xl font-bold text-green-600">
              {
                onlineRiders.length
              }
            </h2>

          </div>

          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-5">

            <p>
              Assigned
            </p>

            <h2 className="text-4xl font-bold text-orange-600">
              {
                assignedOrders.length
              }
            </h2>

          </div>

          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-5">

            <p>
              Accepted
            </p>

            <h2 className="text-4xl font-bold text-blue-600">
              {
                acceptedOrders.length
              }
            </h2>

          </div>

          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-5">

            <p>
              Delivered
            </p>

            <h2 className="text-4xl font-bold text-purple-600">
              {
                deliveredOrders.length

            </h2>

        </div>

              Online Riders
            </h2>

            <div className="space-y-4">

              {onlineRiders.map(
                (rider) => (
                  <div
                    key={
                      rider.id
                    }
                    className="border rounded-2xl p-4"
                  >
                    <div className="flex justify-between">

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function DeliveryManagementPage() {
  const [orders, setOrders] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadOrders();
  }, []);

  function loadOrders() {
    const data = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem(
        "freshcut-orders"
      ) || "[]"
    );

    setOrders(data);
  }

  function assignRider(
    id: number,
    rider: string
  ) {
    const updated =
      orders.map((order) =>
        order.id === id
          ? {
              ...order,
              rider,
            }
          : order
      );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-orders",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setOrders(updated);
  }

  function updateStatus(
    id: number,
    status: string
  ) {
    const updated =
      orders.map((order) =>
        order.id === id
          ? {
              ...order,
              status,
            }
          : order
      );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-orders",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setOrders(updated);
  }

  const pendingDeliveries =
    orders.filter(
      (o) =>
        o.status !==
        "Delivered"
    ).length;

  const deliveredOrders =
    orders.filter(
      (o) =>
        o.status ===
        "Delivered"
    ).length;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto">

        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-8">
          Delivery Management
        </h1>

        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-6 mb-8">

          <div className="bg-yellow-50 rounded-3xl p-6">

            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold">
              Pending Deliveries
            </h2>

            <div className="text-5xl font-bold text-yellow-600 mt-3">
              {pendingDeliveries}
            </div>

          </div>

          <div className="bg-green-50 rounded-3xl p-6">

            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold">
              Delivered Orders
            </h2>

            <div className="text-5xl font-bold text-green-600 mt-3">
              {deliveredOrders}
            </div>

          </div>

        </div>

        <div className="space-y-5">

          {orders.map(
            (order) => (

              <div
                key={order.id}
                className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6"
              >

                <div className="flex justify-between">

                  <div>

                    <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">
                      FC-{order.id}
                    </h2>

                    <p>
                      {order.customer}
                    </p>

                    <p>
                      {order.phone}
                    </p>

                    <p>
                      {order.address}
                    </p>

                  </div>

                  <div className="text-right">

                    <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-green-600">
                      Ã¢â€šÂ¹{order.total}
                    </p>

                    <p>
                      {order.status}
                    </p>

                  </div>

                </div>

                <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mt-5">

                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={
                      order.rider || ""
                    }
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      assignRider(
                        order.id,
                        e.target.value
                      )
                    }
                    placeholder="Delivery Rider Name"
                    className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
                  />

                  <select
                    value={
                      order.status ||
                      "Pending"
                    }
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      updateStatus(
                        order.id,
                        e.target.value
                      )
                    }
                    className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
                  >
                    <option>
                      Pending
                    </option>
                    <option>
                      Packed
                    </option>
                    <option>
                      Out For Delivery
                    </option>
                    <option>
                      Delivered
                    </option>
                  </select>

                </div>

              </div>

            )
          )}

        </div>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function LiveMapPage() {
  const [riders, setRiders] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  const [loading, setLoading] =
    useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadRiders();

    const interval =
      setInterval(
        loadRiders,
        10000
      );

    return () =>
      clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  async function loadRiders() {
    try {
      const res =
        await fetch(
          "/api/admin-delivery-riders"
        );

      const data =
        await res.json();

      setRiders(
        Array.isArray(data)
          ? data
          : []
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  function openMap(
    latitude: number,
    longitude: number
  ) {
    window.open(
      `https://www.google.com/maps?q=${latitude},${longitude}`,
      "_blank"
    );
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="p-10">
        Loading Live Riders...
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto">

        <div className="mb-8">

          <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold">
            Live Rider Map
          </h1>

          <p className="text-gray-500 mt-2">
            Real Time GPS Tracking
          </p>

        </div>

        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">

          {riders.map(
            (rider: any) => (
              <div
                key={rider.id}
                className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6"
              >

                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">

                  <div>

                    <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">
                      {rider.name}
                    </h2>

                    <p className="text-gray-500">
                      {rider.mobile}
                    </p>

                  </div>

                  <span
                    className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-sm ${
                      rider.status ===
                        "Online" ||
                      rider.status ===
                        "On Delivery"
                        ? "bg-green-100 text-green-700"
                        : "bg-red-100 text-red-700"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {rider.status}
                  </span>

                </div>

                <div className="mt-5 space-y-2">

                  <p>
                    ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã¢â‚¬Å“Ã‚Â Latitude:
                    {" "}
                    {rider.latitude ||
                      "-"}
                  </p>

                  <p>
                    ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã¢â‚¬Å“Ã‚Â Longitude:
                    {" "}
                    {rider.longitude ||
                      "-"}
                  </p>

                  <p>
                    ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã…Â¡Ã‚Â´ Speed:
                    {" "}
                    {rider.speed ||
                      0}
                    {" "}km/h
                  </p>

                  <p>
                    ÃƒÂ¢Ã‚Â­Ã‚Â Rating:
                    {" "}
                    {rider.rating}
                  </p>

                  <p>
                    ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã¢â‚¬Å“Ã‚Â¦ Completed:
                    {" "}
                    {
                      rider.completedDeliveries
                    }
                  </p>

                  <p>
                    ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã¢â‚¬â„¢Ã‚Â° Earnings:
                    Ã¢â€šÂ¹
                    {rider.earnings}
                  </p>

                </div>

                <div className="mt-4 text-sm text-gray-500">

                  Last Seen:
                  {" "}
                  {rider.lastSeen
                    ? new Date(
                        rider.lastSeen
                      ).toLocaleString()
                    : "N/A"}

                </div>

                <button
                  disabled={
                    !rider.latitude ||
                    !rider.longitude
                  }
                  onClick={() =>
                    openMap(
                      rider.latitude,
                      rider.longitude
                    )
                  }
                  className="mt-5 w-full bg-green-600 text-white py-3 rounded-xl disabled:bg-gray-300"

==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\delivery-orders\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function DeliveryOrdersPage() {
  const [orders, setOrders] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  const [loading, setLoading] =
    useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadOrders();
  }, []);

  async function loadOrders() {
    try {
      const res =
        await fetch(
          "/api/admin-delivery-orders"
        );

      const data =
        await res.json();

      setOrders(
        Array.isArray(data)
          ? data
          : []
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  async function reassignOrder(
    assignmentId: number
  ) {
    try {
      const res =
        await fetch(
          "/api/delivery-auto-reassign",
          {
            method: "POST",
            headers: {
              "Content-Type":
                "application/json",
            },
            body: JSON.stringify({
              assignmentId,
            }),
          }
        );

      const data =
        await res.json();

      if (!res.ok) {
        alert(
          data.error ||
            "Reassign Failed"
        );
        return;
      }

      alert(
        "Order Reassigned Successfully"
      );

      loadOrders();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function DeliveryPerformancePage() {
  const [riders, setRiders] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  const [loading, setLoading] =
    useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, []);

  async function loadData() {
    try {
      const res =
        await fetch(
          "/api/admin-delivery-performance"
        );

      const data =
        await res.json();

      setRiders(
        Array.isArray(data)
          ? data
          : []
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  const totalRiders =
    riders.length;

  const totalDeliveries =
    riders.reduce(
      (
        sum,
        rider
      ) =>
        sum +
        rider.completed,
      0
    );

  const totalEarnings =
    riders.reduce(
      (
        sum,
        rider
      ) =>
        sum +
        rider.earnings,
      0
    );

  const activeRiders =
    riders.filter(
      (
        rider
      ) =>
        rider.status ===
        "Online" ||
        rider.status ===
        "On Delivery"
    ).length;

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="p-8">
        Loading...
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6 space-y-6">

      <div>

        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function DeliveryRatingsPage() {
  const [riders, setRiders] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  const [loading, setLoading] =
    useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadRiders();
  }, []);

  async function loadRiders() {
    try {
      setLoading(true);

      const res = await fetch(
        "/api/delivery-boys"
      );

      const data =
        await res.json();

      setRiders(data || []);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  function getPriorityScore(
    rider: any
  ) {
    const rating =
      rider.rating || 5;

    const completed =
      rider.completedDeliveries ||
      0;

    const score =
      rating * 20 +
      completed;

    return Math.round(score);
  }

  const totalRiders =
    riders.length;

  const onlineRiders =
    riders.filter(
      (r) =>
        r.status === "Online"
    ).length;

  const topRider =
    riders.length > 0
      ? [...riders].sort(
          (a, b) =>
            (b.rating || 0) -
            (a.rating || 0)
        )[0]
      : null;

  const totalDeliveries =
    riders.reduce(
      (sum, rider) =>
        sum +
        (rider.completedDeliveries ||
          0),
      0
    );

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto">

        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-8">

          <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold">
            Delivery Ratings
          </h1>

          <button
            onClick={
              loadRiders
            }
            className="bg-green-600 text-white px-5 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold"
          >
            Refresh
          </button>

        </div>

        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-4 gap-5 mb-8">

          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6">

            <p className="text-gray-500">
              Total Riders
            </p>

            <h2 className="text-4xl font-bold mt-2">
              {totalRiders}
            </h2>

          </div>

          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6">

            <p className="text-gray-500">
              Online Riders
            </p>

            <h2 className="text-4xl font-bold text-green-600 mt-2">
              {onlineRiders}
            </h2>

          </div>

          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6">

            <p className="text-gray-500">
              Deliveries
            </p>

            <h2 className="text-4xl font-bold text-blue-600 mt-2">
              {totalDeliveries}
            </h2>

          </div>

          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6">

            <p className="text-gray-500">
              Top Rider
            </p>

            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-purple-600 mt-2">
              {topRider?.name ||
                "-"}
            </h2>

          </div>

        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow overflow-hidden">

          <div className="p-6 border-b">

            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold">
              Rider Performance
            </h2>

          </div>

          {loading ? (
            <div className="p-10 text-center">
              Loading...
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">

              <table className="w-full">

                <thead>

                  <tr className="bg-gray-50">

                    <th className="p-4 text-left">
                      Rider
                    </th>

                    <th className="p-4 text-left">
                      Mobile
                    </th>

                    <th className="p-4 text-left">
                      Status
                    </th>

                    <th className="p-4 text-left">
                      Rating
                    </th>

                    <th className="p-4 text-left">
                      Deliveries
                    </th>

                    <th className="p-4 text-left">
                      Earnings
                    </th>

                    <th className="p-4 text-left">
                      Priority
                    </th>

                  </tr>

                </thead>

                <tbody>

                  {riders.map(
                    (
                      rider
                    ) => (
                      <tr
                        key={
                          rider.id
                        }
                        className="border-b"
                      >

                        <td className="p-4">

                          <div className="font-bold">
                            {
                              rider.name
                            }
                          </div>

                        </td>

                        <td className="p-4">
                          {
                            rider.mobile
                          }
                        </td>

                        <td className="p-4">

                          <span
                            className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-sm font-bold ${
                              rider.status ===
                              "Online"
                                ? "bg-green-100 text-green-700"
                                : "bg-gray-100 text-gray-700"
                            }`}
                          >
                            {
                              rider.status
                            }
                          </span>

                        </td>

                        <td className="p-4 font-bold text-orange-600">
                          ÃƒÂ¢Ã‚Â­Ã‚Â{" "}
                          {(
                            rider.rating ||
                            5
                          ).toFixed(
                            1
                          )}
                        </td>

                        <td className="p-4 font-bold text-blue-600">
                          {
                            rider.completedDeliveries
                          }
                        </td>

                        <td className="p-4 font-bold text-green-600">
                          Ã¢â€šÂ¹
                          {rider.earnings ||
                            0}
                        </td>

                        <td className="p-4">

                          <span className="bg-purple-100 text-purple-700 px-3 py-1 rounded-full font-bold">

                            {getPriorityScore(
                              rider
                            )}

                          </span>

                        </td>

                      </tr>
                    )
                  )}

                  {riders.length ===
                    0 && (
                    <tr>

                      <td
                        colSpan={
                          7
                        }
                        className="text-center p-10 text-gray-500"
                      >
                        No Riders Found
                      </td>

                    </tr>
                  )}

                </tbody>

              </table>

            </div>
          )}

        </div>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function DeliveryRidersPage() {
  const [riders, setRiders] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  const [loading, setLoading] =
    useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadRiders();

    const interval =
      setInterval(() => {
        loadRiders();
      }, 10000);

    return () =>
      clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  async function loadRiders() {
    try {
      const res =
        await fetch(
          "/api/admin-delivery-riders"
        );

      const data =
        await res.json();

      setRiders(
        Array.isArray(data)
          ? data
          : []
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  async function forceOffline(
    riderId: number
  ) {
    try {
      await fetch(
        "/api/delivery-live-status",
        {
          method: "POST",
          headers: {
            "Content-Type":
              "application/json",
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            riderId,
            status:
              "Offline",
          }),
        }
      );

      loadRiders();

      alert(
        "Rider Marked Offline"
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="p-10">
        Loading...
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6 space-y-6">

      <div>

        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">
          Delivery Riders
        </h1>

        <p className="text-gray-500">
          Rider Management Panel
        </p>

      </div>

      <div className="grid md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">

        <div className="bg-white border rounded-2xl p-5">
          <p className="text-gray-500">
            Total Riders
          </p>

          <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold mt-2">
            {riders.length}
          </h2>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white border rounded-2xl p-5">
          <p className="text-gray-500">
            Online
          </p>

          <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-green-600 mt-2">
            {
              riders.filter(
                (r) =>
                  r.status ===
                  "Online"
              ).length
            }
          </h2>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white border rounded-2xl p-5">
          <p className="text-gray-500">
            On Delivery
          </p>

          <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-blue-600 mt-2">
            {
              riders.filter(
                (r) =>
                  r.status ===
                  "On Delivery"
              ).length
            }
          </h2>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white border rounded-2xl p-5">
          <p className="text-gray-500">
            Offline
          </p>

          <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-red-600 mt-2">
            {
              riders.filter(
                (r) =>
                  r.status ===
                  "Offline"
              ).length
            }
          </h2>
        </div>

      </div>

      <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-2 gap-5">

        {riders.map(
          (rider: any) => (

            <div
              key={rider.id}
              className="bg-white border rounded-2xl p-5"
            >

              <div className="flex items-center gap-4">

                <img
                  src={
                    rider.photo ||
                    "/user.png"
                  }
                  alt=""
                  className="h-20 w-20 rounded-full border object-cover"
                />

                <div>

                  <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">
                    {rider.name}
                  </h2>

                  <p className="text-gray-500">
                    {rider.mobile}
                  </p>

                  <p className="text-gray-500">
                    {rider.vehicle}
                  </p>

                </div>

              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 mt-5">

                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
                    Status
                  </p>

                  <p className="font-semibold">
                    {rider.status}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
                    Current Order
                  </p>

                  <p className="font-semibold">
                    {rider.currentOrder ||
                      "-"}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
                    Today's Deliveries
                  </p>

                  <p className="font-semibold">
                    {
                      rider.todayDeliveries
                    }
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
                    Earnings
                  </p>

                  <p className="font-semibold">
                    Ã¢â€šÂ¹
                    {rider.earnings}
                  </p>
                </div>

              </div>

              <div className="mt-5 space-y-2">

                <p>
                  <strong>
                    Latitude:
                  </strong>{" "}
                  {rider.latitude ||
                    "-"}
                </p>

                <p>
                  <strong>
                    Longitude:
                  </strong>{" "}
                  {rider.longitude ||
                    "-"}
                </p>

                <p>
                  <strong>
                    Speed:
                  </strong>{" "}
                  {rider.speed ||
                    0} km/h
                </p>

              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-5">

                {rider.latitude &&
                  rider.longitude && (

                  <a

                    target="_blank"
                    className="bg-blue-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg"
                  >
                    Open Map

                  onClick={() =>
                    forceOffline(

                >
                  Force Offline
                </button>

              </div>

            </div>

          )
        )}

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

import {

export default function DeliverySLAPage() {

    useState<any>(null);

  const [loading, setLoading] =
    useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, []);

  async function loadData() {
    try {
      const res =
        await fetch(
          "/api/admin-delivery-sla"
        );

      const result =
        await res.json();

      setData(result);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="p-6">
        Loading SLA Report...
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6 space-y-6">

      <div>

        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold">
          Delivery SLA Dashboard
        </h1>

        <p className="text-gray-500">
          Delivery Performance Monitoring
        </p>

      </div>

      <div className="grid md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">

        <div className="bg-white border rounded-2xl p-6">

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function DeliveryTrackingPage() {
  const [riders, setRiders] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadRiders();

    const interval = setInterval(loadRiders, 10000);
    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  async function loadRiders() {
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/admin-live-riders", {
        cache: "no-store",
      });

      const data = await res.json();

      setRiders(
        Array.isArray(data)
          ? data
          : Array.isArray(data?.riders)
          ? data.riders
          : []
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  const filtered = riders.filter((rider) =>
    `${rider.name || ""} ${rider.mobile || ""} ${rider.vehicle || ""}`
      .toLowerCase()
      .includes(search.toLowerCase())
  );

  const online = riders.filter((r) => r.status === "Online").length;
  const delivery = riders.filter((r) => r.status === "On Delivery").length;
  const offline = riders.filter((r) => r.status === "Offline").length;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f8] p-4 md:p-6">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1500px] space-y-6">

        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#159447]">
              Delivery Operations
            </p>
            <h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-bold text-[#123b39]">
              Live Delivery Tracking
            </h1>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
              Real-time rider location and delivery monitoring.
            </p>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={loadRiders}
            className="rounded-xl bg-[#063f3d] px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-[#0a504c]"
          >
            â†» Refresh
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 lg:grid-cols-4">
          <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-5 shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase text-gray-400">Total Riders</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-[#123b39]">{riders.length}</p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-5 shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase text-gray-400">Online</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-green-600">{online}</p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-5 shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase text-gray-400">On Delivery</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-blue-600">{delivery}</p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-5 shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase text-gray-400">Offline</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-red-500">{offline}</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-5 shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
          <div className="mb-5 flex flex-col gap-3 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-[#123b39]">
                Live Rider Locations
              </h2>
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
                Automatically refreshed every 10 seconds.
              </p>
            </div>

            <input
              value={search}
              onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Search rider..."
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#159447] md:w-80"
            />
          </div>

          {loading ? (
            <div className="rounded-xl bg-gray-50 p-12 text-center text-gray-500">
              Loading live riders...
            </div>
          ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="rounded-xl bg-gray-50 p-12 text-center text-gray-500">
              No Riders Found
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
              {filtered.map((rider) => (
                <div
                  key={rider.id}
                  className="rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 bg-[#fbfcfc] p-5 hover:shadow-md"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <h3 className="font-bold text-[#123b39]">
                        {rider.name}
                      </h3>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
                        {rider.mobile || "â€”"}
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
                        {rider.vehicle || "â€”"}
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    <span
                      className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold ${
                        rider.status === "Online"
                          ? "bg-green-100 text-green-700"
                          : rider.status === "On Delivery"
                          ? "bg-blue-100 text-blue-700"
                          : "bg-gray-100 text-gray-600"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {rider.status || "Offline"}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <div className="mt-5 grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                    <div className="rounded-xl bg-white p-3">
                      <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">Latitude</p>
                      <p className="mt-1 truncate text-sm font-bold text-gray-700">
                        {rider.latitude ?? "â€”"}
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    <div className="rounded-xl bg-white p-3">
                      <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">Longitude</p>
                      <p className="mt-1 truncate text-sm font-bold text-gray-700">
                        {rider.longitude ?? "â€”"}
                      </p>
                    </div>

                    <div className="rounded-xl bg-white p-3">
                      <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">Speed</p>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-sm font-bold text-gray-700">
                        {rider.speed || 0} km/h
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {rider.latitude && rider.longitude && (
                    <a
                      href={`https://www.google.com/maps?q=${rider.latitude},${rider.longitude}`}
                      target="_blank"
                      rel="noreferrer"
                      className="mt-4 block rounded-xl bg-[#159447] py-3 text-center text-sm font-bold text-white hover:bg-[#117b3b]"
                    >
                      Open Rider Location
                    </a>
                  )}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}







==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\delivery-zones\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

interface DeliveryZone {
  id: number;
  zoneName: string;
  sectors: string;
  charge: number;
  createdAt: string;
}

export default function DeliveryZonesPage() {
  const [zones, setZones] =
    useState<DeliveryZone[]>([]);

  const [loading, setLoading] =
    useState(false);

  const [zoneName, setZoneName] =
    useState("");

  const [sectors, setSectors] =
    useState("");

  const [charge, setCharge] =
    useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadZones();
  }, []);

  async function loadZones() {
    try {
      setLoading(true);

      const res = await fetch(
        "/api/delivery-zones"
      );

      const data =
        await res.json();

      setZones(data);
    } catch (error) {
      alert(
        "Failed To Load Zones"
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  async function addZone() {
    if (
      !zoneName ||
      !sectors ||
      !charge
    ) {
      alert("Fill All Fields");
      return;
    }

    await fetch(
      "/api/delivery-zones",
      {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type":
            "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          zoneName,
          sectors,
          charge,
        }),
      }
    );

    setZoneName("");
    setSectors("");
    setCharge("");

    loadZones();
  }

  async function editZone(
    zone: DeliveryZone
  ) {
    const newName =
      prompt(
        "Zone Name",
        zone.zoneName
      );

    if (!newName) return;

    const newSectors =
      prompt(
        "Sectors",
        zone.sectors
      );

    if (!newSectors) return;

    const newCharge =
      prompt(
        "Charge",
        String(zone.charge)
      );

    if (!newCharge) return;

    await fetch(
      "/api/delivery-zones",
      {
        method: "PUT",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type":
            "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          id: zone.id,
          zoneName:
            newName,
          sectors:
            newSectors,
          charge:
            Number(
              newCharge
            ),
        }),
      }
    );

    loadZones();
  }

  async function deleteZone(
    id: number
  ) {
    const ok = confirm(
      "Delete This Zone?"
    );

    if (!ok) return;

    await fetch(
      "/api/delivery-zones",
      {
        method:
          "DELETE",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type":
            "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          id,
        }),
      }
    );

    loadZones();
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto">

        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-8">
          Delivery Zones
        </h1>

        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow mb-8">

          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">
            Add Zone
          </h2>

          <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">

            <input
              value={zoneName}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setZoneName(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              placeholder="Zone Name"
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

            <input
              value={sectors}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setSectors(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              placeholder="Sectors"
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

            <input
              type="number"
              value={charge}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setCharge(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              placeholder="Charge"
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

          </div>

          <button
            onClick={addZone}
            className="mt-5 bg-green-600 text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-xl"
          >
            Add Zone
          </button>

        </div>

        {loading ? (
          <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-3xl">
            Loading...
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">

            {zones.map(
              (zone) => (
                <div
                  key={zone.id}
                  className="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow"
                >
                  <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold">
                    {
                      zone.zoneName
                    }
                  </h2>

                  <p className="mt-3">
                    {
                      zone.sectors
                    }
                  </p>

                  <p className="mt-3 text-green-600 font-bold text-xl">
                    Ã¢â€šÂ¹
                    {
                      zone.charge
                    }
                  </p>

                  <div className="flex gap-2 mt-5">

                    <button
                      onClick={() =>
                        editZone(
                          zone
                        )
                      }
                      className="flex-1 bg-blue-600 text-white py-2 rounded-xl"
                    >
                      Edit
                    </button>

                    <button
                      onClick={() =>
                        deleteZone(
                          zone.id
                        )
                      }
                      className="flex-1 bg-red-600 text-white py-2 rounded-xl"
                    >
                      Delete
                    </button>

                  </div>

                </div>
              )
            )}

          </div>
        )}

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import {
  useEffect,
  useState,
} from "react";

export default function DispatchCenterPage() {
  const [orders,
    setOrders] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  const [loading,
    setLoading] =
    useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadOrders();
  }, []);

  async function loadOrders() {
    try {
      const res =
        await fetch(
          "/api/admin-order-board"
        );

      const data =
        await res.json();

      setOrders(
        Array.isArray(data)
          ? data
          : []
      );
    } catch (
      error
    ) {
      console.error(
        error
      );
    }
  }

  async function dispatchOrder(
    order: any
  ) {
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const res =
        await fetch(
          "/api/admin-auto-dispatch",
          {
            method:
              "POST",
            headers: {
              "Content-Type":
                "application/json",
            },
            body: JSON.stringify(
              {
                orderId:
                  order.id,

                latitude:
                  28.6139,

                longitude:
                  77.209,
              }
            ),
          }
        );

      const data =
        await res.json();

      if (!res.ok) {
        alert(
          data.error
        );
        return;
      }

      alert(
        `Assigned To ${data.rider.name}
Distance : ${data.distance} KM`
      );

      loadOrders();
    } catch (
      error
    ) {
      console.error(
        error
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(
        false
      );
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6">

      <div className="mb-6">

        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">
          Smart Dispatch Center
        </h1>

        <p className="text-gray-500">
          Nearest Rider Auto Assignment
        </p>

      </div>

      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border overflow-hidden">

        <table className="w-full">

          <thead className="bg-gray-50">

            <tr>

              <th className="p-4 text-left">
                Order
              </th>

              <th className="p-4 text-left">
                Customer
              </th>

              <th className="p-4 text-left">
                Amount
              </th>

              <th className="p-4 text-left">
                Status
              </th>

              <th className="p-4 text-left">
                Action
              </th>

            </tr>

          </thead>

          <tbody>

            {orders.map(
              (
                order
              ) => (
                <tr
                  key={
                    order.id
                  }
                  className="border-t"
                >

                  <td className="p-4">
                    #
                    {
                      order.id
                    }
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">
                    {
                      order.customer
                    }
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">
                    Ã¢â€šÂ¹
                    {
                      order.amount
                    }
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">
                    {
                      order.status
                    }
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">

                    <button
                      disabled={
                        loading
                      }
                      onClick={() =>
                        dispatchOrder(
                          order
                        )
                      }
                      className="bg-green-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg"
                    >
                      Auto Dispatch
                    </button>

                  </td>

                </tr>
              )
            )}

          </tbody>

        </table>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function EmployeesPage() {
  const [employees, setEmployees] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  const [form, setForm] = useState({
    name: "",
    phone: "",
    role: "",
    salary: "",
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    loadEmployees();
  }, []);

  function loadEmployees() {
    const data = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem(
        "freshcut-employees"
      ) || "[]"
    );

    setEmployees(data);
  }

  function addEmployee() {
    if (
      !form.name ||
      !form.phone
    ) {
      alert("Fill Required Fields");
      return;
    }

    const employee = {
      id: Date.now(),
      ...form,
      attendance: "Present",
      salaryPaid: false,
      joiningDate:
        new Date().toLocaleDateString(),
    };

    const updated = [
      employee,
      ...employees,
    ];

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-employees",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setForm({
      name: "",
      phone: "",
      role: "",
      salary: "",
    });

    loadEmployees();
  }

  function toggleAttendance(
    id: number
  ) {
    const updated =
      employees.map((emp) =>
        emp.id === id
          ? {
              ...emp,
              attendance:
                emp.attendance ===
                "Present"
                  ? "Absent"
                  : "Present",
            }
          : emp
      );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-employees",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setEmployees(updated);
  }

  function markSalaryPaid(
    id: number
  ) {
    const updated =
      employees.map((emp) =>
        emp.id === id
          ? {
              ...emp,
              salaryPaid: true,
            }
          : emp
      );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-employees",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setEmployees(updated);
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto">

        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-8">
          Employee Management
        </h1>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6 mb-8">

          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">
            Add Employee
          </h2>

          <div className="grid md:grid-cols-4 gap-4">

            <input
              placeholder="Employee Name"
              value={form.name}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setForm({
                  ...form,
                  name:
                    e.target.value,
                })
              }
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

            <input
              placeholder="Phone Number"
              value={form.phone}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setForm({
                  ...form,
                  phone:
                    e.target.value,
                })
              }
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

            <input
              placeholder="Role"
              value={form.role}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setForm({
                  ...form,
                  role:
                    e.target.value,
                })
              }
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

            <input
              placeholder="Monthly Salary"
              value={form.salary}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setForm({
                  ...form,
                  salary:
                    e.target.value,
                })
              }
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

          </div>

          <button
            onClick={addEmployee}
            className="mt-6 bg-green-600 text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-xl"
          >
            Add Employee
          </button>

        </div>

        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">

          {employees.map(
            (employee) => (

              <div
                key={employee.id}
                className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6"
              >

                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold">
                  {employee.name}
                </h2>

                <p>
                  ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã¢â‚¬Å“Ã…Â¾ {employee.phone}
                </p>

                <p>
