==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\expenses\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function ExpensesPage() {
  const [expenses, setExpenses] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadExpenses();
  }, []);

  function loadExpenses() {
    try {
      setExpenses(
        JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("freshcut-expenses") || "[]")
      );
    } catch {
      setExpenses([]);
    }
  }

  function addExpense() {
    if (!title || !amount) {
      alert("Fill All Fields");
      return;
    }

    const updated = [
      {
        id: Date.now(),
        title,
        amount: Number(amount),
        date: new Date().toLocaleDateString(),
      },
      ...expenses,
    ];

    localStorage.setItem("freshcut-expenses", JSON.stringify(updated));
    setExpenses(updated);
    setTitle("");
    setAmount("");
  }

  function deleteExpense(id: number) {
    const updated = expenses.filter((item) => item.id !== id);
    localStorage.setItem("freshcut-expenses", JSON.stringify(updated));
    setExpenses(updated);
  }

  const totalExpense = expenses.reduce(
    (sum, item) => sum + Number(item.amount || 0),
    0
  );

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1600px]">
        <div className="mb-7">
          <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-emerald-700">
            Fresh2Cook Admin
          </p>
          <h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38] md:text-4xl">
            Expense Management
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
            Track operational expenses and business spending.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-7 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
            Add Expense
          </h2>

          <div className="mt-5 grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
            <input
              value={title}
              onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Expense Title"
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
            />

            <input
              value={amount}
              onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Amount"
              type="number"
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
            />

            <button
              onClick={addExpense}
              className="rounded-xl bg-[#063f3d] px-6 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white hover:bg-[#0a514d]"
            >
              + Add Expense
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-7 rounded-2xl border border-red-100 bg-red-50 p-6">
          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-red-700">Total Expenses</p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-4xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
            â‚¹{totalExpense.toLocaleString("en-IN")}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
          <div className="mb-5">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
              Expense History
            </h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
              Recent business expenses.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-3">
            {expenses.length === 0 && (
              <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 p-10 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
                No Expenses Found
              </div>
            )}

            {expenses.map((item) => (
              <div
                key={item.id}
                className="flex flex-col gap-4 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 bg-slate-50 p-4 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between"
              >
                <div>
                  <p className="font-bold text-[#123b38]">{item.title}</p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">{item.date}</p>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-5 md:justify-end">
                  <span className="font-extrabold text-red-700">
                    â‚¹{Number(item.amount || 0).toLocaleString("en-IN")}
                  </span>

                  <button
                    onClick={() => deleteExpense(item.id)}
                    className="rounded-lg bg-red-50 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-red-700 hover:bg-red-100"
                  >
                    Delete
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}







==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\feedback\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";

type Feedback = {
  id: number;
  customer: string;
  phone: string;
  rating: number;
  comment: string;
  createdAt: string;
};

export default function AdminFeedbackPage() {
  const [feedback, setFeedback] =
    useState<Feedback[]>([]);

  const [loading, setLoading] =
    useState(true);

  const [search, setSearch] =
    useState("");

  const [ratingFilter, setRatingFilter] =
    useState("All");

  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] =
    useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadFeedback();
  }, []);

  async function loadFeedback() {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setErrorMessage("");

      const response =
        await fetch("/api/feedback", {
          cache: "no-store",
        });

      const data =
        await response.json();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          data?.error ||
            "Failed to load feedback"
        );
      }

      setFeedback(
        Array.isArray(data)
          ? data
          : []
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(
        "LOAD FEEDBACK ERROR:",
        error
      );

      setErrorMessage(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "Failed to load feedback"
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  const filteredFeedback =
    useMemo(() => {
      const query =
        search
          .trim()
          .toLowerCase();

      return feedback.filter(
        (item) => {
          const matchesSearch =
            !query ||
            item.customer
              .toLowerCase()
              .includes(query) ||
            item.phone
              .toLowerCase()
              .includes(query) ||
            item.comment
              .toLowerCase()
              .includes(query);

          const matchesRating =
            ratingFilter === "All" ||
            item.rating ===
              Number(ratingFilter);

          return (
            matchesSearch &&
            matchesRating
          );
        }
      );
    }, [
      feedback,
      search,
      ratingFilter,
    ]);

  const averageRating =
    feedback.length > 0
      ? (
          feedback.reduce(
            (sum, item) =>
              sum +
              Number(
                item.rating || 0
              ),
            0
          ) / feedback.length
        ).toFixed(1)
      : "0.0";

  const fiveStarCount =
    feedback.filter(
      (item) =>
        item.rating === 5
    ).length;

  const fourStarCount =
    feedback.filter(
      (item) =>
        item.rating === 4
    ).length;

  function openWhatsApp(
    item: Feedback
  ) {
    const number =
      item.phone.replace(
        /\D/g,
        ""
      );

    if (!number) {
      alert(
        "Customer mobile number is not available."
      );
      return;
    }

    const whatsappNumber =
      number.length === 10
        ? `91${number}`
        : number;

    const message = [
      `Hello ${item.customer},`,
      "",
      "Thank you for your valuable feedback for Fresh2Cook! ÃƒÂ¢Ã‚ÂÃ‚Â¤ÃƒÂ¯Ã‚Â¸Ã‚Â",
      "",
      `Your Rating: ${item.rating}/5 ÃƒÂ¢Ã‚Â­Ã‚Â`,
      `Your Review: ${item.comment}`,
      "",
      "We truly appreciate your support.",
    ].join("\n");

    window.open(
      `https://wa.me/${whatsappNumber}?text=${encodeURIComponent(
        message
      )}`,
      "_blank",
      "noopener,noreferrer"
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto">

        {/* HEADER */}

        <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between gap-4 mb-8">

          <div>

            <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold">
              Customer Feedback & Reviews
            </h1>

            <p className="text-gray-500 mt-1">
              View customer ratings and reviews for Fresh2Cook.
            </p>

          </div>

          <button
            onClick={loadFeedback}
            className="bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700 text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold"
          >
            Refresh Reviews
          </button>

        </div>

        {/* ERROR */}

        {errorMessage && (
          <div className="mb-6 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 rounded-2xl p-4 font-semibold">
            {errorMessage}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* STATS */}

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 md:grid-cols-4 gap-4 mb-6">

          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow p-5">

            <div className="text-sm text-gray-500">
              Total Reviews
            </div>

            <div className="text-3xl font-extrabold mt-1">
              {feedback.length}
            </div>

          </div>

          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow p-5">

            <div className="text-sm text-gray-500">
              Average Rating
            </div>

            <div className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-yellow-500 mt-1">
              {averageRating} ÃƒÂ¢Ã‚Â­Ã‚Â
            </div>

          </div>

          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow p-5">

            <div className="text-sm text-gray-500">
              5 Star Reviews
            </div>

            <div className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-green-600 mt-1">
              {fiveStarCount}
            </div>

          </div>

          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow p-5">

            <div className="text-sm text-gray-500">
              4 Star Reviews
            </div>

            <div className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-blue-600 mt-1">
              {fourStarCount}
            </div>

          </div>

        </div>

        {/* FILTERS */}

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-5 mb-6">

          <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-4">

            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Search customer, mobile or review..."
              value={search}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setSearch(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              className="flex-1 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 outline-none focus:border-green-500"
            />

            <select
              value={ratingFilter}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setRatingFilter(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              className="border border-gray-200 rounded-xl p-4 outline-none focus:border-green-500"
            >

              <option value="All">
                All Ratings
              </option>

              <option value="5">
                5 Stars
              </option>

              <option value="4">
                4 Stars
              </option>

              <option value="3">
                3 Stars
              </option>

              <option value="2">
                2 Stars
              </option>

              <option value="1">
                1 Star
              </option>

            </select>

          </div>

        </div>

        {/* REVIEWS */}

        {loading ? (
          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-10 text-center">
            Loading Reviews...
          </div>
        ) : filteredFeedback.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-12 text-center">

            <div className="text-6xl mb-4">
              ÃƒÂ¢Ã‚Â­Ã‚Â
            </div>

            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold">
              No Reviews Found
            </h2>

            <p className="text-gray-500 mt-2">
              Customer feedback will appear here.
            </p>

          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-5">

            {filteredFeedback.map(
              (item) => (
                <div
                  key={item.id}
                  className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6"
                >

                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">

                    <div>

                      <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">
                        {item.customer}
                      </h2>

                      <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mt-1">
                        ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã¢â‚¬Å“Ã‚Â± {item.phone}
                      </p>

                    </div>

                    <div className="text-right">

                      <div className="text-yellow-500 text-xl">
                        {"ÃƒÂ¢Ã‹Å“Ã¢â‚¬Â¦".repeat(
                          Math.max(
                            0,
                            Math.min(
                              5,
                              Number(
                                item.rating
                              )
                            )
                          )
                        )}
                      </div>

                      <div className="text-xs text-gray-400">
                        {new Date(
                          item.createdAt
                        ).toLocaleString(
                          "en-IN"
                        )}
                      </div>

                    </div>

                  </div>

                  <div className="bg-gray-50 rounded-2xl p-5 mt-5">

                    <p className="text-gray-700 leading-7">
                      ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã…â€œ{item.comment}ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚Â
                    </p>

                  </div>

                  <button
                    onClick={() =>
                      openWhatsApp(item)
                    }
                    className="w-full mt-5 bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700 text-white py-3 rounded-xl font-bold"
                  >
                    WhatsApp Customer
                  </button>

                </div>
              )
            )}

          </div>
        )}

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function FinanceGSTPage() {
  const [stats, setStats] =
    useState({
      revenue: 0,
      expenses: 0,
      profit: 0,
      gst: 0,
      orders: 0,
    });

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, []);

  function loadData() {
    const orders = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem(
        "freshcut-orders"
      ) || "[]"
    );

    const expenses = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem(
        "freshcut-expenses"
      ) || "[]"
    );

    const revenue =
      orders.reduce(
        (
          sum: number,
          item: any
        ) =>
          sum +
          Number(
            item.total || 0
          ),
        0
      );

    const totalExpense =
      expenses.reduce(
        (
          sum: number,
          item: any
        ) =>
          sum +
          Number(
            item.amount || 0
          ),
        0
      );

    const gst =
      revenue * 0.05;

    const profit =
      revenue -
      totalExpense -
      gst;

    setStats({
      revenue,
      expenses:
        totalExpense,
      profit,
      gst,
      orders:
        orders.length,
    });
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto">

        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-8">
          Finance & GST Dashboard
        </h1>

        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-5 gap-4 mb-8">

          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow">
            <p>Revenue</p>
            <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-green-600">
              Ã¢â€šÂ¹{stats.revenue}
            </h2>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow">
            <p>Expenses</p>
            <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-red-600">
              Ã¢â€šÂ¹{stats.expenses}
            </h2>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow">
            <p>GST</p>
            <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-orange-600">
              Ã¢â€šÂ¹{stats.gst}
            </h2>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow">
            <p>Profit</p>
            <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-blue-600">
              Ã¢â€šÂ¹{stats.profit}
            </h2>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow">
            <p>Orders</p>
            <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold">
              {stats.orders}
            </h2>
          </div>

        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6">

          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">
            GST Summary
          </h2>

          <div className="space-y-3">

            <div className="flex justify-between border-b pb-3">
              <span>
                Taxable Revenue
              </span>
              <span>
                Ã¢â€šÂ¹{stats.revenue}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="flex justify-between border-b pb-3">
              <span>
                GST @ 5%
              </span>
              <span>
                Ã¢â€šÂ¹{stats.gst}
              </span>
            </div>

            <div className="flex justify-between font-bold text-xl">
              <span>
                Net Profit
              </span>
              <span>
                Ã¢â€šÂ¹{stats.profit}
              </span>
            </div>

          </div>

        </div>

      </div>

==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\franchise\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function FranchisePage() {
  const [franchises, setFranchises] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  const [name, setName] =
    useState("");

  const [city, setCity] =
    useState("");

  const [phone, setPhone] =
    useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, []);

  function loadData() {
    const data = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem(
        "freshcut-franchises"
      ) || "[]"
    );

    setFranchises(data);
  }

  function addFranchise() {
    if (
      !name ||
      !city ||
      !phone
    ) {
      alert(
        "Fill All Fields"
      );
      return;
    }

    const updated = [
      {
        id: Date.now(),
        name,
        city,
        phone,
        status: "Active",
        createdAt:
          new Date().toLocaleDateString(),
      },
      ...franchises,
    ];

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-franchises",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setName("");
    setCity("");
    setPhone("");

    loadData();
  }

  function removeFranchise(
    id: number
  ) {
    const updated =
      franchises.filter(
        (item) =>
          item.id !== id
      );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-franchises",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    loadData();
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto">

        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-8">
          Franchise Management
        </h1>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6 mb-8">

          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">
            Add Franchise
          </h2>

          <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">

            <input
              value={name}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setName(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              placeholder="Franchise Owner"
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

            <input
              value={city}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setCity(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              placeholder="City"
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

            <input
              value={phone}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setPhone(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              placeholder="Mobile Number"
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

          </div>

          <button
            onClick={
              addFranchise
            }
            className="mt-5 bg-green-600 text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold"
          >
            Add Franchise
          </button>

        </div>

        <div className="space-y-4">

          {franchises.map(
            (item: any) => (
              <div
                key={item.id}
                className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6"
              >

                <div className="flex justify-between items-center">

                  <div>

                    <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">
                      {item.name}
                    </h2>

                    <p>
                      ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã¢â‚¬Å“Ã‚Â {item.city}
                    </p>

                    <p>
                      ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã¢â‚¬Å“Ã…Â¾ {item.phone}
                    </p>

                    <p className="text-green-600 font-bold mt-2">
                      {item.status}
                    </p>

                  </div>

                  <button
                    onClick={() =>
                      removeFranchise(
                        item.id
                      )
                    }
                    className="bg-red-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl"
                  >
                    Delete
                  </button>

                </div>

              </div>
            )
          )}

        </div>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function FranchiseLeadsPage() {
  const [leads, setLeads] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  const [name, setName] =
    useState("");

  const [city, setCity] =
    useState("");

  const [phone, setPhone] =
    useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadLeads();
  }, []);

  function loadLeads() {
    const data = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem(
        "freshcut-franchise-leads"
      ) || "[]"
    );

    setLeads(data);
  }

  function addLead() {
    if (
      !name ||
      !city ||
      !phone
    ) {
      alert(
        "Fill All Fields"
      );
      return;
    }

    const updated = [
      {
        id: Date.now(),
        name,
        city,
        phone,
        status: "New Lead",
        createdAt:
          new Date().toLocaleDateString(),
      },
      ...leads,
    ];

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-franchise-leads",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setName("");
    setCity("");
    setPhone("");

    loadLeads();
  }

  function updateStatus(
    id: number,
    status: string
  ) {
    const updated =
      leads.map(
        (lead: any) =>
          lead.id === id
            ? {
                ...lead,
                status,
              }
            : lead
      );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-franchise-leads",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    loadLeads();
  }

  function deleteLead(
    id: number
  ) {
    const updated =
      leads.filter(
        (lead: any) =>
          lead.id !== id
      );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-franchise-leads",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    loadLeads();
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto">

        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-8">
          Franchise Leads
        </h1>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6 mb-8">

          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">
            Add New Lead
          </h2>

          <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">

            <input
              value={name}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setName(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              placeholder="Lead Name"
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

            <input
              value={city}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setCity(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              placeholder="City"
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

            <input
              value={phone}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setPhone(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              placeholder="Phone Number"
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

          </div>

          <button
            onClick={addLead}
            className="mt-5 bg-green-600 text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold"
          >
            Save Lead
          </button>

        </div>

        <div className="space-y-4">

          {leads.map(
            (lead: any) => (
              <div
                key={lead.id}
                className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6"
              >

                <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:justify-between md:items-center gap-4">

                  <div>

                    <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">
                      {lead.name}
                    </h2>

                    <p>
                      ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã¢â‚¬Å“Ã‚Â {lead.city}
                    </p>

                    <p>
                      ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã¢â‚¬Å“Ã…Â¾ {lead.phone}
                    </p>

                    <p className="mt-2 font-bold text-blue-600">
                      {lead.status}
                    </p>

                  </div>

                  <div className="flex gap-2 flex-wrap">

                    <button
                      onClick={() =>
                        updateStatus(
                          lead.id,
                          "Contacted"
                        )
                      }
                      className="bg-blue-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl"
                    >
                      Contacted
                    </button>

                    <button
                      onClick={() =>
                        updateStatus(
                          lead.id,
                          "Qualified"
                        )
                      }
                      className="bg-green-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl"
                    >
                      Qualified
                    </button>

                    <button
                      onClick={() =>
                        updateStatus(
                          lead.id,
                          "Converted"
                        )
                      }
                      className="bg-purple-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl"
                    >
                      Converted
                    </button>

                    <button
                      onClick={() =>
                        deleteLead(
                          lead.id
                        )
                      }
                      className="bg-red-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl"
                    >
                      Delete
                    </button>

                  </div>

                </div>

              </div>
            )
          )}

        </div>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function FranchiseMasterPage() {
  const [franchises, setFranchises] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, []);

  function loadData() {
    const data = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem(
        "freshcut-franchises"
      ) || "[]"
    );

    setFranchises(data);
  }

  const totalRevenue =
    franchises.reduce(
      (sum, item) =>
        sum +
        Number(
          item.revenue || 0
        ),
      0
    );

  const totalStores =
    franchises.length;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto">

        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-8">
          Franchise Master Dashboard
        </h1>

        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-4 gap-4 mb-8">

          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow">
            <p>Total Franchise</p>
            <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-blue-600">
              {totalStores}
            </h2>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow">
            <p>Total Revenue</p>
            <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-green-600">
              Ã¢â€šÂ¹{totalRevenue}
            </h2>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow">
            <p>Growth Rate</p>
            <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-purple-600">
              18%
            </h2>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow">
            <p>Active Stores</p>
            <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-orange-600">
              {totalStores}
            </h2>
          </div>

        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6">

          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">
            Franchise Network
          </h2>

          <div className="space-y-4">

            {franchises.map(
              (item, index) => (
                <div
                  key={index}
                  className="border rounded-xl p-4 flex justify-between"
                >
                  <div>
                    <h3 className="font-bold">
                      {item.name}
                    </h3>

                    <p>
                      {item.city}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <div className="text-right">
                    <p className="font-bold text-green-600">
                      Ã¢â€šÂ¹{item.revenue}
                    </p>

                    <p>
                      {item.orders} Orders
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )
            )}

            {franchises.length === 0 && (
              <div className="text-center text-gray-500 py-10">
                No Franchise Data Found
              </div>
            )}

          </div>

        </div>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\import\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useState } from "react";

export default function ImportProductsPage() {
  const [csvData, setCsvData] =
    useState("");

  function importProducts() {
    if (!csvData) {
      alert("Paste CSV Data");
      return;
    }

    const rows =
      csvData.trim().split("\n");

    const products =
      JSON.parse(
        localStorage.getItem(
          "freshcut-products"
        ) || "[]"
      );

    rows.forEach((row) => {
      const cols = row.split(",");

      if (cols.length >= 5) {
        products.push({
          id: Date.now() + Math.random(),
          name: cols[0],
          price: Number(cols[1]),
          stock: cols[2],
          pack: cols[3],
          image: cols[4],
        });
      }
    });

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-products",
      JSON.stringify(products)
    );

    alert(
      "Products Imported Successfully"
    );

    setCsvData("");
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">

      <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto">

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-xl p-8">

          <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-6">
            Bulk Product Import
          </h1>

          <div className="bg-yellow-50 border rounded-xl p-4 mb-6">

            <p className="font-bold">
              CSV Format
            </p>

            <p>
              Name,Price,Stock,Pack,ImageURL
            </p>

            <p>
              Bhindi,49,100,500gm,https://image.jpg
            </p>

          </div>

          <textarea
            value={csvData}
            onChange={(e) =>
              setCsvData(
                e.target.value
              )
            }
            rows={15}
            className="w-full border rounded-xl p-4"
            placeholder="Paste CSV Here..."
          />

          <button
            onClick={importProducts}
            className="mt-6 bg-green-600 text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold"
          >
            Import Products
          </button>

        </div>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}









==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\inventory\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function InventoryPage() {
  const [items, setItems] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  const [name, setName] =
    useState("");

  const [stock, setStock] =
    useState("");

  const [unit, setUnit] =
    useState("Kg");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadInventory();
  }, []);

  function loadInventory() {
    const data = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem(
        "freshcut-inventory"
      ) || "[]"
    );

    setItems(data);
  }

  function addItem() {
    if (!name || !stock) {
      alert(
        "Fill All Fields"
      );
      return;
    }

    const item = {
      id: Date.now(),
      name,
      stock,
      unit,
      updatedAt:
        new Date().toLocaleDateString(),
    };

    const updated = [
      item,
      ...items,
    ];

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-inventory",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setItems(updated);

    setName("");
    setStock("");

    alert(
      "Inventory Added"
    );
  }

  function updateStock(
    id: number,
    value: string
  ) {
    const updated =
      items.map((item) =>
        item.id === id
          ? {
              ...item,
              stock: value,
              updatedAt:
                new Date().toLocaleDateString(),
            }
          : item
      );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-inventory",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setItems(updated);
  }

  function deleteItem(
    id: number
  ) {
    if (
      !confirm(
        "Delete Item?"
      )
    )
      return;

    const updated =
      items.filter(
        (item) =>
          item.id !== id
      );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-inventory",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setItems(updated);
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto">

        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-8">
          Inventory Management
        </h1>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6 mb-8">

          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">
            Add Inventory
          </h2>

          <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">

            <input
              value={name}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setName(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              placeholder="Item Name"
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

            <input
              value={stock}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setStock(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              placeholder="Stock"
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

            <select
              value={unit}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setUnit(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            >
              <option>Kg</option>
              <option>Gram</option>
              <option>Piece</option>
              <option>Packet</option>
              <option>Box</option>
            </select>

          </div>

          <button
            onClick={addItem}
            className="mt-5 bg-green-600 text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold"
          >
            Add Item
          </button>

        </div>

        <div className="space-y-4">

          {items.map(
            (item) => (

              <div
                key={item.id}
                className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6 flex justify-between items-center"
              >

                <div>

                  <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">
                    {item.name}
                  </h2>

                  <p className="text-gray-500">
                    Updated:
                    {" "}
                    {item.updatedAt}
                  </p>

                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">

                  <input
                    value={item.stock}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      updateStock(
                        item.id,
                        e.target.value
                      )
                    }
                    className="border p-2 rounded-lg w-24"
                  />

                  <span>
                    {item.unit}
                  </span>

                  <button
                    onClick={() =>
                      deleteItem(
                        item.id
                      )
                    }
                    className="bg-red-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl"
                  >
                    Delete
                  </button>

                </div>

              </div>

            )
          )}

        </div>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}









==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\login\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { FormEvent, useState } from "react";
import {
  ShieldCheck,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
  Loader2,
  UserPlus,
} from "lucide-react";

export default function AdminLoginPage() {
  const [setupMode, setSetupMode] = useState(false);
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [mobile, setMobile] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");

  async function submit(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault();

    setLoading(true);
    setMessage("");

    try {
      const endpoint = setupMode
        ? "/api/auth/staff-setup"
        : "/api/auth/staff-login";

      const body = setupMode
        ? {
            name,
            mobile,
            password,
            role: "SUPER_ADMIN",
          }
        : {
            mobile,
            password,
            role: "SUPER_ADMIN",
          };

      const response = await fetch(endpoint, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify(body),
      });

      const data = await response.json();

      if (!response.ok || !data.success) {
        if (
          !setupMode &&
          data.code === "NO_ACCOUNT"
        ) {
          setSetupMode(true);
        }

        throw new Error(
          data.message ||
            "Unable to continue"
        );
      }

      window.location.href = "/admin";
    } catch (error) {
      setMessage(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "Unable to continue"
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-50 via-white to-emerald-50 px-4 py-10">
      <div className="mx-auto flex min-h-[80vh] max-w-md items-center justify-center">

        <div className="w-full rounded-3xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-7 shadow-xl">

          <div className="mb-7 text-center">

            <div className="mx-auto mb-4 flex h-16 w-16 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-slate-900 text-white">
              <ShieldCheck size={34} />
            </div>

            <h1 className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-slate-900">
              Super Admin
            </h1>

            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
              {setupMode
                ? "Create the first Super Admin account"
                : "Secure admin login"}
            </p>

          </div>

          <form
            onSubmit={submit}
            className="space-y-4"
          >

            {setupMode && (
              <input
                value={name}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setName(e.target.value)
                }
                placeholder="Full Name"
                className="h-12 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"
              />
            )}

            <input
              value={mobile}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setMobile(
                  e.target.value
                    .replace(/\D/g, "")
                    .slice(0, 10)
                )
              }
              placeholder="Mobile Number"
              inputMode="numeric"
              className="h-12 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"
            />

            <div className="relative">

              <input
                value={password}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setPassword(
                    e.target.value
                  )
                }
                type={
                  showPassword
                    ? "text"
                    : "password"
                }
                placeholder="Password"
                className="h-12 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 pr-12 outline-none focus:border-emerald-500"
              />

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() =>
                  setShowPassword(
                    (value) => !value
                  )
                }
                className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-500"
                aria-label="Toggle password visibility"
              >
                {showPassword ? (
                  <EyeOff size={20} />
                ) : (
                  <Eye size={20} />
                )}
              </button>

            </div>

            {message && (
              <div className="rounded-xl bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-red-700">
                {message}
              </div>
            )}

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading}
              className="flex h-12 w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 font-bold text-white transition hover:bg-emerald-700 disabled:opacity-60"
            >
              {loading ? (
                <Loader2
                  className="animate-spin"
                  size={19}
                />
              ) : setupMode ? (
                <UserPlus size={19} />
              ) : (
                <ShieldCheck size={19} />
              )}

              {loading
                ? "Please wait..."
                : setupMode
                  ? "Create Super Admin"
                  : "Login"}
            </button>

          </form>

          <div className="mt-5 text-center">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setSetupMode(
                  (value) => !value
                );
                setMessage("");
              }}
              className="text-sm font-semibold text-emerald-700 hover:underline"
            >
              {setupMode
                ? "Back to Login"
                : "First time setup"}
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-6 border-t pt-5 text-center text-xs text-slate-400">
            Processing Team Login:{" "}
            <a
              className="font-semibold text-emerald-700"
              href="/processing/login"
            >
              /processing/login
            </a>
          </div>

        </div>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}








==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\loyalty\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function LoyaltyPage() {
  const [customers, setCustomers] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadCustomers();
  }, []);

  function loadCustomers() {
    const orders = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem(
        "freshcut-orders"
      ) || "[]"
    );

    const customerMap: any = {};

    orders.forEach(
      (order: any) => {
        const phone =
          order.phone;

        if (
          !customerMap[phone]
        ) {
          customerMap[phone] = {
            customer:
              order.customer,
            phone:
              order.phone,
            orders: 0,
            spend: 0,
            points: 0,
          };
        }

        customerMap[
          phone
        ].orders += 1;

        customerMap[
          phone
        ].spend += Number(
          order.total || 0
        );

        customerMap[
          phone
        ].points =
          Math.floor(
            customerMap[
              phone
            ].spend / 100
          );
      }
    );

    setCustomers(
      Object.values(
        customerMap
      )
    );
  }

  function redeemPoints(
    phone: string
  ) {
    const updated =
      customers.map(
        (customer: any) =>
          customer.phone ===
          phone
            ? {
                ...customer,
                points: 0,
              }
            : customer
      );

    setCustomers(updated);

    alert(
      "Points Redeemed Successfully"
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto">

        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-8">
          Loyalty Program
        </h1>

        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">

          {customers.map(
            (
              customer: any,
              index
            ) => (

              <div
                key={index}
                className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6"
              >

                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold">
                  {
                    customer.customer
                  }
                </h2>

                <p className="mt-2">
                  ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã¢â‚¬Å“Ã…Â¾{" "}
                  {
                    customer.phone
                  }
                </p>

                <div className="mt-5">

                  <div className="bg-blue-50 rounded-xl p-3 mb-3">

                    Orders:
                    {" "}
                    <strong>
                      {
                        customer.orders
                      }
                    </strong>

                  </div>

                  <div className="bg-green-50 rounded-xl p-3 mb-3">

                    Spend:
                    {" "}
                    <strong>
                      Ã¢â€šÂ¹
                      {
                        customer.spend
                      }
                    </strong>

                  </div>

                  <div className="bg-yellow-50 rounded-xl p-3">

                    Reward Points:
                    {" "}
                    <strong>
                      {
                        customer.points
                      }
                    </strong>

                  </div>

                </div>

                <button
                  onClick={() =>
                    redeemPoints(
                      customer.phone
                    )
                  }
                  className="w-full mt-5 bg-green-600 text-white py-3 rounded-xl font-bold"
                >
                  Redeem Points
                </button>

              </div>

            )
          )}

        </div>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\notifications\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function NotificationsPage() {
  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadNotifications();
  }, []);

  function loadNotifications() {
    const data = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem("freshcut-notifications") || "[]"
    );

    setNotifications(data);
  }

  function sendNotification() {
    if (!title || !message) {
      alert("Fill All Fields");
      return;
    }

    const notification = {
      id: Date.now(),
      title,
      message,
      date: new Date().toLocaleString(),
      status: "Sent",
    };

    const updated = [notification, ...notifications];

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-notifications",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setNotifications(updated);
    setTitle("");
    setMessage("");

    alert("Notification Sent Successfully");
  }

  function deleteNotification(id: number) {
    if (!confirm("Delete Notification?")) return;

    const updated = notifications.filter(
      (item) => item.id !== id
    );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-notifications",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setNotifications(updated);
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-50 via-white to-emerald-50 p-6">

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto space-y-6">

        <div className="bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-xl border border-white rounded-3xl shadow-xl p-8">

          <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold text-slate-800">
            Notification Center
          </h1>

          <p className="text-slate-500 mt-2">
            Customer Alerts & Push Notifications
          </p>

        </div>

        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-5">

          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-lg p-6">
            <p className="text-slate-500">Total Notifications</p>
            <h2 className="text-4xl font-bold text-slate-800 mt-2">
              {notifications.length}
            </h2>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-lg p-6">
            <p className="text-slate-500">Today's Broadcast</p>
            <h2 className="text-4xl font-bold text-emerald-600 mt-2">
              {notifications.length}
            </h2>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-lg p-6">
            <p className="text-slate-500">Status</p>
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-blue-600 mt-3">
              Active
            </h2>
          </div>

        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-xl p-8">

          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-6">
            Send Notification
          </h2>

          <div className="space-y-4">

            <input
              value={title}
              onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Notification Title"
              className="w-full border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-2xl"
            />

            <textarea
              value={message}
              onChange={(e) => setMessage(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Notification Message"
              rows={5}
              className="w-full border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-2xl"
            />

            <button
              onClick={sendNotification}
              className="bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-2xl font-semibold"
            >
              Send Notification
            </button>

          </div>

        </div>

        <div className="space-y-4">

          {notifications.map((item) => (

            <div
              key={item.id}
              className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-lg p-6"
            >

              <div className="flex justify-between gap-4">

                <div>

                  <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-800">
                    {item.title}
                  </h2>

                  <p className="mt-2 text-slate-600">
                    {item.message}
                  </p>

                  <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-slate-400">
                    {item.date}
                  </p>

                </div>

                <button
                  onClick={() =>
                    deleteNotification(item.id)
                  }
                  className="bg-red-500 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl h-fit"
                >
                  Delete
                </button>

              </div>

            </div>

          ))}

        </div>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function OrderBoardPage() {
  const [board, setBoard] =
    useState<any>({
      pendingAcceptance: [],
      accepted: [],
      pickedUp: [],
      outForDelivery: [],
      delivered: [],
    });

  useEffect(() => {
    loadBoard();

    const interval =
      setInterval(
        loadBoard,
        10000
      );

    return () =>
      clearInterval(
        interval
      );
  }, []);

  async function loadBoard() {
    try {
      const res =
        await fetch(
          "/api/admin-order-board"
        );

      const data =
        await res.json();

      setBoard(data);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);

  const columns = [

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";

export default function OrdersPage() {
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [boys, setBoys] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState("All");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, []);

  async function loadData() {
    try {
      const [ordersRes, boysRes] = await Promise.all([
        fetch("/api/orders"),
        fetch("/api/delivery-boys"),
      ]);

      const ordersData = await ordersRes.json();
      const boysData = await boysRes.json();

      setOrders(Array.isArray(ordersData) ? ordersData : ordersData?.orders || []);
      setBoys(
        Array.isArray(boysData)
          ? boysData
          : boysData?.riders || []
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  async function assignOrder(orderId: number, riderId: number) {
    if (!riderId) return;

    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/delivery-control", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          orderId,
          riderId,
        }),
      });

      const data = await res.json();

      if (!res.ok) {
        alert(data.error || "Assignment Failed");
        return;
      }

      alert("Order Assigned Successfully");
      loadData();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
      alert("Something Went Wrong");
    }
  }

  const statuses = useMemo(() => {
    const values = orders
      .map((order) => order.status)
      .filter(Boolean);

    return ["All", ...Array.from(new Set(values))];
  }, [orders]);

  const filteredOrders = useMemo(() => {
    const keyword = search.toLowerCase().trim();

    return orders.filter((order) => {
      const matchesSearch =
        !keyword ||
        String(order.id || "").toLowerCase().includes(keyword) ||
        String(order.customer || "").toLowerCase().includes(keyword) ||
        String(order.phone || "").toLowerCase().includes(keyword) ||
        String(order.address || "").toLowerCase().includes(keyword);

      const matchesStatus =
        statusFilter === "All" ||
        order.status === statusFilter;

      return matchesSearch && matchesStatus;
    });
  }, [orders, search, statusFilter]);

  const totalAmount = orders.reduce(
    (sum, order) => sum + Number(order.amount || 0),
    0
  );

  const pendingCount = orders.filter(
    (order) =>
      !["Delivered", "Cancelled"].includes(order.status)
  ).length;

  const assignedCount = orders.filter((order) =>
    String(order.status || "")
      .toLowerCase()
      .includes("assigned")
  ).length;

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-[70vh] flex items-center justify-center bg-[#f4f7f7]">
        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-200 px-8 py-6 text-center">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 border-4 border-[#159447] border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto" />
          <p className="mt-4 text-sm font-semibold text-gray-600">
            Loading Orders...
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f4f7f7] p-4 md:p-6">
      <div className="max-w-[1500px] mx-auto space-y-6">

        {/* HEADER */}
        <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-2xl shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
          <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-[#063f3d] to-[#0b625c] px-6 py-7 text-white">
            <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row lg:items-center lg:justify-between gap-5">
              <div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-white/10 border border-white/20 flex items-center justify-center text-2xl">
                    ðŸ“¦
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold">
                      Orders Management
                    </h1>

                    <p className="text-sm text-white/70 mt-1">
                      Manage orders and assign delivery riders
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <button
                onClick={loadData}
                className="bg-white text-[#063f3d] px-5 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm hover:bg-gray-100 transition"
              >
                â†» Refresh Orders
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* STATS */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 divide-x divide-gray-100">
            <div className="p-5">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                Total Orders
              </p>
              <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-[#063f3d] mt-1">
                {orders.length}
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="p-5">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                Active Orders
              </p>
              <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-orange-600 mt-1">
                {pendingCount}
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="p-5">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                Assigned
              </p>
              <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-blue-600 mt-1">
                {assignedCount}
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="p-5">
              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                Order Value
              </p>
              <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-[#159447] mt-1">
                â‚¹{totalAmount.toLocaleString("en-IN")}
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* FILTER BAR */}
        <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-2xl shadow-sm p-4">
          <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-3">
            <div className="relative flex-1">
              <span className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400">
                ðŸ”
              </span>

              <input
                value={search}
                onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="Search order, customer, mobile or address..."
                className="w-full border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl pl-11 pr-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#159447]/20 focus:border-[#159447]"
              />
            </div>

            <select
              value={statusFilter}
              onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value)}
              className="md:w-56 border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#159447]/20 focus:border-[#159447]"
            >
              {statuses.map((status) => (
                <option key={status} value={status}>
                  {status === "All" ? "All Statuses" : status}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* ORDERS */}
        <div className="space-y-4">

          {filteredOrders.map((order: any) => {
            const status = String(order.status || "Unknown");

            const statusLower = status.toLowerCase();

            const statusClass =
              statusLower.includes("deliver")
                ? "bg-green-50 text-green-700 border-green-200"
                : statusLower.includes("cancel")
                ? "bg-red-50 text-red-700 border-red-200"
                : statusLower.includes("assign")
                ? "bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border-blue-200"
                : statusLower.includes("process")
                ? "bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border-purple-200"
                : "bg-orange-50 text-orange-700 border-orange-200";

            return (
              <div
                key={order.id}
                className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-2xl shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition overflow-hidden"
              >
                <div className="p-5 md:p-6">

                  <div className="flex flex-col xl:flex-row xl:items-start xl:justify-between gap-5">

                    {/* ORDER INFO */}
                    <div className="flex-1">

                      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 mb-5">
                        <div className="bg-[#063f3d] text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold">
                          #{order.id}
                        </div>

                        <span
                          className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-full border text-xs font-bold ${statusClass}`}
                        >
                          {status}
                        </span>
                      </div>

                      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4 gap-4">

                        <div className="bg-gray-50 rounded-xl p-4 border border-gray-100">
                          <p className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider font-bold text-gray-400">
                            Customer
                          </p>
                          <p className="font-bold text-gray-800 mt-1">
                            {order.customer || "â€”"}
                          </p>
                        </div>

                        <div className="bg-gray-50 rounded-xl p-4 border border-gray-100">
                          <p className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider font-bold text-gray-400">
                            Mobile
                          </p>
                          <p className="font-bold text-gray-800 mt-1">
                            {order.phone || "â€”"}
                          </p>
                        </div>

                        <div className="bg-gray-50 rounded-xl p-4 border border-gray-100">
                          <p className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider font-bold text-gray-400">
                            Order Value
                          </p>
                          <p className="font-bold text-[#159447] mt-1">
                            â‚¹{Number(order.amount || 0).toLocaleString("en-IN")}
                          </p>
                        </div>

                        <div className="bg-gray-50 rounded-xl p-4 border border-gray-100">
                          <p className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider font-bold text-gray-400">
                            Delivery
                          </p>
                          <p className="font-bold text-gray-800 mt-1">
                            {statusLower.includes("deliver")
                              ? "Completed"
                              : statusLower.includes("assign")
                              ? "Rider Assigned"
                              : "Awaiting Assignment"}
                          </p>
                        </div>

                      </div>

                      <div className="mt-4 bg-[#f8faf9] border border-gray-100 rounded-xl p-4">
                        <p className="text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider font-bold text-gray-400 mb-1">
                          Delivery Address
                        </p>

                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed">
                          {order.address || "Address not available"}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* ASSIGN RIDER */}
                    <div className="xl:w-80 bg-[#f8faf9] border border-gray-200 rounded-2xl p-5">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3">
                        <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-lg bg-[#063f3d] text-white flex items-center justify-center">
                          ðŸšš
                        </div>

                        <div>

                            Delivery Rider
                          </p>
                          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">
                            Assign this order
                          </p>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <select
                        className="w-full border border-gray-200 bg-white p-3 rounded-xl text-sm font-semibold outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#159447]/20 focus:border-[#159447]"
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          assignOrder(
                            order.id,
                            Number(e.target.value)
                          )
                        }
                        defaultValue=""
                      >
                        <option value="">
                          Select Rider
                        </option>

                        {(boys || []).map((boy: any) => (
                          <option
                            key={boy.id}
                            value={boy.id}
                          >
                            {boy.name} - {boy.mobile}
                          </option>
                        ))}
                      </select>

                      {boys.length === 0 && (
                        <p className="text-xs text-red-600 mt-3">

          {filteredOrders.length === 0 && (
            <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-2xl shadow-sm p-14 text-center">
              <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-gray-100 flex items-center justify-center text-3xl mx-auto">

  );

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function PaymentCenterPage() {
  const [config, setConfig] = useState<any>(null);
  const [health, setHealth] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  async function loadPaymentStatus() {
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const [configResponse, healthResponse] = await Promise.all([
        fetch("/api/payments/config"),
        fetch("/api/payments/health"),
      ]);

      const configData = await configResponse.json();
      const healthData = await healthResponse.json();

      setConfig(configData);
      setHealth(healthData);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Payment Center error:", error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    loadPaymentStatus();
  }, []);

  const gatewayStatus = health?.status || "Unknown";

  const items = [
    {
      title: "Razorpay Key",
      status: config?.razorpayConfigured ? "Configured" : "Missing",
      ok: !!config?.razorpayConfigured,
      icon: "KEY",
    },
    {
      title: "Razorpay Secret",
      status: config?.razorpaySecretConfigured ? "Configured" : "Missing",
      ok: !!config?.razorpaySecretConfigured,
      icon: "SEC",
    },
    {
      title: "Gateway Health",
      status: gatewayStatus,
      ok: String(gatewayStatus).toLowerCase() === "ok" ||
          String(gatewayStatus).toLowerCase() === "healthy",
      icon: "API",
    },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1600px]">
        <div className="mb-7 flex flex-col gap-4 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-emerald-700">
              Fresh2Cook Admin
            </p>
            <h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38] md:text-4xl">
              Payment Center
            </h1>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
              Payment gateway configuration and system health.
            </p>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={loadPaymentStatus}
            className="w-fit rounded-xl bg-[#063f3d] px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white hover:bg-[#0a514d]"
          >
            {loading ? "Checking..." : "â†» Refresh Status"}
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-7 grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-3">
          {items.map((item) => (
            <div
              key={item.title}
              className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm"
            >
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
                <div className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-[#e7f3f1] text-xs font-extrabold text-[#063f3d]">
                  {item.icon}
                </div>

                <span
                  className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold ${
                    item.ok
                      ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700"
                      : "bg-red-50 text-red-700"
                  }`}
                >
                  {item.status}
                </span>
              </div>

              <h2 className="mt-5 text-lg font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
                {item.title}
              </h2>

              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
                Payment system status
              </p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
                Payment Gateway
              </h2>
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
                Current Razorpay integration status.

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function PayoutsPage() {
  const [vendors, setVendors] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [payouts, setPayouts] = useState<any[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      setVendors(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("freshcut-vendors") || "[]"));
      setPayouts(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("freshcut-payouts") || "[]"));
    } catch {
      setVendors([]);
      setPayouts([]);
    }
  }, []);

  function makePayment(vendor: any) {
    const amount = prompt(`Enter Payment Amount For ${vendor.name}`);
    if (!amount) return;

    const payout = {
      id: Date.now(),
      vendorId: vendor.id,
      vendor: vendor.name,
      mobile: vendor.mobile,
      amount,
      date: new Date().toLocaleDateString(),
      status: "Paid",
    };

    const updated = [payout, ...payouts];
    localStorage.setItem("freshcut-payouts", JSON.stringify(updated));
    setPayouts(updated);
    alert("Payment Recorded Successfully");
  }

  const totalPayout = payouts.reduce(
    (sum, item) => sum + Number(item.amount || 0),
    0
  );

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1600px]">
        <div className="mb-7">
          <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-emerald-700">
            Fresh2Cook Admin
          </p>
          <h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38] md:text-4xl">
            Vendor Payouts
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
            Manage vendor payments and payout history.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-7 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-100 bg-emerald-50 p-6 shadow-sm">
          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-emerald-700">Total Payout</p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-4xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
            â‚¹{totalPayout.toLocaleString("en-IN")}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-8 grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
          {vendors.map((vendor) => (
            <div
              key={vendor.id}
              className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm"
            >
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
                    {vendor.name}
                  </h2>
                  <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">
                    {vendor.mobile || "No mobile"}
                  </p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
                    {vendor.category || "Vendor"}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <span className="rounded-full bg-emerald-50 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-emerald-700">
                  ACTIVE
                </span>
              </div>

              <button
                onClick={() => makePayment(vendor)}
                className="mt-5 w-full rounded-xl bg-[#063f3d] py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white hover:bg-[#0a514d]"
              >
                Record Payment
              </button>
            </div>
          ))}

          {!vendors.length && (
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-white p-10 text-center text-sm text-slate-500 md:col-span-2 xl:col-span-3">
              No vendors available.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
          <div className="mb-5">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
              Payment History
            </h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
              Recently recorded vendor payouts.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-3">
            {payouts.map((item) => (
              <div
                key={item.id}
                className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-xl border border-slate-100 bg-slate-50 p-4 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between"
              >
                <div>
                  <p className="font-bold text-[#123b38]">{item.vendor}</p>
                  <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">{item.mobile}</p>
                </div>

                <div className="text-left md:text-right">
                  <p className="font-extrabold text-emerald-700">
                    â‚¹{Number(item.amount || 0).toLocaleString("en-IN")}
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">{item.date}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}

            {!payouts.length && (
              <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 p-10 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
                No payment history available.
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
