"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function PayrollPage() {
  const [staff, setStaff] = useState<any[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadStaff();
  }, []);

  function loadStaff() {
    try {
      setStaff(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("freshcut-staff") || "[]"));
    } catch {
      setStaff([]);
    }
  }

  function markPaid(id: number) {
    const updated = staff.map((item) =>
      item.id === id
        ? {
            ...item,
            salaryStatus: "Paid",
            paidDate: new Date().toLocaleDateString(),
          }
        : item
    );

    localStorage.setItem("freshcut-staff", JSON.stringify(updated));
    setStaff(updated);
  }

  const totalSalary = staff.reduce(
    (sum, item) => sum + Number(item.salary || 0),
    0
  );

  const paidSalary = staff
    .filter((item) => item.salaryStatus === "Paid")
    .reduce((sum, item) => sum + Number(item.salary || 0), 0);

  const pendingSalary = totalSalary - paidSalary;

  const cards = [
    ["Total Salary", totalSalary, "bg-blue-50", "text-blue-700"],
    ["Paid Salary", paidSalary, "bg-emerald-50", "text-emerald-700"],
    ["Pending Salary", pendingSalary, "bg-red-50", "text-red-700"],
  ];

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1600px]">
        <div className="mb-7">
          <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-emerald-700">
            Fresh2Cook Admin
          </p>
          <h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38] md:text-4xl">
            Payroll Management
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
            Track salary payments and pending payroll.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-7 grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
          {cards.map(([title, value, bg, color]) => (
            <div
              key={String(title)}
              className={`rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 ${bg} p-6 shadow-sm`}
            >
              <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-600">
                {title}
              </p>
              <p className={`mt-2 text-3xl font-extrabold ${color}`}>
                â‚¹{Number(value).toLocaleString("en-IN")}
              </p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-4">
          {staff.map((employee) => (
            <div
              key={employee.id}
              className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm"
            >
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
                    {employee.name}
                  </h2>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
                    {employee.role}
                  </p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
                    {employee.mobile || employee.phone || "â€”"}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="text-left md:text-right">
                  <p className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-emerald-700">
                    â‚¹{Number(employee.salary || 0).toLocaleString("en-IN")}
                  </p>

                  <span
                    className={`mt-2 inline-block rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold ${
                      employee.salaryStatus === "Paid"
                        ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700"
                        : "bg-amber-50 text-amber-700"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {employee.salaryStatus || "Pending"}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
                <button
                  onClick={() => markPaid(employee.id)}
                  className="rounded-xl bg-[#063f3d] px-6 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white hover:bg-[#0a514d]"
                >
                  Mark Salary Paid
                </button>

                {employee.paidDate && (
                  <div className="flex items-center rounded-xl bg-slate-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-500">
                    Paid On: {employee.paidDate}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}

          {!staff.length && (
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-white p-12 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
              No staff payroll records available.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}







==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\pos\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useState } from "react";

export default function POSPage() {
  const [customer, setCustomer] = useState("");
  const [mobile, setMobile] = useState("");
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState("");
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");

  async function generateInvoice() {
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/orders", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          customer,
          amount: Number(amount),
          status: "Paid",
        }),
      });

      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error("Failed");
      }

      const data = await res.json();

      setMessage(
        `Invoice Created Successfully. Order ID: ${data.id}`
      );

      setCustomer("");
      setMobile("");
      setAmount("");
    } catch (err) {
      setMessage("Error creating invoice");
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-8">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-6">
        POS Billing
      </h1>

      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl p-6 shadow max-w-xl">
        <input
          value={customer}
          onChange={(e) => setCustomer(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Customer Name"
          className="border p-3 w-full mb-4"
        />

        <input
          value={mobile}
          onChange={(e) => setMobile(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Mobile Number"
          className="border p-3 w-full mb-4"
        />

        <input
          value={amount}
          onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="Bill Amount"
          type="number"
          className="border p-3 w-full mb-4"
        />

        <button
          onClick={generateInvoice}
          className="bg-green-600 text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-lg"
        >
          Generate Invoice
        </button>

        {message && (
          <div className="mt-4 text-green-700 font-semibold">
            {message}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect,useState } from "react";

export default function Page(){

 const [payment,setPayment] =
  useState<any>();

 const [whatsapp,setWhatsapp] =
  useState<any>();

 const [wallet,setWallet] =
  useState<any>();

 useEffect(()=>{

  fetch("/api/payments/razorpay-status")
   .then(r=>r.json())
   .then(setPayment);

  fetch("/api/whatsapp/status")
   .then(r=>r.json())
   .then(setWhatsapp);

  fetch("/api/wallet/summary")
   .then(r=>r.json())
   .then(setWallet);

 },[]);

 return(

  <div className="p-6">

   <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-6">
    Production Control Center
   </h1>

   <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">

    <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow">
      Razorpay
      <div className="text-xl font-bold">
       {payment?.configured ? "Connected" : "Pending"}
      </div>
    </div>

    <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow">
      WhatsApp API
      <div className="text-xl font-bold">
       {whatsapp?.configured ? "Connected" : "Pending"}
      </div>
    </div>

    <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow">
      Wallet Value
      <div className="text-xl font-bold">
       ?{wallet?.totalWallet || 0}
      </div>
    </div>

   </div>

  </div>

 );

}








==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\products-manage\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";

type Product = {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  category?: string;
  pack?: string;
  mrp?: number;
  price?: number;
  stock?: number;
  image?: string;
  description?: string;
  bestSeller?: boolean;
  active?: boolean;
};

type ProductForm = {
  name: string;
  category: string;
  pack: string;
  mrp: string;
  price: string;
  stock: string;
  image: string;
  description: string;
  bestSeller: boolean;
  active: boolean;
};

const emptyForm: ProductForm = {
  name: "",
  category: "Vegetables",
  pack: "Piece",
  mrp: "",
  price: "",
  stock: "",
  image: "",
  description: "",
  bestSeller: false,
  active: true,
};

async function getResponseData(response: Response) {
  const text = await response.text();

  try {
    return text ? JSON.parse(text) : {};
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

export default function ProductsManagePage() {
  const [products, setProducts] = useState<Product[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState("");
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [categoryFilter, setCategoryFilter] = useState("All");
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState("All");
  const [editingProduct, setEditingProduct] = useState<Product | null>(null);

  const [newProduct, setNewProduct] =
    useState<ProductForm>(emptyForm);

  const [editForm, setEditForm] =
    useState<ProductForm>(emptyForm);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadProducts();
  }, []);

  async function loadProducts() {
    try {
      setLoading(true);
      setErrorMessage("");

      const response = await fetch("/api/products", {
        cache: "no-store",
      });

      const data = await getResponseData(response);

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          data?.error || "Failed to load products"
        );
      }

      const list = Array.isArray(data)
        ? data
        : data?.products || [];

      setProducts(list);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);

      setErrorMessage(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "Failed to load products"
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  function validateForm(form: ProductForm) {
    const name = form.name.trim();
    const pack = form.pack.trim();
    const mrp = Number(form.mrp || 0);
    const price = Number(form.price || 0);
    const stock = Number(form.stock || 0);

    if (!name) {
      return "Please enter Product Name.";
    }

    if (!pack) {
      return "Please enter Pack Size.";
    }

    if (!Number.isFinite(mrp) || mrp < 0) {
      return "Please enter a valid MRP.";
    }

    if (!Number.isFinite(price) || price < 0) {
      return "Please enter a valid Our Price.";
    }

    if (mrp < price) {
      return "MRP cannot be less than Our Price.";
    }

    if (!Number.isFinite(stock) || stock < 0) {
      return "Please enter a valid Stock.";
    }

    return "";
  }

  async function addProduct() {
    const validationError = validateForm(newProduct);

    if (validationError) {
      setErrorMessage(validationError);
      return;
    }

    try {
      setSaving(true);
      setErrorMessage("");

      const response = await fetch("/api/products", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          name: newProduct.name.trim(),
          category: newProduct.category,
          pack: newProduct.pack,
          mrp: Number(newProduct.mrp || 0),
          price: Number(newProduct.price || 0),
          stock: Number(newProduct.stock || 0),
          image: newProduct.image.trim(),
          description: newProduct.description.trim(),
          bestSeller: newProduct.bestSeller,
          active: newProduct.active,
        }),
      });

      const data = await getResponseData(response);

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          data?.error || "Failed to add product"
        );
      }

      const created = data?.product || data;

      setProducts((current) => [
        created,
        ...current,
      ]);

      setNewProduct(emptyForm);

      alert("Product Added Successfully");
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);

      setErrorMessage(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "Failed to add product"
      );
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  function openEdit(product: Product) {
    setEditingProduct(product);

    setEditForm({
      name: product.name || "",
      category: product.category || "Vegetables",
      pack: product.pack || "Piece",
      mrp: String(product.mrp ?? ""),
      price: String(product.price ?? ""),
      stock: String(product.stock ?? ""),
      image: product.image || "",
      description: product.description || "",
      bestSeller: product.bestSeller === true,
      active: product.active !== false,
    });

    setErrorMessage("");
  }

  function closeEdit() {
    setEditingProduct(null);
    setEditForm(emptyForm);
  }

  async function saveEdit() {
    if (!editingProduct) return;

    const validationError = validateForm(editForm);

    if (validationError) {
      setErrorMessage(validationError);
      return;
    }

    try {
      setSaving(true);
      setErrorMessage("");

      const response = await fetch(
        `/api/products/${editingProduct.id}`,
        {
          method: "PATCH",
          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            name: editForm.name.trim(),
            category: editForm.category,
            pack: editForm.pack,
            mrp: Number(editForm.mrp || 0),
            price: Number(editForm.price || 0),
            stock: Number(editForm.stock || 0),
            image: editForm.image.trim(),
            description: editForm.description.trim(),
            bestSeller: editForm.bestSeller,
            active: editForm.active,
          }),
        }
      );

      const data = await getResponseData(response);

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          data?.error || "Failed to update product"
        );
      }

      setProducts((current) =>
        current.map((product) =>
          product.id === editingProduct.id
            ? data?.product || data
            : product
        )
      );

      closeEdit();

      alert("Product Updated Successfully");
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);

      setErrorMessage(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "Failed to update product"
      );
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function toggleBestSeller(product: Product) {
    try {
      setErrorMessage("");

      const response = await fetch(
        `/api/products/${product.id}`,
        {
          method: "PATCH",
          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            bestSeller: product.bestSeller !== true,
          }),
        }
      );

      const data = await getResponseData(response);

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          data?.error ||
            "Failed to update Best Seller"
        );
      }

      setProducts((current) =>
        current.map((item) =>
          item.id === product.id
            ? data?.product || data
            : item
        )
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);

      setErrorMessage(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "Failed to update Best Seller"
      );
    }
  }

  async function toggleActive(product: Product) {
    try {
      setErrorMessage("");

      const response = await fetch(
        `/api/products/${product.id}`,
        {
          method: "PATCH",
          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            active: product.active === false,
          }),
        }
      );

      const data = await getResponseData(response);

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          data?.error ||
            "Failed to update status"
        );
      }

      setProducts((current) =>
        current.map((item) =>
          item.id === product.id
            ? data?.product || data
            : item
        )
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);

      setErrorMessage(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "Failed to update status"
      );
    }
  }

  async function deleteProduct(product: Product) {
    const confirmed = window.confirm(
      `Delete "${product.name}"?`
    );

    if (!confirmed) return;

    try {
      setErrorMessage("");

      const response = await fetch(
        `/api/products/${product.id}`,
        {
          method: "DELETE",
        }
      );

      const data = await getResponseData(response);

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(
          data?.error || "Failed to delete product"
        );
      }

      setProducts((current) =>
        current.filter(
          (item) => item.id !== product.id
        )
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);

      setErrorMessage(
        error instanceof Error
          ? error.message
          : "Failed to delete product"
      );
    }
  }

  function handleNewImage(
    e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>
  ) {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];

    if (!file) return;

    const reader = new FileReader();

    reader.onloadend = () => {
      setNewProduct((current) => ({
        ...current,
        image: reader.result as string,
      }));
    };

    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  }

  function handleEditImage(
    e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>
  ) {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];

    if (!file) return;

    const reader = new FileReader();

    reader.onloadend = () => {
      setEditForm((current) => ({
        ...current,
        image: reader.result as string,
      }));
    };

    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  }

  const categories = useMemo(() => {
    const values = products
      .map((product) => product.category)
      .filter(Boolean) as string[];

    return [
      "All",
      ...Array.from(new Set(values)),
    ];
  }, [products]);

  const filteredProducts = useMemo(() => {
    const keyword = search
      .toLowerCase()
      .trim();

    return products.filter((product) => {
      const matchesSearch =
        !keyword ||
        product.name
          ?.toLowerCase()
          .includes(keyword) ||
        product.category
          ?.toLowerCase()
          .includes(keyword) ||
        product.pack
          ?.toLowerCase()
          .includes(keyword);

      const matchesCategory =
        categoryFilter === "All" ||
        product.category === categoryFilter;

      const matchesStatus =
        statusFilter === "All" ||
        (statusFilter === "Active"
          ? product.active !== false
          : product.active === false);

      return (
        matchesSearch &&
        matchesCategory &&
        matchesStatus
      );
    });
  }, [
    products,
    search,
    categoryFilter,
    statusFilter,
  ]);

  const totalProducts = products.length;

  const activeProducts = products.filter(
    (product) => product.active !== false
  ).length;

  const bestSellers = products.filter(
    (product) => product.bestSeller === true
  ).length;

  const lowStock = products.filter(
    (product) =>
      Number(product.stock || 0) <= 5
  ).length;

  const newSaving = Math.max(
    Number(newProduct.mrp || 0) -
      Number(newProduct.price || 0),
    0
  );

  const editSaving = Math.max(
    Number(editForm.mrp || 0) -
      Number(editForm.price || 0),
    0
  );

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f4f7f7] p-4 md:p-6">
      <div className="max-w-[1600px] mx-auto space-y-6">

        {/* HEADER */}
        <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-2xl shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
          <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-[#063f3d] to-[#0b625c] px-6 py-7 text-white">
            <div className="flex flex-col xl:flex-row xl:items-center xl:justify-between gap-5">

              <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-xl bg-white/10 border border-white/20 flex items-center justify-center text-2xl">
                  ??
                </div>

                <div>
                  <h1 className="text-2xl md:text-3xl font-bold">
                    Product Management
                  </h1>

                  <p className="text-sm text-white/70 mt-1">
                    Add, edit and manage Fresh2Cook products
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex gap-3">
                <button
                  onClick={loadProducts}
                  className="bg-white text-[#063f3d] px-5 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm hover:bg-gray-100 transition"
                >
                  ? Refresh
                </button>

                <a
                  href="/admin/bulk-products"
                  className="bg-[#159447] text-white px-5 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm hover:bg-[#117b3b] transition"
                >
                  + Bulk Add
                </a>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* KPI */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 divide-x divide-gray-100">

            <div className="p-5">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-gray-400">
                Total Products
              </p>
              <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-[#063f3d] mt-1">
                {totalProducts}
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="p-5">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-gray-400">
                Active
              </p>
              <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-[#159447] mt-1">
                {activeProducts}
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="p-5">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-gray-400">
                Best Sellers
              </p>
              <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-yellow-600 mt-1">
                {bestSellers}
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="p-5">
              <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-gray-400">
                Low Stock
              </p>
              <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-red-600 mt-1">
                {lowStock}
              </p>
            </div>

          </div>
        </div>

        {/* ERROR */}
        {errorMessage && (
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-5 py-4 text-red-700 font-semibold">
            {errorMessage}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* ADD PRODUCT */}
        <div className="bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-2xl shadow-sm overflow-hidden">

          <div className="px-6 py-5 border-b border-gray-100 flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-[#063f3d]">
                Add New Product
              </h2>
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mt-1">
                Create a new Fresh2Cook product
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="hidden md:flex w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-green-50 text-[#159447] items-center justify-center text-xl">
              +
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="p-6">

            <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 gap-4">

              <input
                value={newProduct.name}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setNewProduct({
                    ...newProduct,
                    name: e.target.value,
                  })
                }
                placeholder="Product Name"
                className="border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 p-3.5 rounded-xl outline-none focus:border-[#159447] focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#159447]/10"
              />

              <select
                value={newProduct.category}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setNewProduct({
                    ...newProduct,
                    category: e.target.value,
                  })
                }

              >

              </select>

                  })
                }

                <option value="500g">500g</option>
                <option value="1kg">1kg</option>
              </select>

              <input
                type="number"
                value={newProduct.mrp}
                onChange={(e) =>
                  setNewProduct({
                    ...newProduct,
                    mrp: e.target.value,
                  })
                }
                placeholder="MRP"
                className="border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 p-3.5 rounded-xl outline-none focus:border-[#159447]"
              />

              <input
                type="number"

                  setNewProduct({
                    ...newProduct,

                  })
                }
                placeholder="Our Price"
                className="border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 p-3.5 rounded-xl outline-none focus:border-[#159447]"
              />

              <input
                type="number"

                  setNewProduct({
                    ...newProduct,

                  })

                  Product Image
                </label>

                <input
                  type="file"
                  accept="image/*"
                  onChange={handleNewImage}
                  className="w-full text-sm"
                />

                {newProduct.image && (

                    src={newProduct.image}
                    alt="Preview"
                    className="mt-3 h-24 w-24 object-cover rounded-xl border"
                  />
                )}
              </div>

              <textarea
                value={newProduct.description}
                onChange={(e) =>

                    description: e.target.value,
                  })
                }
                placeholder="Product Description"
                rows={4}
                className="md:col-span-2 xl:col-span-3 border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 p-3.5 rounded-xl outline-none focus:border-[#159447]"
              />

              <button

              >
                {saving ? "Saving..." : "+ Add Product"}

            </div>

        {/* EDIT PRODUCT */}

              ))}

                setStatusFilter(e.target.value)
              }
              className="lg:w-48 border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold outline-none focus:border-[#159447]"
            >
              <option value="All">

              </option>
              <option value="Active">

              </option>
              <option value="Inactive">

              </option>
            </select>

                ??
              </div>

              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-[#063f3d] mt-5">

                Try changing your search or filters.
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-5 2xl:grid-cols-6 gap-4">

              {filteredProducts.map((product) => {
                const productSaving = Math.max(
                  Number(product.mrp || 0) -
                    Number(product.price || 0),
                  0
                );

                const stock = Number(
                  product.stock || 0
                );

                return (
                  <div
                    key={product.id}
                    className={`bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border overflow-hidden hover:shadow-lg transition ${
                      product.bestSeller
                        ? "border-yellow-400"
                        : "border-gray-200"
                    }`}
                  >

                    <div className="relative h-36 bg-gradient-to-br from-gray-50 to-green-50 flex items-center justify-center">

                      <img
                        src={
                          product.image?.trim()
                            ? product.image
                            : "/placeholder-product.png"
                        }
                        alt={product.name}
                        className="w-full h-full object-contain p-3 transition-transform duration-300 hover:scale-110"
                        onError={(e) => {
                          e.currentTarget.src =
                            "/placeholder-product.png";
                        }}
                      />

                      {product.bestSeller && (
                        <div className="absolute top-3 left-3 bg-yellow-400 text-black px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[10px] font-extrabold">
                          BEST SELLER
                        </div>
                      )}

                      <div
                        className={`absolute top-3 right-3 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold ${
                          product.active === false
                            ? "bg-gray-200 text-gray-600"
                            : "bg-green-100 text-green-700"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {product.active === false
                          ? "INACTIVE"
                          : "ACTIVE"}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="p-4">

                      <h3 className="font-bold text-base text-gray-800 truncate">
                        {product.name}
                      </h3>

                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mt-1">
                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">
                          {product.category || "Product"}
                        </p>

                        <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#063f3d]">
                          {product.pack}
                        </p>
                      </div>

                      <div className="mt-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl p-3 space-y-1">

                        <div className="flex justify-between text-xs">
                          <span className="text-gray-500">
                            MRP
                          </span>

                          <span className="line-through text-gray-400">
                            ?{product.mrp || 0}
                          </span>
                        </div>

                        <div className="flex justify-between text-xs">
                          <span className="text-gray-500">
                            Our Price
                          </span>

                          <span className="text-[#159447] font-bold">
                            ?{product.price || 0}
                          </span>
                        </div>

                        <div className="flex justify-between text-xs">
                          <span className="text-gray-500">
                            Saving
                          </span>

                          <span className="text-red-600 font-bold">
                            ?{productSaving}
                          </span>
                        </div>

                      </div>

                      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mt-3">
                        <span className="text-xs text-gray-500">
                          Stock
                        </span>

                        <span
                          className={`text-xs font-bold ${
                            stock <= 5
                              ? "text-red-600"
                              : "text-[#159447]"
                          }`}
                        >
                          {stock}
                        </span>
                      </div>

                      <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-1.5 mt-4">

                        <button
                          onClick={() =>
                            openEdit(product)
                          }
                          className="bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white py-2 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-bold"
                        >
                          Edit
                        </button>

                        <button
                          onClick={() =>
                            toggleBestSeller(product)
                          }
                          className={`py-2 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-bold ${
                            product.bestSeller
                              ? "bg-yellow-400 text-black"
                              : "bg-gray-200 text-gray-700"
                          }`}
                        >
                          Best
                        </button>

                        <button
                          onClick={() =>
                            toggleActive(product)
                          }
                          className={`py-2 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-bold ${
                            product.active === false
                              ? "bg-green-600 text-white"
                              : "bg-gray-700 text-white"
                          }`}
                        >
                          {product.active === false
                            ? "On"
                            : "Off"}
                        </button>

                        <button
                          onClick={() =>
                            deleteProduct(product)
                          }
                          className="bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 text-white py-2 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-bold"
                        >
                          Del
                        </button>

                      </div>

                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}

            </div>
          )}
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useState } from "react";

export default function ProductUploadPage() {
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [price, setPrice] = useState("");
  const [stock, setStock] = useState("");
  const [pack, setPack] = useState("");
  const [image, setImage] = useState("");

  function handleImage(
    e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>
  ) {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];

    if (!file) return;

    const reader = new FileReader();

    reader.onload = () => {
      setImage(
        reader.result as string
      );
    };

    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  }

  function saveProduct() {
    if (!name || !price) {
      alert(
        "Name and Price Required"
      );
      return;
    }

    const products = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem(
        "freshcut-products"
      ) || "[]"
    );

    products.unshift({
      id: Date.now(),
      name,
      price: Number(price),
      stock,
      pack,
      image,
    });

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-products",
      JSON.stringify(products)
    );

    alert(
      "Product Saved Successfully"
    );

    setName("");
    setPrice("");
    setStock("");
    setPack("");
    setImage("");
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">

      <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto">

              value={name}

                setName(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }

                  e.target.value

            />

              value={stock}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setStock(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              placeholder="Stock"
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

              onChange={(e) =>
                setPack(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              placeholder="Pack Size"
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

          </div>

          <div className="mt-6">

            <input
              type="file"
              accept="image/*"
              onChange={handleImage}
            />

          </div>

            <div className="mt-6">

              <img
                src={image}
                alt="Preview"
                className="w-64 rounded-2xl border shadow"
              />

            </div>

            onClick={saveProduct}

          >
            Save Product
          </button>

        </div>

      </div>

==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\profit-loss\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function ProfitLossPage() {
  const [revenue, setRevenue] = useState(0);
  const [expenses, setExpenses] = useState(0);
  const [purchases, setPurchases] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => { loadData(); }, []);

  function loadData() {
    try {
      const orders = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("freshcut-orders") || "[]");
      const expensesData = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("freshcut-expenses") || "[]");
      const purchasesData = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("freshcut-purchases") || "[]");

      setRevenue(orders.reduce((sum:number,item:any) => sum + Number(item.total || 0), 0));
      setExpenses(expensesData.reduce((sum:number,item:any) => sum + Number(item.amount || 0), 0));
      setPurchases(purchasesData.reduce((sum:number,item:any) => sum + Number(item.amount || 0), 0));
    } catch {}
  }

  const cost = expenses + purchases;
  const profit = revenue - cost;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1500px]">
        <Header title="Profit & Loss" subtitle="Business revenue, expenses and purchase analysis." />
        <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
          <Kpi title="Revenue" value={`â‚¹${revenue}`} green />
          <Kpi title="Expenses" value={`â‚¹${expenses}`} red />
          <Kpi title="Purchases" value={`â‚¹${purchases}`} />
          <Kpi title="Net Profit" value={`â‚¹${profit}`} green={profit >= 0} red={profit < 0} />
        </div>
        <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">Financial Summary</h2>
          <div className="mt-5 space-y-4">
            <Row title="Total Revenue" value={revenue} />
            <Row title="Total Expenses" value={expenses} />
            <Row title="Total Purchases" value={purchases} />
            <div className="border-t pt-4"><Row title="Net Profit" value={profit} bold /></div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
function Header({title,subtitle}:any){return <div className="mb-7"><p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[.18em] text-emerald-700">Fresh2Cook Admin</p><h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38] md:text-4xl">{title}</h1><p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">{subtitle}</p></div>}
function Kpi({title,value,green,red}:any){return <div className={`rounded-2xl border p-6 shadow-sm ${green ? "border-emerald-100 bg-emerald-50" : red ? "border-red-100 bg-red-50" : "border-slate-200 bg-white"}`}><p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500">{title}</p><p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">{value}</p></div>}
function Row({title,value,bold}:any){return <div className={`flex justify-between ${bold ? "text-xl font-extrabold" : "text-sm"}`}><span className="text-slate-600">{title}</span><strong className={value >= 0 ? "text-[#123b38]" : "text-red-600"}>â‚¹{value}</strong></div>}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function PurchasesPage() {
  const [purchases, setPurchases] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [vendor, setVendor] = useState("");
  const [item, setItem] = useState("");
  const [quantity, setQuantity] = useState("");
  const [amount, setAmount] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadPurchases();
  }, []);

  function loadPurchases() {
    try {
      setPurchases(
        JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("freshcut-purchases") || "[]")
      );
    } catch {
      setPurchases([]);
    }
  }

  function addPurchase() {
    if (!vendor || !item || !quantity || !amount) {
      alert("Fill All Fields");
      return;
    }

    const purchase = {
      id: Date.now(),
      vendor,
      item,
      quantity,
      amount,
      date: new Date().toLocaleDateString(),
    };

    const updated = [purchase, ...purchases];

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-purchases",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setPurchases(updated);
    setVendor("");
    setItem("");
    setQuantity("");
    setAmount("");

    alert("Purchase Added Successfully");
  }

  function deletePurchase(id: number) {
    if (!confirm("Delete Purchase?")) return;

    const updated = purchases.filter((purchase) => purchase.id !== id);

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-purchases",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setPurchases(updated);
  }

  const totalPurchase = purchases.reduce(
    (sum, purchase) => sum + Number(purchase.amount || 0),
    0
  );

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1600px]">
        <div className="mb-7">
          <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-emerald-700">
            Fresh2Cook Admin
          </p>
          <h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38] md:text-4xl">
            Purchase Management
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
            Record purchases, supplier costs and procurement history.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-7 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
          <div className="mb-5">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
              Add Purchase
            </h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">
              Enter supplier and purchase details.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
            <input
              value={vendor}
              onChange={(e) => setVendor(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Vendor Name"
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
            />

            <input
              value={item}
              onChange={(e) => setItem(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Item Name"
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
            />

            <input
              value={quantity}
              onChange={(e) => setQuantity(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Quantity"
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
            />

            <input
              value={amount}
              onChange={(e) => setAmount(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Amount"
              type="number"
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
            />
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={addPurchase}
            className="mt-5 rounded-xl bg-[#063f3d] px-7 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white hover:bg-[#0a514d]"
          >
            + Add Purchase
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-7 rounded-2xl border border-orange-100 bg-orange-50 p-6">
          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-orange-700">
            Total Purchase Cost
          </p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-4xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
            â‚¹{totalPurchase.toLocaleString("en-IN")}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-4">
          {purchases.map((purchase) => (
            <div
              key={purchase.id}
              className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm"
            >
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
                    {purchase.item}
                  </h2>

                  <div className="mt-2 space-y-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
                    <p>Vendor: {purchase.vendor}</p>
                    <p>Quantity: {purchase.quantity}</p>
                    <p>Date: {purchase.date}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="text-left md:text-right">
                  <p className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-orange-700">
                    â‚¹{Number(purchase.amount || 0).toLocaleString("en-IN")}
                  </p>

                  <button
                    onClick={() => deletePurchase(purchase.id)}
                    className="mt-3 rounded-xl bg-red-50 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-bold text-red-700 hover:bg-red-100"
                  >
                    Delete
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}

          {!purchases.length && (
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-white p-12 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
              No purchase records available.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function RealAnalyticsPage() {
  const [data, setData] = useState<any>();
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/api/analytics/overview")
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(setData)
      .catch(() => {})
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, []);

  const cards = [
    ["Total Customers", data?.customers ?? 0],
    ["Total Orders", data?.orders ?? 0],
    ["Total Products", data?.products ?? 0],
  ];

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1500px]">
        <Header title="Real Analytics Dashboard" subtitle="Live business overview from the analytics API." />

          {cards.map(([title,value]) => (
            <div key={title} className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-7 shadow-sm">
              <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500">{title}</p>
              <p className="mt-3 text-4xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">{loading ? "ï¿½" : value}</p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">Live Status</h2>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">{loading ? "Fetching live analytics..." : "Analytics data is connected."}</p>
        </div>

}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function ReportsPage() {
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<any[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      setOrders(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("freshcut-orders") || "[]"));
    } catch {
      setOrders([]);
    }
  }, []);

  const totalOrders = orders.length;
  const totalRevenue = orders.reduce(
    (sum, order) => sum + Number(order.total || order.amount || 0),
    0
  );
  const averageOrder = totalOrders ? Math.round(totalRevenue / totalOrders) : 0;
  const customers = new Set(orders.map((o) => o.phone).filter(Boolean)).size;

  const cards = [
    ["Total Orders", totalOrders, "text-[#123b38]", "bg-slate-50"],
    ["Revenue", `â‚¹${totalRevenue.toLocaleString("en-IN")}`, "text-emerald-700", "bg-emerald-50"],
    ["Avg Order", `â‚¹${averageOrder.toLocaleString("en-IN")}`, "text-blue-700", "bg-blue-50"],
    ["Customers", customers, "text-violet-700", "bg-violet-50"],
  ];

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1600px]">
        <div className="mb-7">
          <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-emerald-700">
            Fresh2Cook Admin

        </div>

          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="mb-7 grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-3">
          {[
            ["Daily Sales", totalRevenue, "emerald"],

==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\reviews\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function ReviewsPage() {
  const [reviews, setReviews] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [customer, setCustomer] = useState("");
  const [rating, setRating] = useState("5");
  const [review, setReview] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadReviews();
  }, []);

  function loadReviews() {
    try {
      setReviews(
        JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("freshcut-reviews") || "[]")
      );
    } catch {
      setReviews([]);
    }
  }

  function addReview() {
    if (!customer.trim() || !review.trim()) {
      alert("Fill All Fields");
      return;
    }

    const newReview = {
      id: Date.now(),
      customer: customer.trim(),
      rating,
      review: review.trim(),
      date: new Date().toLocaleDateString(),
    };

    const updated = [newReview, ...reviews];

    localStorage.setItem("freshcut-reviews", JSON.stringify(updated));
    setReviews(updated);
    setCustomer("");
    setRating("5");
    setReview("");
  }

  function deleteReview(id: number) {
    if (!confirm("Delete Review?")) return;

    const updated = reviews.filter((item) => item.id !== id);

    localStorage.setItem("freshcut-reviews", JSON.stringify(updated));
    setReviews(updated);
  }

  const averageRating = reviews.length
    ? (
        reviews.reduce(
          (sum, item) => sum + Number(item.rating || 0),
          0
        ) / reviews.length
      ).toFixed(1)
    : "0.0";

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1600px]">
        <div className="mb-7">
          <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-emerald-700">
            Fresh2Cook Admin
          </p>
          <h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38] md:text-4xl">
            Customer Reviews
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
            Manage customer feedback and ratings.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-7 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500">
              Total Reviews
            </p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
              {reviews.length}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-amber-100 bg-amber-50 p-5 shadow-sm">
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-amber-700">
              Average Rating
            </p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-extrabold text-amber-800">
              {averageRating} / 5
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-7 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
            Add Customer Review
          </h2>

          <div className="mt-5 grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
            <input
              value={customer}
              onChange={(e) => setCustomer(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Customer Name"
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
            />

            <select
              value={rating}
              onChange={(e) => setRating(e.target.value)}
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
            >
              <option value="5">â˜…â˜…â˜…â˜…â˜… â€” 5 Stars</option>
              <option value="4">â˜…â˜…â˜…â˜…â˜† â€” 4 Stars</option>
              <option value="3">â˜…â˜…â˜…â˜†â˜† â€” 3 Stars</option>
              <option value="2">â˜…â˜…â˜†â˜†â˜† â€” 2 Stars</option>
              <option value="1">â˜…â˜†â˜†â˜†â˜† â€” 1 Star</option>
            </select>

            <button
              onClick={addReview}
              className="rounded-xl bg-[#063f3d] px-6 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white hover:bg-[#0a514d]"
            >
              + Add Review
            </button>
          </div>

          <textarea
            value={review}
            onChange={(e) => setReview(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Customer Review"
            rows={4}
            className="mt-4 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
          {reviews.map((item) => (
            <div
              key={item.id}
              className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm"
            >
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
                    {item.customer}
                  </h2>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-400">
                    {item.date}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <span className="rounded-full bg-amber-50 px-3 py-1 text-sm font-bold text-amber-700">
                  {"â˜…".repeat(Number(item.rating || 0))}
                </span>
              </div>

              <p className="mt-5 text-sm leading-6 text-slate-600">
                {item.review}
              </p>

              <button
                onClick={() => deleteReview(item.id)}
                className="mt-5 w-full rounded-xl bg-red-50 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-red-700 hover:bg-red-100"
              >
                Delete Review
              </button>
            </div>
          ))}

          {!reviews.length && (
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-white p-12 text-center text-sm text-slate-500 md:col-span-2 xl:col-span-3">
              No customer reviews available.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";

type Rider = {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  mobile: string;
  vehicle: string;
  approvalStatus: string;
};

export default function RiderApprovalsPage() {
  const [riders, setRiders] = useState<Rider[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");

  async function loadRiders() {
    try {
      setLoading(true);

      const res = await fetch("/api/admin-rider-approvals", {
        cache: "no-store",
      });

      const data = await res.json();

      setRiders(
        Array.isArray(data)
          ? data
          : Array.isArray(data?.riders)
          ? data.riders
          : Array.isArray(data?.data)
          ? data.data
          : []
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
      setRiders([]);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  async function updateStatus(riderId: number, status: string) {
    try {
      const res = await fetch("/api/admin-rider-approvals", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          riderId,
          status,
        }),
      });

      const data = await res.json();

      if (!res.ok || !data.success) {
        throw new Error(data?.error || "Status update failed");
      }

      await loadRiders();
    } catch (error: any) {
      console.error(error);
      alert(error?.message || "Status update failed");
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    loadRiders();
  }, []);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const q = search.toLowerCase().trim();

    if (!q) return riders;

    return riders.filter((rider) =>
      `${rider.name} ${rider.mobile} ${rider.vehicle} ${rider.approvalStatus}`
        .toLowerCase()
        .includes(q)
    );
  }, [riders, search]);

  const pending = riders.filter(
    (r) => r.approvalStatus === "Pending"
  ).length;

  const approved = riders.filter(
    (r) => r.approvalStatus === "Approved"
  ).length;

  const rejected = riders.filter(
    (r) => r.approvalStatus === "Rejected"
  ).length;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f8] p-4 md:p-6">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1500px] space-y-6">

        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#159447]">
              Rider Management
            </p>
            <h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-bold text-[#123b39]">
              Rider Approvals
            </h1>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
              Review and manage delivery partner approval status.
            </p>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={loadRiders}
            className="rounded-xl bg-[#063f3d] px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-[#0a504c]"
          >
            â†» Refresh
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 lg:grid-cols-4">
          <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-5 shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
            <p className="text-xs uppercase font-semibold text-gray-400">Total</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-[#123b39]">{riders.length}</p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-5 shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
            <p className="text-xs uppercase font-semibold text-gray-400">Pending</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-orange-500">{pending}</p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-5 shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
            <p className="text-xs uppercase font-semibold text-gray-400">Approved</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-green-600">{approved}</p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-5 shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
            <p className="text-xs uppercase font-semibold text-gray-400">Rejected</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-red-500">{rejected}</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white border border-gray-100 shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
          <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 border-b border-gray-100 p-5 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-[#123b39]">
                Approval Queue
              </h2>
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
                {filtered.length} rider(s)
              </p>
            </div>

            <input
              value={search}
              onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Search rider..."
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#159447] md:w-80"
            />
          </div>

          {loading ? (
            <div className="p-12 text-center text-gray-500">
              Loading approvals...
            </div>
          ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="p-12 text-center text-gray-500">
              No riders found.
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full min-w-[800px]">
                <thead className="bg-[#f8faf9]">
                  <tr>
                    <th className="p-4 text-left text-xs uppercase text-gray-400">Rider</th>
                    <th className="p-4 text-left text-xs uppercase text-gray-400">Mobile</th>
                    <th className="p-4 text-left text-xs uppercase text-gray-400">Vehicle</th>
                    <th className="p-4 text-left text-xs uppercase text-gray-400">Status</th>
                    <th className="p-4 text-left text-xs uppercase text-gray-400">Action</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>

                <tbody>
                  {filtered.map((rider) => (
                    <tr key={rider.id} className="border-t border-gray-100">
                      <td className="p-4 font-semibold text-[#123b39]">
                        {rider.name}
                      </td>

                      <td className="p-4 text-sm text-gray-600">
                        {rider.mobile}
                      </td>

                      <td className="p-4 text-sm text-gray-600">
                        {rider.vehicle}
                      </td>

                      <td className="p-4">
                        <span
                          className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold ${
                            rider.approvalStatus === "Approved"
                              ? "bg-green-100 text-green-700"
                              : rider.approvalStatus === "Rejected"
                              ? "bg-red-100 text-red-700"
                              : "bg-orange-100 text-orange-700"
                          }`}
                        >
                          {rider.approvalStatus}
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      <td className="p-4">
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                          <button
                            onClick={() => updateStatus(rider.id, "Approved")}
                            className="rounded-lg bg-green-600 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-white hover:bg-green-700"
                          >
                            Approve
                          </button>

                          <button
                            onClick={() => updateStatus(rider.id, "Rejected")}
                            className="rounded-lg bg-red-600 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-white hover:bg-red-700"
                          >
                            Reject
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function RiderDocumentsPage() {
  const [riders, setRiders] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadDocuments();
  }, []);

  async function loadDocuments() {
    try {
      setLoading(true);

      const res = await fetch("/api/admin-rider-documents", {
        cache: "no-store",
      });

      const data = await res.json();

      setRiders(
        Array.isArray(data)
          ? data
          : Array.isArray(data?.riders)
          ? data.riders
          : []
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
      setRiders([]);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  const filtered = riders.filter((rider) =>
    `${rider.name || ""} ${rider.mobile || ""} ${rider.vehicle || ""}`
      .toLowerCase()
      .includes(search.toLowerCase())
  );

  function DocumentButton({
    href,
    label,
    tone,
  }: {
    href?: string;
    label: string;
    tone: string;
  }) {
    if (!href) {
      return (
        <span className="rounded-lg bg-gray-100 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-gray-400">
          {label}: Not Uploaded
        </span>
      );
    }

    return (
      <a
        href={href}
        target="_blank"
        rel="noreferrer"
        className={`rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-white ${tone}`}
      >
        {label}
      </a>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f8] p-4 md:p-6">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1500px] space-y-6">

        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#159447]">
              Compliance
            </p>
            <h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-bold text-[#123b39]">
              Rider Documents
            </h1>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
              Review delivery partner identity and vehicle documents.
            </p>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={loadDocuments}
            className="rounded-xl bg-[#063f3d] px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-[#0a504c]"
          >
            â†» Refresh
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
          <div className="mb-5 flex flex-col gap-3 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-[#123b39]">
                Document Verification
              </h2>
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
                {filtered.length} rider(s) displayed
              </p>
            </div>

            <input
              value={search}
              onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Search rider..."
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#159447] md:w-80"
            />
          </div>

          {loading ? (
            <div className="rounded-xl bg-gray-50 p-12 text-center text-gray-500">
              Loading documents...
            </div>
          ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="rounded-xl bg-gray-50 p-12 text-center text-gray-500">
              No Documents Found
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="grid gap-4">
              {filtered.map((rider) => (
                <div
                  key={rider.id}
                  className="rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 bg-[#fbfcfc] p-5"
                >
                  <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 lg:flex-row lg:items-start lg:justify-between">
                    <div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-[#063f3d] font-bold text-white">
                          {rider.name?.charAt(0)?.toUpperCase() || "R"}
                        </div>

                        <div>
                          <h3 className="font-bold text-[#123b39]">
                            {rider.name}
                          </h3>
                          <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
                            {rider.mobile || "â€”"}
                          </p>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <div className="mt-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-sm">
                        <span className="rounded-lg bg-white px-3 py-2 text-gray-600">
                          Vehicle: <b>{rider.vehicle || "â€”"}</b>
                        </span>

                        <span className="rounded-lg bg-white px-3 py-2 text-gray-600">
                          Status: <b>{rider.approvalStatus || "â€”"}</b>
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex max-w-2xl flex-wrap gap-2">

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function StoresPage() {
  const [stores, setStores] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [storeName, setStoreName] = useState("");
  const [city, setCity] = useState("");
  const [manager, setManager] = useState("");
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadStores();
  }, []);

  function loadStores() {
    try {
      const data = JSON.parse(
        localStorage.getItem("freshcut-stores") || "[]"
      );
      setStores(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
    } catch {
      setStores([]);
    }
  }

  function addStore() {
    if (!storeName.trim() || !city.trim()) {
      alert("Fill Required Fields");
      return;
    }

    const newStore = {
      id: Date.now(),
      storeName: storeName.trim(),
      city: city.trim(),
      manager: manager.trim(),
      sales: 0,
      inventory: 0,
      status: "Active",
    };

    const updated = [newStore, ...stores];

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-stores",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setStores(updated);
    setStoreName("");
    setCity("");
    setManager("");
  }

  function updateSales(id: number) {
    const updated = stores.map((store) =>
      store.id === id
        ? { ...store, sales: store.sales + 1000 }
        : store
    );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-stores",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setStores(updated);
  }

  function updateInventory(id: number) {
    const updated = stores.map((store) =>
      store.id === id
        ? { ...store, inventory: store.inventory + 50 }
        : store
    );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-stores",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setStores(updated);
  }

  const totalSales = stores.reduce(
    (sum, store) => sum + Number(store.sales || 0),
    0
  );

  const totalInventory = stores.reduce(
    (sum, store) => sum + Number(store.inventory || 0),
    0
  );

  const filtered = stores.filter((store) =>
    `${store.storeName || ""} ${store.city || ""} ${store.manager || ""}`
      .toLowerCase()
      .includes(search.toLowerCase())
  );

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f8] p-4 md:p-6">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1500px] space-y-6">

        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-[#159447]">
              Business Network
            </p>
            <h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-bold text-[#123b39]">
              Store Management
            </h1>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
              Manage Fresh2Cook stores, sales and inventory.
            </p>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={loadStores}
            className="rounded-xl bg-[#063f3d] px-5 py-3 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-[#0a504c]"
          >
            â†» Refresh
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 lg:grid-cols-3">
          <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-5 border border-gray-100 shadow-sm">
            <p className="text-xs uppercase font-semibold text-gray-400">
              Total Stores
            </p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-[#123b39]">
              {stores.length}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-5 border border-gray-100 shadow-sm">
            <p className="text-xs uppercase font-semibold text-gray-400">
              Total Sales
            </p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-green-600">
              â‚¹{totalSales}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-5 border border-gray-100 shadow-sm">
            <p className="text-xs uppercase font-semibold text-gray-400">
              Total Inventory
            </p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-blue-600">
              {totalInventory}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white border border-gray-100 p-5 shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-[#123b39]">
            Add New Store
          </h2>

          <div className="mt-4 grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
            <input
              placeholder="Store Name"
              value={storeName}
              onChange={(e) => setStoreName(e.target.value)}
              className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#159447]"
            />

            <input
              placeholder="City"
              value={city}
              onChange={(e) => setCity(e.target.value)}
              className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#159447]"
            />

            <input
              placeholder="Manager"
              value={manager}
              onChange={(e) => setManager(e.target.value)}
              className="rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-[#159447]"
            />
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={addStore}
            className="mt-4 rounded-xl bg-[#159447] px-6 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white hover:bg-[#117b3b]"
          >
            + Add Store
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white border border-gray-100 shadow-sm">

                {filtered.length} store(s)
              </p>

              placeholder="Search store, city, manager..."

              No stores found.

          ) : (
            <div className="grid gap-4 p-5 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
              {filtered.map((store) => (
                <div
                  key={store.id}
                  className="rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 bg-[#fbfcfc] p-5 hover:shadow-md"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-start justify-between">

                      <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-[#123b39]">
                        {store.storeName}

                      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
                        {store.city}

                      {store.status || "Active"}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <div className="mt-5 space-y-3 border-t border-gray-100 pt-4">
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">
                      Manager:{" "}
                      <b className="text-[#123b39]">

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function RiderLeaderboardPage() {
  const [data, setData] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, []);

  async function loadData() {
    try {
      setLoading(true);

      const res = await fetch("/api/admin-rider-leaderboard", {
        cache: "no-store",
      });

      const result = await res.json();
      setData(result);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error(error);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  const riders = Array.isArray(data?.leaderboard)
    ? data.leaderboard
    : Array.isArray(data?.riders)
    ? data.riders
    : [];

  const topRider = data?.summary?.topRider;

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f8] p-6">
        <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-12 text-center text-gray-500 shadow-sm">
          Loading leaderboard...
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f8] p-4 md:p-6">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1500px] space-y-6">

        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">

              Delivery partner performance overview.

            <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase text-gray-400">Highest Rating</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-orange-500">
              {topRider?.rating ?? "ï¿½"}
            </p>
