==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\roles\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

const permissions = [
  ["dashboard", "Dashboard"],
  ["orders", "Orders"],
  ["products", "Products"],
  ["customers", "Customers"],
  ["reports", "Reports"],
];

export default function RolesPage() {
  const [roles, setRoles] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [roleName, setRoleName] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadRoles();
  }, []);

  function loadRoles() {
    try {
      setRoles(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("freshcut-roles") || "[]"));
    } catch {
      setRoles([]);
    }
  }

  function addRole() {
    if (!roleName.trim()) {
      alert("Enter Role Name");
      return;
    }

    const role = {
      id: Date.now(),
      name: roleName.trim(),
      dashboard: true,
      orders: true,
      products: true,
      customers: true,
      reports: false,
    };

    const updated = [role, ...roles];
    localStorage.setItem("freshcut-roles", JSON.stringify(updated));
    setRoles(updated);
    setRoleName("");
  }

  function togglePermission(id: number, field: string) {
    const updated = roles.map((role) =>
      role.id === id
        ? { ...role, [field]: !role[field] }
        : role
    );

    localStorage.setItem("freshcut-roles", JSON.stringify(updated));
    setRoles(updated);
  }

  function deleteRole(id: number) {
    if (!confirm("Delete Role?")) return;

    const updated = roles.filter((role) => role.id !== id);
    localStorage.setItem("freshcut-roles", JSON.stringify(updated));
    setRoles(updated);
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1600px]">
        <div className="mb-7">
          <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-emerald-700">
            Fresh2Cook Admin
          </p>
          <h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38] md:text-4xl">
            Role Management
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
            Create roles and control access to admin modules.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-7 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
            Create New Role
          </h2>

          <div className="mt-5 flex flex-col gap-3 md:flex-row">
            <input
              value={roleName}
              onChange={(e) => setRoleName(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Role Name"
              className="flex-1 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
            />

            <button
              onClick={addRole}
              className="rounded-xl bg-[#063f3d] px-8 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white hover:bg-[#0a514d]"
            >
              + Add Role
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-5">
          {roles.map((role) => (
            <div
              key={role.id}
              className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm"
            >
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 border-b border-slate-100 pb-5 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
                    {role.name}
                  </h2>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">
                    Configure module permissions
                  </p>
                </div>

                <button
                  onClick={() => deleteRole(role.id)}
                  className="rounded-xl bg-red-50 px-5 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-red-700 hover:bg-red-100"
                >
                  Delete Role
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-5 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5">
                {permissions.map(([field, label]) => (
                  <label
                    key={field}
                    className={`flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border p-4 ${
                      role[field]
                        ? "border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50"
                        : "border-slate-200 bg-slate-50"
                    }`}
                  >
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      checked={!!role[field]}
                      onChange={() =>
                        togglePermission(role.id, field)
                      }
                      className="h-4 w-4 accent-emerald-600"
                    />

                    <span
                      className={`text-sm font-bold ${
                        role[field]
                          ? "text-emerald-800"
                          : "text-slate-500"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {label}
                    </span>
                  </label>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}

          {!roles.length && (
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-white p-12 text-center text-sm text-slate-500">
              No roles created yet.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}







==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\salary\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function SalaryPage() {
  const [staff, setStaff] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [salaryData, setSalaryData] = useState<any[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      setStaff(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("freshcut-staff") || "[]"));
      setSalaryData(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("freshcut-salary") || "[]"));
    } catch {
      setStaff([]);
      setSalaryData([]);
    }
  }, []);

  function updateSalary(employee: any, amount: string) {
    const updated = salaryData.filter((s) => s.staffId !== employee.id);

    updated.push({
      staffId: employee.id,
      name: employee.name,
      role: employee.role,
      salary: amount,
    });

    setSalaryData(updated);
    localStorage.setItem("freshcut-salary", JSON.stringify(updated));
  }

  function getSalary(id: number) {
    return salaryData.find((s) => s.staffId === id)?.salary || "";
  }

  const totalSalary = salaryData.reduce(
    (sum, item) => sum + Number(item.salary || 0),
    0
  );

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1600px]">
        <div className="mb-7">
          <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-emerald-700">
            Fresh2Cook Admin
          </p>
          <h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38] md:text-4xl">
            Salary Management
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
            Manage monthly staff salary records.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-7 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-100 bg-emerald-50 p-6 shadow-sm">
          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-emerald-700">
            Monthly Salary Budget
          </p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-4xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
            â‚¹{totalSalary.toLocaleString("en-IN")}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">
          {staff.map((employee) => (
            <div
              key={employee.id}
              className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm"
            >
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
                    {employee.name}
                  </h2>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
                    {employee.role}
                  </p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
                    {employee.mobile || employee.phone || "â€”"}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <span className="rounded-full bg-blue-50 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold text-blue-700">
                  SALARY
                </span>
              </div>

              <input
                type="number"
                placeholder="Monthly Salary"
                defaultValue={getSalary(employee.id)}
                onBlur={(e) => updateSalary(employee, e.target.value)}
                className="mt-5 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
              />
            </div>
          ))}

          {!staff.length && (
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-white p-12 text-center text-sm text-slate-500 md:col-span-2">
              No staff members available.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}







==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\sales-analytics\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function AnalyticsPage() {
  const [stats, setStats] = useState({
    orders: 0,
    revenue: 0,
    customers: 0,
    products: 0,
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    loadAnalytics();
  }, []);

  function loadAnalytics() {
    try {
      const orders = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("freshcut-orders") || "[]");
      const products = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("freshcut-products") || "[]");

      const revenue = orders.reduce(
        (sum: number, order: any) =>
          sum + Number(order.total || order.amount || 0),
        0
      );

      const customers = new Set(
        orders.map((o: any) => o.phone).filter(Boolean)
      ).size;

      setStats({
        orders: orders.length,
        revenue,
        customers,
        products: products.length,
      });
    } catch {
      setStats({ orders: 0, revenue: 0, customers: 0, products: 0 });
    }
  }

  const averageOrder =
    stats.orders > 0 ? Math.round(stats.revenue / stats.orders) : 0;

  const conversion =
    stats.customers > 0
      ? Math.round((stats.orders / stats.customers) * 10) / 10
      : 0;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1600px]">
        <div className="mb-7 flex flex-col gap-4 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-emerald-700">
              Fresh2Cook Admin
            </p>
            <h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38] md:text-4xl">
              Sales Analytics
            </h1>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
              Key performance indicators from current business records.
            </p>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={loadAnalytics}
            className="w-fit rounded-xl bg-[#063f3d] px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white hover:bg-[#0a514d]"
          >
            â†» Refresh
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-7 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
          {[
            ["Orders", stats.orders, "text-[#123b38]", "bg-slate-50"],
            ["Revenue", `â‚¹${stats.revenue.toLocaleString("en-IN")}`, "text-emerald-700", "bg-emerald-50"],
            ["Customers", stats.customers, "text-blue-700", "bg-blue-50"],
            ["Products", stats.products, "text-violet-700", "bg-violet-50"],
          ].map(([title, value, color, bg]) => (
            <div key={String(title)} className={`rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 ${bg} p-5 shadow-sm`}>
              <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500">{title}</p>
              <p className={`mt-2 text-3xl font-extrabold ${color}`}>{value}</p>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2">
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-7 shadow-sm">
            <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-500">Average Order Value</p>
            <p className="mt-3 text-5xl font-extrabold text-emerald-700">
              â‚¹{averageOrder.toLocaleString("en-IN")}
            </p>
            <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-slate-500">
              Calculated from total revenue and recorded orders.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-7 shadow-sm">
            <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-500">Orders / Customer</p>
            <p className="mt-3 text-5xl font-extrabold text-blue-700">
              {conversion}x
            </p>
            <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-slate-500">
              Based on current order and customer records.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6 rounded-2xl bg-[#063f3d] p-6 text-white shadow-sm">
          <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-emerald-300">
            Analytics Center
          </p>
          <h2 className="mt-1 text-xl font-extrabold">
            Fresh2Cook business performance at a glance.
          </h2>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}







==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\settings\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

type Settings = {
  businessName: string;
  phone: string;
  email: string;
  address: string;
  deliveryCharge: string;
  minOrder: string;
};

export default function SettingsPage() {
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<Settings>({
    businessName: "Fresh2Cook",
    phone: "9811001901",
    email: "info@freshcut.com",
    address: "New Delhi",
    deliveryCharge: "40",
    minOrder: "199",
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      const data = localStorage.getItem("freshcut-settings");
      if (data) setSettings(JSON.parse(data));
    } catch {
      // keep defaults
    }
  }, []);

  function update(key: keyof Settings, value: string) {
    setSettings((current) => ({
      ...current,
      [key]: value,
    }));
  }

  function saveSettings() {
    localStorage.setItem("freshcut-settings", JSON.stringify(settings));
    alert("Settings Saved Successfully");
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1200px]">
        <div className="mb-7">
          <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-emerald-700">
            Fresh2Cook Admin
          </p>
          <h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38] md:text-4xl">
            System Settings
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
            Manage core business and delivery configuration.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm md:p-8">
          <div className="mb-7 border-b border-slate-100 pb-5">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
              Business Configuration
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
              Update the information used across the system.
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">
            <Field
              label="Business Name"
              value={settings.businessName}
              onChange={(value) => update("businessName", value)}
            />

            <Field
              label="Mobile Number"
              value={settings.phone}
              onChange={(value) => update("phone", value)}
            />

            <Field
              label="Email Address"
              value={settings.email}
              onChange={(value) => update("email", value)}
            />

            <Field
              label="Delivery Charge"
              value={settings.deliveryCharge}
              onChange={(value) => update("deliveryCharge", value)}
            />

            <Field
              label="Minimum Order Value"
              value={settings.minOrder}
              onChange={(value) => update("minOrder", value)}
            />

            <div className="md:col-span-2">
              <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-bold text-slate-600">
                Business Address
              </label>
              <textarea
                value={settings.address}
                onChange={(e) => update("address", e.target.value)}
                rows={4}
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={saveSettings}
            className="mt-7 rounded-xl bg-[#063f3d] px-10 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white hover:bg-[#0a514d]"
          >
            Save Settings
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Field({
  label,
  value,
  onChange,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <div>
      <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-bold text-slate-600">
        {label}
      </label>
      <input
        value={value}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
        className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
      />
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function StaffPage() {
  const [staff, setStaff] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState("");
  const [role, setRole] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadStaff();
  }, []);

  function loadStaff() {
    try {
      setStaff(
        JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("freshcut-staff") || "[]")
      );
    } catch {
      setStaff([]);
    }
  }

  function addStaff() {
    if (!name || !phone || !role) {
      alert("Fill All Fields");
      return;
    }

    const member = {
      id: Date.now(),
      name,
      phone,
      role,
      status: "Active",
      joiningDate: new Date().toLocaleDateString(),
    };

    const updated = [member, ...staff];

    localStorage.setItem("freshcut-staff", JSON.stringify(updated));
    setStaff(updated);

    setName("");
    setPhone("");
    setRole("");

    alert("Staff Added Successfully");
  }

  function toggleStatus(id: number) {
    const updated = staff.map((item) =>
      item.id === id
        ? {
            ...item,
            status: item.status === "Active" ? "Inactive" : "Active",
          }
        : item
    );

    localStorage.setItem("freshcut-staff", JSON.stringify(updated));
    setStaff(updated);
  }

  function deleteStaff(id: number) {
    if (!confirm("Delete Staff?")) return;

    const updated = staff.filter((item) => item.id !== id);

    localStorage.setItem("freshcut-staff", JSON.stringify(updated));
    setStaff(updated);
  }

  const activeStaff = staff.filter((item) => item.status === "Active").length;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1600px]">
        <div className="mb-7">
          <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-emerald-700">
            Fresh2Cook Admin
          </p>
          <h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38] md:text-4xl">
            Staff Management
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
            Manage staff members, roles and active status.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-7 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-500">Total Staff</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
              {staff.length}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-100 bg-emerald-50 p-5 shadow-sm">
            <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-emerald-700">Active Staff</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-extrabold text-emerald-800">
              {activeStaff}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-7 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
            Add Staff Member
          </h2>

          <div className="mt-5 grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
            <input
              value={name}
              onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Staff Name"
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
            />

            <input
              value={phone}
              onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Mobile Number"
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
            />

            <input
              value={role}
              onChange={(e) => setRole(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Role"
              className="rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-4 py-3 text-sm outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
            />
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={addStaff}
            className="mt-5 rounded-xl bg-[#063f3d] px-8 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white hover:bg-[#0a514d]"
          >
            + Add Staff
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
          {staff.map((member) => (
            <div
              key={member.id}
              className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm"
            >
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
                    {member.name}
                  </h2>
                  <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">
                    {member.phone}
                  </p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
                    {member.role}
                  </p>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-400">
                    Joined {member.joiningDate}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <span
                  className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-bold ${
                    member.status === "Active"
                      ? "bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700"
                      : "bg-red-50 text-red-700"
                  }`}
                >
                  {member.status}
                </span>
              </div>

              <div className="mt-5 flex gap-2">
                <button
                  onClick={() => toggleStatus(member.id)}
                  className="flex-1 rounded-xl bg-blue-50 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-100"
                >
                  Status
                </button>

                <button
                  onClick={() => deleteStaff(member.id)}
                  className="flex-1 rounded-xl bg-red-50 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-red-700 hover:bg-red-100"
                >
                  Delete
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}

          {!staff.length && (
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-white p-12 text-center text-sm text-slate-500 md:col-span-2 xl:col-span-3">
              No staff members added yet.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function StockAlertsPage() {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<any[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadInventory();
  }, []);

  function loadInventory() {
    try {
      const data = JSON.parse(
        localStorage.getItem("freshcut-inventory") || "[]"
      );
      setItems(
        data.filter((item: any) => Number(item.stock) <= 10)
      );
    } catch {
      setItems([]);
    }
  }

  function updateStock(id: number, stock: string) {
    try {
      const inventory = JSON.parse(
        localStorage.getItem("freshcut-inventory") || "[]"
      );

      const updated = inventory.map((item: any) =>
        item.id === id ? { ...item, stock } : item
      );

      localStorage.setItem(
        "freshcut-inventory",
        JSON.stringify(updated)
      );

      loadInventory();
      alert("Stock Updated");
    } catch {
      alert("Unable to update stock");
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-[#f5f7f7] p-5 md:p-7">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-[1400px]">
        <div className="mb-7 flex flex-col gap-4 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-emerald-700">
              Fresh2Cook Admin
            </p>
            <h1 className="mt-1 text-3xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38] md:text-4xl">
              Stock Alerts
            </h1>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
              Monitor products requiring inventory attention.
            </p>
          </div>

          <button
            onClick={loadInventory}
            className="w-fit rounded-xl bg-[#063f3d] px-5 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white hover:bg-[#0a514d]"
          >
            â†» Refresh
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-7 rounded-2xl border border-red-100 bg-red-50 p-6">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm font-bold text-red-700">
                Items Running Low
              </p>
              <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-red-600">
                Products with stock at or below 10 units.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="text-3xl font-extrabold text-red-700">
              {items.length}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        {items.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-100 bg-white p-14 text-center shadow-sm">
            <div className="mx-auto flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700">
              âœ“
            </div>
            <h2 className="mt-4 text-xl font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
              All Stock Levels Healthy
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-500">
              No low-stock products require attention.
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {items.map((item: any) => (
              <div
                key={item.id}
                className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm"
              >
                <div className="flex flex-col gap-5 md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between">
                  <div>
                    <h2 className="text-lg font-extrabold text-[#123b38]">
                      {item.name}
                    </h2>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-red-600">
                      Remaining: {item.stock} {item.unit || "units"}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex gap-3">
                    <input
                      type="number"
                      defaultValue={item.stock}
                      id={`stock-${item.id}`}
                      className="w-28 rounded-xl border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-emerald-600"
                    />

                    <button
                      onClick={() =>
                        updateStock(
                          item.id,
                          (
                            document.getElementById(

                            ) as HTMLInputElement
                          ).value

                    >

                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function StoresPage() {
  const [stores, setStores] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  const [storeName, setStoreName] =
    useState("");

  const [city, setCity] =
    useState("");

  const [manager, setManager] =
    useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadStores();
  }, []);

  function loadStores() {
    const data = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem(
        "freshcut-stores"
      ) || "[]"
    );

    setStores(data);
  }

  function addStore() {
    if (
      !storeName ||
      !city
    ) {
      alert("Fill Required Fields");
      return;
    }

    const newStore = {
      id: Date.now(),
      storeName,
      city,
      manager,
      sales: 0,
      inventory: 0,
      status: "Active",
    };

    const updated = [
      newStore,
      ...stores,
    ];

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-stores",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setStoreName("");
    setCity("");
    setManager("");

    loadStores();
  }

  function updateSales(
    id: number
  ) {
    const updated =
      stores.map((store) =>
        store.id === id
          ? {
              ...store,
              sales:
                store.sales + 1000,
            }
          : store
      );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-stores",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setStores(updated);
  }

  function updateInventory(
    id: number
  ) {
    const updated =
      stores.map((store) =>
        store.id === id
          ? {
              ...store,
              inventory:
                store.inventory + 50,
            }
          : store
      );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-stores",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setStores(updated);
  }

  const totalSales =
    stores.reduce(
      (sum, store) =>
        sum + store.sales,
      0
    );

  const totalInventory =
    stores.reduce(
      (sum, store) =>
        sum +
        store.inventory,
      0
    );

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto">

        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-8">
          Multi Store ERP
        </h1>

        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-4 mb-8">

          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow p-6">
            <p>Total Stores</p>
            <h2 className="text-4xl font-bold">
              {stores.length}
            </h2>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow p-6">
            <p>Total Sales</p>
            <h2 className="text-4xl font-bold text-green-600">
              Ã¢â€šÂ¹{totalSales}
            </h2>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow p-6">
            <p>Total Inventory</p>
            <h2 className="text-4xl font-bold text-blue-600">
              {totalInventory}
            </h2>
          </div>

        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow p-6 mb-8">

          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">
            Add New Store
          </h2>

          <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">

                setStoreName(

                )
              }
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

            <input

              value={city}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setCity(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

            <input
              placeholder="Manager"
              value={manager}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setManager(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

          </div>

          <button
            onClick={addStore}
            className="mt-4 bg-green-600 text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-xl"
          >
            Add Store
          </button>

        </div>

        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">

          {stores.map(
            (store) => (

              <div
                key={store.id}
                className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6"
              >

                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold">
                  {
                    store.storeName
                  }
                </h2>

                <p>
                  ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã¢â‚¬Å“Ã‚Â {store.city}
                </p>

                <p>
                  ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã¢â‚¬ËœÃ‚Â¨ÃƒÂ¢Ã¢â€šÂ¬Ã‚ÂÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã¢â‚¬â„¢Ã‚Â¼{" "}
                  {store.manager}
                </p>

                <p className="mt-2 font-bold text-green-600">

                  Ã¢â€šÂ¹

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function SubscriptionBoxesPage() {
  const [plans, setPlans] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  const [name, setName] =
    useState("");

  const [price, setPrice] =
    useState("");

  const [items, setItems] =
    useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadPlans();
  }, []);

  function loadPlans() {
    const data = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem(
        "freshcut-subscription-boxes"
      ) || "[]"
    );

    setPlans(data);
  }

  function addPlan() {
    if (
      !name ||
      !price ||
      !items
    ) {
      alert(
        "Fill All Fields"
      );
      return;
    }

    const updated = [
      {
        id: Date.now(),
        name,
        price:
          Number(price),
        items,
        subscribers: 0,
        status: "Active",
      },
      ...plans,
    ];

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-subscription-boxes",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setName("");
    setPrice("");
    setItems("");

    loadPlans();
  }

  function deletePlan(
    id: number
  ) {
    const updated =
      plans.filter(
        (plan: any) =>
          plan.id !== id
      );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-subscription-boxes",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    loadPlans();
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto">

        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-8">

          </h2>

              value={name}

                setName(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              placeholder="Plan Name"
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

            <input
              value={price}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setPrice(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              placeholder="Monthly Price"
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

            <input
              value={items}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setItems(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              placeholder="Included Items"
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

          </div>

          <button
            onClick={addPlan}
            className="mt-5 bg-green-600 text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold"
          >
            Create Plan
          </button>

        </div>

        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">

          {plans.map(
            (plan: any) => (
              <div
                key={plan.id}
                className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6"
              >

                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold">
                  {plan.name}
                </h2>

                <p className="mt-2">
                  Ã¢â€šÂ¹{plan.price}/Month
                </p>

                <p className="mt-2 text-gray-600">
                  {plan.items}
                </p>

                <p className="mt-3 font-bold text-green-600">
                  {plan.status}
                </p>

                <p className="mt-2">
                  Subscribers:
                  {" "}

                    deletePlan(
                      plan.id
                    )
                  }
                  className="mt-4 bg-red-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl"
                >
                  Delete
                </button>

              </div>
            )
          )}

        </div>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function SubscriptionsPage() {
  const [subscriptions, setSubscriptions] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  const [customer, setCustomer] =
    useState("");

  const [phone, setPhone] =
    useState("");

  const [plan, setPlan] =
    useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadSubscriptions();
  }, []);

  function loadSubscriptions() {
    const data = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem(
        "freshcut-subscriptions"
      ) || "[]"
    );

    setSubscriptions(data);
  }

  function addSubscription() {
    if (
      !customer ||
      !phone ||
      !plan
    ) {
      alert(
        "Fill All Fields"
      );
      return;
    }

    const subscription = {
      id: Date.now(),
      customer,
      phone,
      plan,
      status: "Active",
      startDate:
        new Date().toLocaleDateString(),
    };

    const updated = [
      subscription,
      ...subscriptions,
    ];

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-subscriptions",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setSubscriptions(updated);

    setCustomer("");
    setPhone("");
    setPlan("");

    alert(
      "Subscription Added Successfully"
    );
  }

  function toggleStatus(
    id: number
  ) {
    const updated =
      subscriptions.map(
        (item) =>
          item.id === id
            ? {
                ...item,
                status:
                  item.status ===
                  "Active"
                    ? "Paused"
                    : "Active",
              }
            : item
      );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-subscriptions",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setSubscriptions(updated);
  }

  function deleteSubscription(
    id: number
  ) {
    if (
      !confirm(
        "Delete Subscription?"
      )

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function TestimonialsPage() {
  const [testimonials, setTestimonials] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  const [name, setName] =
    useState("");

  const [rating, setRating] =
    useState("5");

  const [message, setMessage] =
    useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadTestimonials();
  }, []);

  function loadTestimonials() {
    const data = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem(
        "freshcut-testimonials"
      ) || "[]"
    );

    setTestimonials(data);
  }

  function addTestimonial() {
    if (
      !name ||
      !message
    ) {
      alert(
        "Fill All Fields"
      );
      return;
    }

    const testimonial = {
      id: Date.now(),
      name,
      rating,
      message,
      status: "Approved",
      date:
        new Date().toLocaleDateString(),
    };

    const updated = [
      testimonial,
      ...testimonials,
    ];

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-testimonials",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setTestimonials(updated);

    setName("");
    setRating("5");
    setMessage("");

    alert(
      "Testimonial Added Successfully"
    );
  }

  function toggleStatus(
    id: number
  ) {
    const updated =
      testimonials.map(
        (item) =>
          item.id === id
            ? {
                ...item,
                status:
                  item.status ===
                  "Approved"
                    ? "Pending"
                    : "Approved",
              }
            : item
      );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-testimonials",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setTestimonials(updated);
  }

  function deleteTestimonial(
    id: number
  ) {
    if (
      !confirm(
        "Delete Review?"
      )
    )
      return;

    const updated =
      testimonials.filter(
        (item) =>
          item.id !== id
      );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-testimonials",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setTestimonials(updated);
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto">

        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-8">
          Testimonials Manager
        </h1>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6 mb-8">

          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-5">
            Add Review
          </h2>

          <div className="grid gap-4">

            <input
              value={name}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setName(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              placeholder="Customer Name"
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

            <select
              value={rating}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setRating(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            >
              <option value="5">
                ÃƒÂ¢Ã‚Â­Ã‚ÂÃƒÂ¢Ã‚Â­Ã‚ÂÃƒÂ¢Ã‚Â­Ã‚ÂÃƒÂ¢Ã‚Â­Ã‚ÂÃƒÂ¢Ã‚Â­Ã‚Â
              </option>

              <option value="4">
                ÃƒÂ¢Ã‚Â­Ã‚ÂÃƒÂ¢Ã‚Â­Ã‚ÂÃƒÂ¢Ã‚Â­Ã‚ÂÃƒÂ¢Ã‚Â­Ã‚Â
              </option>

==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\tracking-center\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect,useState } from "react";

export default function Page(){

 const [rider,setRider] =
  useState<any>();

 const [delivery,setDelivery] =
  useState<any>();

 const [push,setPush] =
  useState<any>();

 useEffect(()=>{

  fetch("/api/tracking/rider-location")
   .then(r=>r.json())
   .then(setRider);

  fetch("/api/tracking/delivery-status")
   .then(r=>r.json())
   .then(setDelivery);

  fetch("/api/push/status")
   .then(r=>r.json())
   .then(setPush);

 },[]);

 return(

  <div className="p-6">

   <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-6">
    Tracking Center
   </h1>

   <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">

    <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow">
      Rider
      <div>{rider?.rider?.name}</div>
      <div>{rider?.rider?.status}</div>
    </div>

    <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow">
      Delivery
      <div>{delivery?.status}</div>
      <div>{delivery?.eta}</div>
    </div>

    <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl shadow">
      Push Engine
      <div>{push?.pushNotifications}</div>
    </div>

   </div>

  </div>

 );

}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function VendorPortalPage() {
  const [vendors, setVendors] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  const [name, setName] =
    useState("");

  const [company, setCompany] =
    useState("");

  const [phone, setPhone] =
    useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadVendors();
  }, []);

  function loadVendors() {
    const data = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem(
        "freshcut-vendors"
      ) || "[]"
    );

    setVendors(data);
  }

  function addVendor() {
    if (
      !name ||
      !company ||
      !phone
    ) {
      alert(
        "Fill All Fields"
      );
      return;
    }

    const updated = [
      {
        id: Date.now(),
        name,
        company,
        phone,
        orders: 0,
        amount: 0,
        status: "Active",
        joined:
          new Date().toLocaleDateString(),
      },
      ...vendors,
    ];

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-vendors",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setName("");
    setCompany("");
    setPhone("");

    loadVendors();
  }

  function removeVendor(
    id: number
  ) {
    const updated =
      vendors.filter(
        (vendor: any) =>
          vendor.id !== id
      );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-vendors",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    loadVendors();
  }

  const totalVendors =
    vendors.length;

  const activeVendors =
    vendors.filter(
      (v: any) =>
        v.status ===
        "Active"
    ).length;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto">

        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-8">

            <p>
              Active Vendors
            </p>

            <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-green-600">
              {activeVendors}
            </h2>

          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow">

            <p>
              Monthly Purchase
            </p>

            <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-purple-600">
              Ã¢â€šÂ¹
              {vendors.reduce(
                (
                  sum: number,
                  item: any
                ) =>
                  sum +
                  Number(
                    item.amount || 0
                  ),
                0
              )}
            </h2>

          </div>

        </div>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6 mb-8">

          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">
            Add Vendor
          </h2>

          <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">

            <input
              value={name}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setName(
                  e.target.value
                )

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function VendorsPage() {
  const [vendors, setVendors] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  const [name, setName] =
    useState("");

  const [mobile, setMobile] =
    useState("");

  const [category, setCategory] =
    useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadVendors();
  }, []);

  function loadVendors() {
    const data = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem(
        "freshcut-vendors"
      ) || "[]"
    );

    setVendors(data);
  }

  function addVendor() {
    if (
      !name ||
      !mobile ||
      !category
    ) {
      alert(
        "Fill All Fields"
      );
      return;
    }

    const vendor = {
      id: Date.now(),
      name,
      mobile,
      category,
      status: "Active",
      createdAt:
        new Date().toLocaleDateString(),
    };

    const updated = [
      vendor,
      ...vendors,
    ];

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-vendors",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setVendors(updated);

    setName("");
    setMobile("");
    setCategory("");

    alert(
      "Vendor Added Successfully"
    );
  }

  function deleteVendor(
    id: number
  ) {
    if (
      !confirm(
        "Delete Vendor?"
      )
    )
      return;

    const updated =
      vendors.filter(
        (item) =>
          item.id !== id
      );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-vendors",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setVendors(updated);
  }

  function toggleStatus(
    id: number
  ) {
    const updated =
      vendors.map((item) =>
        item.id === id
          ? {
              ...item,
              status:
                item.status ===
                "Active"
                  ? "Inactive"
                  : "Active",
            }
          : item
      );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-vendors",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setVendors(updated);
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto">

        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-8">
          Vendor Management
        </h1>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6 mb-8">

          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-5">
            Add Vendor
          </h2>

          <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">

            <input
              value={name}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setName(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              placeholder="Vendor Name"
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

            <input
              value={mobile}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setMobile(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              placeholder="Mobile Number"
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

            <input
              value={category}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setCategory(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              placeholder="Vegetables / Fruits / Packaging"
              className="border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

          </div>

          <button
            onClick={addVendor}
            className="mt-5 bg-green-600 text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold"
          >
            Add Vendor
          </button>

        </div>

        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">

          {vendors.map(
            (vendor) => (

              <div
                key={vendor.id}
                className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6"
              >

                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold">
                  {vendor.name}
                </h2>

                <p className="mt-2">
                  ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã¢â‚¬Å“Ã…Â¾ {vendor.mobile}
                </p>

                <p className="mt-2">
                  ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã¢â‚¬Å“Ã‚Â¦ {vendor.category}
                </p>

                <p className="mt-2">
                  ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã¢â‚¬Å“Ã¢â‚¬Â¦ {vendor.createdAt}
                </p>

                <div className="mt-4">

                  <span
                    className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-full ${
                      vendor.status ===
                      "Active"
                        ? "bg-green-100 text-green-700"
                        : "bg-red-100 text-red-700"
                    }`}
                  >
                    {vendor.status}
                  </span>

                </div>

                <div className="flex gap-2 mt-5">

                  <button
                    onClick={() =>
                      toggleStatus(
                        vendor.id
                      )
                    }
                    className="flex-1 bg-blue-600 text-white py-2 rounded-xl"
                  >
                    Status
                  </button>

                  <button
                    onClick={() =>
                      deleteVendor(
                        vendor.id
                      )
                    }
                    className="flex-1 bg-red-600 text-white py-2 rounded-xl"
                  >
                    Delete
                  </button>

                </div>

              </div>

            )
          )}

        </div>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function WhatsAppAutomationPage() {
  const [rules, setRules] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadRules();
  }, []);

  function loadRules() {
    const saved =
      JSON.parse(
        localStorage.getItem(
          "freshcut-whatsapp-rules"
        ) || "[]"
      );

    if (saved.length > 0) {
      setRules(saved);
      return;
    }

    const defaultRules = [
      {
        id: 1,
        name:
          "Birthday Wishes",
        enabled: true,
        template:
          "ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã…Â½Ã¢â‚¬Â° Happy Birthday! Fresh2Cook ki taraf se aapko dher sari shubhkamnaye.",
      },
      {
        id: 2,
        name:
          "Reorder Reminder",
        enabled: true,
        template:
          "ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã‚Â¥Ã‚Â¬ Aapka regular order due hai. Fresh vegetables order karein.",
      },
      {
        id: 3,
        name:
          "Cart Abandonment",
        enabled: true,
        template:
          "ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã¢â‚¬ÂºÃ¢â‚¬â„¢ Aapke cart me items pending hain. Order complete karein.",
      },
      {
        id: 4,
        name:
          "Subscription Renewal",
        enabled: true,
        template:
          "ÃƒÂ°Ã…Â¸Ã¢â‚¬Å“Ã‚Â¦ Aapki subscription renew hone wali hai.",
      },
    ];

    setRules(
      defaultRules
    );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-whatsapp-rules",
      JSON.stringify(
        defaultRules
      )
    );
  }

  function toggleRule(
    id: number
  ) {
    const updated =
      rules.map(
        (rule: any) =>
          rule.id === id
            ? {
                ...rule,
                enabled:
                  !rule.enabled,
              }
            : rule
      );

    setRules(updated);

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-whatsapp-rules",
      JSON.stringify(
        updated
      )
    );
  }

  function runNow(
    name: string
  ) {
    alert(
      `${name} Campaign Triggered`
    );
  }

  const activeRules =
    rules.filter(
      (r: any) =>
        r.enabled
    ).length;

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto">

        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-8">
          WhatsApp Automation Engine
        </h1>

        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-4 mb-8">

          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow">

            <p>
              Total Rules
            </p>

            <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold">
              {rules.length}
            </h2>

          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow">

            <p>
              Active Rules
            </p>

            <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-green-600">
              {activeRules}
            </h2>

          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-3xl shadow">

            <p>
              Today's Triggers
            </p>

            <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-blue-600">
              47
            </h2>

          </div>

        </div>

        <div className="space-y-4">

          {rules.map(
            (rule: any) => (
              <div
                key={rule.id}
                className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6"
              >

                <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:justify-between gap-4">

                  <div>

                    <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">
                      {rule.name}
                    </h2>

                    <p className="mt-2 text-gray-600">
                      {rule.template}
                    </p>

                    <p className="mt-3">

                      Status:

                      <span
                        className={`ml-2 font-bold ${
                          rule.enabled
                            ? "text-green-600"
                            : "text-red-600"
                        }`}
                      >
                        {rule.enabled
                          ? "Active"
                          : "Disabled"}
                      </span>

                    </p>

                  </div>

                  <div className="flex gap-3">

                    <button
                      onClick={() =>
                        toggleRule(
                          rule.id
                        )
                      }
                      className="bg-yellow-500 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl"
                    >
                      {rule.enabled
                        ? "Disable"
                        : "Enable"}
                    </button>

                    <button
                      onClick={() =>
                        runNow(
                          rule.name
                        )
                      }
                      className="bg-green-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl"
                    >
                      Run Now
                    </button>

                  </div>

                </div>

              </div>
            )
          )}

        </div>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}









==================== D:\D Drive\Desktop\CRM\FreshCut-CRM-Professional-Starter\app\admin\whatsapp-campaign\page.tsx ====================

"use client";

export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

export default function WhatsAppCampaignPage() {
  const [campaigns, setCampaigns] =
    useState<any[]>([]);

  const [title, setTitle] =
    useState("");

  const [message, setMessage] =
    useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    loadCampaigns();
  }, []);

  function loadCampaigns() {
    const data = JSON.parse(
      localStorage.getItem(
        "freshcut-whatsapp-campaigns"
      ) || "[]"
    );

    setCampaigns(data);
  }

  function createCampaign() {
    if (
      !title ||
      !message
    ) {
      alert(
        "Fill All Fields"
      );
      return;
    }

    const updated = [
      {
        id: Date.now(),
        title,
        message,
        status: "Draft",
        createdAt:
          new Date().toLocaleDateString(),
      },
      ...campaigns,
    ];

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-whatsapp-campaigns",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    setTitle("");
    setMessage("");

    loadCampaigns();
  }

  function sendCampaign(
    id: number
  ) {
    const updated =
      campaigns.map(
        (item: any) =>
          item.id === id
            ? {
                ...item,
                status: "Sent",
              }
            : item
      );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-whatsapp-campaigns",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    loadCampaigns();

    alert(
      "Campaign Sent Successfully"
    );
  }

  function deleteCampaign(
    id: number
  ) {
    const updated =
      campaigns.filter(
        (item: any) =>
          item.id !== id
      );

    localStorage.setItem(
      "freshcut-whatsapp-campaigns",
      JSON.stringify(updated)
    );

    loadCampaigns();
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-100 p-6">

      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto">

        <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold mb-8">
          WhatsApp Campaigns
        </h1>

        <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6 mb-8">

          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold mb-4">
            Create Campaign
          </h2>

          <div className="space-y-4">

            <input
              value={title}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setTitle(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              placeholder="Campaign Title"
              className="w-full border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

            <textarea
              value={message}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setMessage(
                  e.target.value
                )
              }
              placeholder="WhatsApp Message"
              rows={5}
              className="w-full border p-3 rounded-xl"
            />

            <button
              onClick={
                createCampaign
              }
              className="bg-green-600 text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold"
            >
              Create Campaign
            </button>

          </div>

        </div>

        <div className="space-y-4">

          {campaigns.map(
            (campaign: any) => (
              <div
                key={campaign.id}
                className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow p-6"
              >

                <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:justify-between gap-4">

                  <div>

                    <h2 className="text-xl font-bold">
                      {campaign.title}
                    </h2>

                    <p className="mt-2 text-gray-600">
                      {campaign.message}
                    </p>

                    <p className="mt-3 font-bold text-blue-600">
                      {campaign.status}
                    </p>

                  </div>

                  <div className="flex gap-2 flex-wrap">

                    <button
                      onClick={() =>
                        sendCampaign(
                          campaign.id
                        )
                      }
                      className="bg-green-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl"
                    >
                      Send
                    </button>

                    <button
                      onClick={() =>
                        deleteCampaign(
                          campaign.id
                        )
                      }
                      className="bg-red-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl"
                    >
                      Delete
                    </button>

                  </div>

                </div>

              </div>
            )
          )}

        </div>

      </div>

    </div>
  );
}

          <aside className="fixed lg:sticky top-[68px] left-0 z-40 w-[270px] h-[calc(100vh-68px)] bg-[#063f3d] text-white overflow-y-auto shadow-xl">

            {/* Sidebar brand area */}
            <div className="px-5 py-5 border-b border-white/10">

              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">

                <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl bg-white flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm">
                  <span className="text-[#159447] font-black text-lg">
                    F2C
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <div className="font-bold text-white">
                    
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-[11px] text-emerald-200">
                    
                  </div>
                </div>

              </div>

            </div>

            {/* Main navigation */}
            <nav className="p-3 space-y-2">

              

              {menuGroups.map((group) => {

                const isOpen = openMenu === group.title;

                const groupActive = group.items.some(
                  ([, href]) =>
                    pathname === href ||
                    pathname.startsWith(`${href}/`)
                );

                return (
                  <div
                    key={group.title}
                    className="rounded-xl overflow-hidden"
                  >

                    <button
                      onClick={() =>
                        setOpenMenu(
                          isOpen ? "" : group.title
                        )
                      }
                      className={`w-full flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 text-sm font-semibold transition ${
                        groupActive
                          ? "bg-white/10 text-white"
                          : "text-emerald-50 hover:bg-white/10"
                      }`}
                    >

                      <span className="flex items-center gap-3">

                        <span className="w-6 text-center text-base">
                          {group.icon}
                        </span>

                        {group.title}

                      </span>

                      <span className="text-emerald-200 text-lg">
                        {isOpen ? ?? "-" : "+"}
                      </span>

                    </button>

                    {isOpen && (
                      <div className="ml-4 mr-1 mt-1 mb-2 border-l border-white/10 pl-2 space-y-1">

                        {group.items.map(([name, href]) => {

                          const active =
                            pathname === href ||
                            pathname.startsWith(`${href}/`);

                          return (
                            <Link
                              key={href}
                              href={href}
                              className={`block px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg text-[13px] transition ${
                                active
                                  ? "bg-[#13a64b] text-white font-semibold shadow-sm"
                                  : "text-emerald-100/80 hover:bg-white/10 hover:text-white"
                              }`}
                            >
                              {name}
                            </Link>
                          );
                        })}

                      </div>
                    )}

                  </div>
                );
              })}

            </nav>

            {</aside>
        )}

        {/* MAIN CONTENT */}
        <main
          className={`flex-1 min-w-0 transition-all duration-200 ${
            sidebarOpen ? "lg:ml-0" : ""
          }`}
        >

          {/* Page top strip */}
          <div className="px-4 lg:px-7 pt-5">

            <div className="max-w-[1800px] mx-auto">

              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">

                <div>
                  <div className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[1.5px] text-[#159447]">
                    
                  </div>

                  <div className="text-sm text-slate-400 mt-1">
                    
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-400">
                  <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-green-500" />
                  System Online
                </div>

              </div>

              {/* Existing page */}
              <div className="admin-corporate-surface">
                {children}
              </div>

            </div>

          </div>

        </main>

      </div>

      {/* GLOBAL ADMIN VISUAL THEME */}
      <style jsx global>{`
        html,
        body {
          background: #f4f7f5;
        }

        body {
          color: #1e293b;
        }

        .admin-corporate-surface {
          min-height: calc(100vh - 130px);
        }

        .admin-corporate-surface > div {
          border-color: #e2e8f0;
        }

        .admin-corporate-surface button,
        .admin-corporate-surface a {
          transition:
            transform 0.15s ease,
            box-shadow 0.15s ease,
            background-color 0.15s ease,
            border-color 0.15s ease;
        }

        .admin-corporate-surface button:hover,
        .admin-corporate-surface a:hover {
          transition:
            transform 0.15s ease,
            box-shadow 0.15s ease,
            background-color 0.15s ease,
            border-color 0.15s ease;
        }

        ::-webkit-scrollbar {
          width: 7px;
          height: 7px;
        }

        ::-webkit-scrollbar-track {
          background: #eef3f0;
        }

        ::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
          background: #b7c9c1;
          border-radius: 20px;
        }

        ::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb:hover {
          background: #159447;
        }
      `}</style>

    </div>
  );
}

function Card({
  title,
  value,
}: {
  title: string;
  value: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl border p-5">
      <div className="text-slate-500 text-sm">
        {title}
      </div>

      <div className="text-4xl font-bold mt-2">
        {value}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function OrderRow({
  id,
}: {
  id: string;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="flex justify-between border-b pb-2">
      <span>{id}</span>
      <span className="text-green-600">
        Delivered
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

function Card({
  icon,
  title,
  value,
  growth,
  subtitle,
  bg,
}: any) {
  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border p-2">
      <div className="flex justify-between">
        <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center ${bg}`}>
          {icon}
        </div>

        <div className="text-right">
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{title}</div>
          <div className="text-xl font-bold mt-1">{value}</div>
          <div className="text-green-600 text-sm mt-1">{growth}</div>
          <div className="text-xs text-slate-500">{subtitle}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Row({ color, name, count }: any) {
  return (
    <div className="flex justify-between">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <div className={`w-3 h-3 rounded-full ${color}`} />
        {name}
      </div>
      <div>{count}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Action({ icon, text, bg }: any) {
  return (
    <button
      className={`${bg} rounded-lg p-2 text-left flex items-center gap-2 font-medium hover:scale-105 hover:shadow-lg transition-all duration-200 active:scale-95`}
    >
      {icon}
      {text}
    </button>
  );
}

function OrderRow({ no, customer, items, amount, status }: any) {
  return (
    <tr className="border-t">
      <td className="p-3">{no}</td>
      <td className="p-3">{customer}</td>
      <td className="p-3">{items}</td>
      <td className="p-3">{amount}</td>
      <td className="p-3">

  <span
    className={`px-3 py-1 rounded-lg border text-sm font-semibold
    ${
      status === "Pending"
        ? "bg-orange-50 text-orange-600 border-orange-300"
        : status === "Processing"
        ? "bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 border-blue-300"
        : status === "Ready"
        ? "bg-purple-50 text-purple-600 border-purple-300"
        : "bg-green-50 text-green-600 border-green-300"
    }`}
  >
    {status}
  </span>

</td>
    </tr>
  );
}

function Activity({ text }: any) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
      <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-green-100 flex items-center justify-center">
        <Clock3 size={18} />
      </div>
      <div>{text}</div>
    </div>
  );
}
